### [1] [Ropedia W0: Scaling Loco-Manipulation World Models across Embodiments with Human Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26855)
*Tianqi Liu,Hanfei Zhao,Qifeng Liang,Hui En Pang,Jiarui Xu,Sayang Mu,Penghao Qiu,YingSheng Zhang,Jun Wang,Ziang Cao,Mingze Sun,Yukang Cao,Fangzhou Hong,Zhaoxi Chen,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Ropedia W0，首个跨越人类与多种机器人本体的第一视角移动-操作世界基础模型，并配套大规模数据、统一运动学表示、基准与实时部署能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同时建模移动与操作对具身智能体预测动作后果很重要，但异构动作空间和多模态监督稀缺使跨本体联合建模困难。

Method: 构建 Xp-Data（1 万小时第一视角人类数据与 3 千小时机器人数据，统一多模态标注和质量控制）；采用统一运动学表示，以相机位姿编码移动、以投影骨架编码操作，机器人骨架结合本体特定前向运动学与人工关键点标注校正；提出基于高质量灵巧人形遥操作数据的长时程移动-操作基准 Xp-Teleop。

Result: 在 DreamDojo、Ψ0、ω-HOME、Xp-Teleop、AgiBot 五个跨本体基准及留出人类第一视角视频上取得最先进性能，覆盖零样本、少样本和全数据后训练；扩展人类预训练数据可同时提升人类与机器人评测表现；蒸馏后支持实时、分钟级 rollout，并实现实时 VR 交互。

Conclusion: Ropedia W0 在策略评估、基于模型的规划和世界-动作建模等应用中展示了对具身决策的实用价值。

Abstract: World models enable embodied agents to anticipate action consequences, yet jointly modeling locomotion and manipulation across diverse embodiments remains challenging due to heterogeneous action spaces and limited multimodal supervision. We present Ropedia W0, the first egocentric loco-manipulation world foundation model spanning humans and robots with diverse embodiments. We curate Xp-Data, comprising 10k hours of egocentric human data and 3k hours of robot data with unified multimodal annotations and systematic quality control. A unified kinematic representation encodes locomotion through camera poses and manipulation through projected skeletons, with robot skeletons combining embodiment-specific forward kinematics with extensive manual keypoint annotation and correction. We also introduce Xp-Teleop, a long-horizon loco-manipulation benchmark based on high-quality dexterous humanoid teleoperation data. Evaluations demonstrate state-of-the-art performance across five cross-embodiment benchmarks (DreamDojo, $\Psi_0$, $\omega$-HOME, Xp-Teleop, and AgiBot) and on held-out human egocentric video, from zero-shot inference to few-shot and full-data post-training. Scaling human pre-training data improves performance on both human and robot evaluations. After distillation, our model supports real-time, minute-long rollouts, enabling live VR interaction. Applications in policy evaluation, model-based planning, and world-action modeling further demonstrate the model's utility for embodied decision-making.

</details>


### [2] [PICA-WAM: Visual Perturbation Invariance via Cross-Appearance Self-Alignment in World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26813)
*Yude Zou,Yifei Yao,Guanbo Jiao,Jianming Ma,Guanjie Zheng,Xing Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PICA-WAM 是一种面向世界动作模型（WAM）的自监督训练框架，通过跨外观自对齐提升机器人策略对任务无关视觉扰动的鲁棒性，并在多个仿真基准和真实机器人上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAM 同时预测未来视频并生成动作，但仍易受任务无关视觉外观扰动影响。现有训练存在缺口：视频预测倾向于保留外观信息，而动作生成未显式约束对外观扰动的不变性；数据增强未要求扰动与正常观测产生一致动作表征，对齐外部预训练特征也不能保证所需不变性。

Method: 提出 PICA-WAM，将扰动不变性建模为辅助的跨外观自对齐任务。用 EMA 教师处理正常观测并输出更深层目标，在线 WAM 处理扰动观测，并通过轻量投影头将其较浅层动作表征对齐到教师目标，实现自监督训练。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 2.0、LIBERO-Plus、EBench 和真实机器人上评估四种强 WAM，PICA-WAM 一致提升视觉扰动下的鲁棒性，在仿真中保持正常性能，并提高真实机器人成功率；逐层诊断显示其降低视频特征对任务无关外观的敏感性及其向动作的传播。

Conclusion: PICA-WAM 通过跨外观自对齐为 WAM 提供显式扰动不变性约束，是一种有效且可推广的训练框架，可增强仿真与真实环境中的机器人策略鲁棒性。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) jointly predict future video and generate actions, yet remain sensitive to task-irrelevant visual perturbations, i.e., changes in visual appearance that should not affect robot behavior. We identify a gap in conventional WAM training: video prediction encourages representations to preserve appearance information, while action generation does not explicitly enforce invariance to task-irrelevant appearance. Data augmentation exposes WAMs to such perturbations but does not require perturbed and nominal observations to produce consistent action representations, and alignment with external pretrained features does not guarantee the desired invariance. To address this gap, we propose PICA-WAM, a self-supervised training framework that casts perturbation invariance as an auxiliary cross-appearance self-alignment task for WAMs. An exponential-moving-average (EMA) teacher processes the nominal observation to provide a deeper-layer target, while the online WAM processes a perturbed observation and aligns its shallower-layer action representation to this target through a lightweight projection head. Across four strong WAMs evaluated on RoboTwin 2.0, LIBERO-Plus, EBench, and a real robot, PICA-WAM consistently improves robustness under visual perturbations, preserves nominal performance in simulation, and raises the real robot success rate. PICA-WAM further reduces the sensitivity of video features to task-irrelevant appearance and its propagation into actions, as shown by paired layer-wise diagnostics. Code: this https URL

</details>


### [3] [Foundation Model Driven Optical Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26690)
*Praroop Chanda,Suryansh Kumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究冻结的预训练视觉与深度表征在监督匹配网络下对稠密光流的支持，构建基于TTSFlow、DPT特征解码和单次由粗到细精化的光流流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索能否复用冻结的基础模型骨干（DINOv2与Depth Anything V2）并结合可训练Transformer匹配模块，在仅训练光流匹配部分的情况下获得高质量稠密光流。

Method: 融合冻结DINOv2和Depth Anything V2特征，采用DPT特征解码与一个由粗到细精化阶段，通过可训练的Transformer匹配流水线估计光流；在FlyingChairs和FlyingThings3D上训练。

Result: 在Sintel训练集Final pass上达到2.37 EPE，优于文中FlowSeek (T)的2.52；RoCo-Spring 2026提交取得robustness-balanced score 1.1038、Spring EPE 1.411、RobustSpring proxy EPE 4.739，平衡分数低于12个参考基线中的7个。

Conclusion: 结果表明，复用冻结基础模型骨干并仅进行一次精化的完整流水线可有效支持稠密光流，并在鲁棒性平衡指标上优于多数参考基线。

Abstract: We investigate how frozen pretrained visual and depth representations support dense optical flow when coupled with a supervised matching network. Building on TTSFlow, this paper studies Dense Prediction Transformer (DPT) feature decoding and one coarse-to-fine refinement stage. Our method fuses features from frozen DINOv2 and Depth Anything V2 models and estimates optical flow through a trainable transformer-based matching pipeline. After optical-flow training on FlyingChairs and FlyingThings3D, it achieves 2.37 endpoint error (EPE) on the Sintel training split, Final pass, compared with 2.52 for the FlowSeek (T) configuration reported here. Our RoCo-Spring 2026 entry, this http URL , obtains a robustness-balanced score of 1.1038, Spring EPE of 1.411, and RobustSpring proxy EPE of 4.739, with a lower balanced score than seven of the twelve listed reference baselines. These results characterize the complete pipeline, which reuses frozen foundation backbones and performs one refinement stage. Code is available at: \url{ this https URL }

</details>


### [4] [CondenseVLA: Learnable History Condensation for Efficient Multi-Frame VLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25927)
*Feiyang Hong,Yujie Wei,Bo Zhao,Xiu Su,Hongxun Yao,Shuo Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CondenseVLA 提出一种多帧视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略，在解码器侧引入可学习的历史浓缩模块，将缓存的逐帧视觉特征压缩为固定数量的 slot token，用于动作解码，从而更有效地利用短期视觉上下文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作常依赖短暂接触、临时遮挡和中间物体状态等短期线索，这些线索在单帧中具有歧义，需要跨时间整合观测才能消解。仅通过扩大历史窗口注入历史信息计算代价高，且因冗余大、任务无关且不灵活的观测采样而收益有限。

Method: 给定 VLM 主干缓存的逐帧特征序列，CondenseVLA 使用可学习查询和多头交叉注意力将观测历史蒸馏为固定的一组 slot token。方法加入简单辅助正则：惩罚 slot 注意力图之间的重叠，并使 slot 嵌入去相关，以鼓励互补注意并减少 slot 冗余。

Result: 在评估任务中，CondenseVLA 将 Simpler-WidowX 成功率从 58.7% 提升到 69.8%（+11.1 个百分点），在 LIBERO 上达到 97.2%，真实世界任务达到 52.8%，在依赖记忆的 RMBench 任务上达到 21.6%。

Conclusion: 结果支持在动作解码中选择性浓缩有界近期视觉证据的有效性；同时，更困难的多观测任务暴露出有限历史窗口的局限性。

Abstract: Robot manipulation often depends on short-term cues - such as brief contacts, temporary occlusions, and intermediate object states - that are ambiguous in a single frame and can only be resolved by integrating observations over time. However, injecting historical information solely by enlarging the history window is computationally costly and frequently yields limited gains due to substantial redundancy and task-agnostic, inflexible sampling of observations. We propose CondenseVLA, a multi-frame VLA policy with a learnable decoder-side history condensation module for using short-term visual context in action decoding. Given a sequence of cached per-frame features from the VLM backbone, CondenseVLA distills observation history into a fixed set of slot tokens using learnable queries and multi-head cross-attention. Simple auxiliary regularization penalizes overlap among slot attention maps and decorrelates slot embeddings, encouraging complementary attention and reducing slot redundancy. Across the evaluated tasks, CondenseVLA improves Simpler-WidowX success from 58.7% to 69.8% (+11.1 percentage points), achieves 97.2% on LIBERO, and reaches 52.8% on real-world tasks. It achieves 21.6% success on memory-dependent RMBench tasks. These results support selective condensation of bounded recent visual evidence for action decoding, while harder multi-observation tasks expose the limits of a finite history window. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [5] [Think Densely, Act Sparsely: Latent Expert Cognitive Chains for Vision-Language-Action Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25734)
*Jie Wang,Guang Li,Zhijian Huang,Jinlong Li,Chenxu Dang,Hangjun Ye,Yahong Han,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出“Think Densely, Act Sparsely”范式，并实现为 LECDrive：在 VLA 模型中嵌入由任务特定 latent token 构成的渐进式专家认知链，以稠密潜在世界表征增强自动驾驶感知与规划，同时保持稀疏动作输出和高效推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 自动驾驶模型通常仅用语言指令或轨迹等稀疏信号监督，导致从稠密视觉观测到稀疏表示的映射过程中压缩甚至丢失关键场景信息；作者认为瓶颈不在模型容量，而在缺乏促使 latent space 形成稠密世界表征的显式机制。

Method: LECDrive 模拟人类层次化感知过程，在 VLA 中注入一组紧凑的任务特定 latent token 作为认知载体，使模型内化基础视觉模型提供的稠密知识，包括 DINOv3 的视觉表征、SAM3 的语义结构和 DepthAnything3 的空间几何，并采用课程学习原则按“表征—语义—几何”渐进组织监督，形成相互依赖的潜在专家认知链。

Result: 大量实验表明，LECDrive 在多个自动驾驶基准上持续提升基础 VLM 的性能，覆盖物体感知、状态预测和轨迹规划等关键任务。

Conclusion: 通过结构化潜在专家链构建稠密世界模型，是提升 VLA 自动驾驶性能与可解释性的有效方向，说明稠密潜在认知与稀疏动作输出可以协同实现安全驾驶所需的场景理解。

Abstract: Autonomous driving fundamentally demands dense understanding of visual, semantic, and geometric information, yet existing Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are typically trained with sparse supervision such as language instructions or trajectory signals. This direct mapping from dense visual observations to sparse representations forces the model to compress or even discard critical scene information essential for safe driving. We argue that this limitation does not stem from insufficient model capacity, but from the lack of explicit mechanisms that encourage the formation of dense world representations in the latent space. In this spirit, we propose a novel paradigm for driving intelligence, Think Densely, Act Sparsely, and instantiate it with an efficient and interpretable framework, LECDrive (Latent Expert Cognitive Chains for Vision-Language-Action autonomous Driving). The core idea is to mimic the hierarchical perception process of humans by embedding a progressive chain of latent expert cognition within the VLA model. Concretely, we inject a compact set of task-specific latent tokens as carriers, enabling the model to internalize dense knowledge from foundational vision models, including visual representations (DINOv3), semantic structures (SAM3), and spatial geometry (DepthAnything3), while maintaining efficient inference. This interdependent cognitive chain follows a curriculum learning principle, where supervision is progressively structured from representation to semantics and geometry. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LECDrive consistently improves the performance of base VLMs across multiple autonomous driving benchmarks, covering key tasks such as object perception, state prediction, and trajectory planning. These findings suggest that constructing dense world models through structured latent expert chains is a promising direction for VLA-based autonomous driving.

</details>


### [6] [TNVD-SC: Diagnosing Scan Context Failures in Forests with Terrain-Normalized Vertical Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25637)
*Jineon Kim,Jhonghyun An*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文诊断了森林长期LiDAR位置识别中Scan Context因重复植被和不平坦地形导致的结构混叠问题，并提出地形归一化垂直分布Scan Context（TNVD-SC），在保持环-扇区拓扑的同时编码地形相对垂直概率分布，显著提升检索性能并给出无损高效实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 森林长期LiDAR位置识别困难，重复植被和起伏地形造成严重结构混叠。作者将候选检索与精细匹配分离后发现：TNVD支持键可使约99%查询的有效位置进入top-25，但传统SC匹配仅达61.84% mean R@1；相同原始SC候选集下，地形归一化对max-height单元仅提升1.21点，而对垂直分布单元提升19.72点，说明应重点建模地形相对垂直分布。

Method: 提出TNVD-SC：保留Scan Context的ring-sector拓扑，将地形相对垂直概率分布作为描述子编码；并实现无损计数编码描述子与精确层次Jensen-Shannon搜索，以降低存储和计算开销而不改变审计后的检索输出。

Result: 在标准化TreeLoc++ Wild-Places协议下，TNVD-SC达到91.83% mean R@1，在对比方法中取得最高R@1均值与R@1稳定性；但TreeLoc++在mean MR、MF1和AUC上更强。未改动方法在四会话CVTNet风格NCLT评估中达到85.14% mean R@1。无损计数编码实现将描述子负载降低3.86倍，计算延迟降低23.8%，且不改变审计检索输出。

Conclusion: TNVD-SC通过地形归一化垂直分布编码有效缓解森林LiDAR位置识别的结构混叠，在R@1均值和稳定性上表现突出，并提供无损、低负载、低延迟的实现；但其并非在所有指标上均优于TreeLoc++。

Abstract: Long-term LiDAR place recognition in forests is difficult because repetitive vegetation and uneven terrain create severe structural aliasing. We diagnose Scan Context (SC) by separating candidate retrieval from fine matching: on the standardized TreeLoc++ Wild-Places protocol, a TNVD support key places a valid location in the top-25 set for approximately 99% of queries, while conventional SC matching on those candidates reaches only 61.84% mean R@1. With an identical raw-SC candidate set, terrain normalization improves max-height cells by only 1.21 points but vertical-distribution cells by 19.72 points. These observations motivate Terrain-Normalized Vertical Distribution Scan Context (TNVD-SC), which preserves the SC ring-sector topology while encoding terrain-relative vertical probability distributions. Under the standardized TreeLoc++ protocol, TNVD-SC reaches 91.83% mean R@1 and attains the highest R@1 mean and R@1 stability among the compared methods, while TreeLoc++ remains stronger in mean MR, MF1, and AUC. The unchanged method reaches 85.14% mean R@1 on the four-session CVTNet-style NCLT evaluation. A lossless count-coded implementation with exact hierarchical Jensen-Shannon search reduces descriptor payload by 3.86x and compute latency by 23.8% without changing the audited retrieval outputs.

</details>


### [7] [Where Do the Gaussians Go? Foreground Attraction for Camera-Based Weakly Supervised 3D Occupancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25631)
*Jeagon Song,Jhonghyun An*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种仅训练时使用的前景吸引损失：利用伪深度将前景伪标签像素反投影到3D，并把附近高斯拉向这些点，以增强稀疏高斯表示对前景的建模；推理网络保持不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于相机3D语义占用模型可通过将预测渲染到图像并用2D伪标签监督来免3D标签训练，但在稀疏高斯表示中，高斯位置仅通过像素级渲染损失学习，小前景目标权重不足；已有GaussianFlowOcc模型中前景体素上的高斯密度平均仅为背景的1.62倍。

Method: 设计仅训练用的前景吸引损失：对前景伪标签像素结合伪深度反投影得到3D点，将附近高斯拉向这些点，并偏向已预测目标类别的高斯；不修改推理网络。

Result: 在Occ3D-nuScenes上，三个随机种子下前景高斯密度集中度提升至2.40；平均前景IoU从6.29升至6.96且每个种子均提高，而平均mIoU从13.53到13.82的变化处于基线种子间波动范围内。单次运行消融表明伪标签目标优于真值体素目标，仅作用于高斯位置不足；单种子测试显示推理时保留更少高斯时模型退化更小。

Conclusion: 该损失能有效提高稀疏高斯表示的前景集中度和前景IoU，但mIoU提升尚不显著；结果支持伪标签反投影目标与位置调整的作用，同时提示需更多种子验证整体分割收益。

Abstract: Camera-based 3D semantic occupancy models can be trained without 3D labels by rendering their predictions into the images and supervising them with 2D pseudo labels. In sparse Gaussian representations trained this way, Gaussian positions are learned only through pixel-wise rendering losses, which we hypothesize give small foreground objects little weight. In trained GaussianFlowOcc models, the Gaussian density on foreground voxels is on average only 1.62 times that on background voxels. We propose a training-only foreground attraction loss that back-projects foreground pseudo-label pixels with pseudo depth and pulls nearby Gaussians toward these 3D points, favoring Gaussians that already predict the target class; the inference network is unchanged. On Occ3D-nuScenes, the loss raises this concentration to 2.40 over three seeds. Over three seeds, the mean foreground IoU increases from 6.29 to 6.96 and is higher for each seed, whereas the change in mean mIoU (13.53 to 13.82) is within the seed-to-seed variation of the baseline. Single-run ablations further indicate that pseudo-label targets outperform targets placed at ground-truth voxels and that acting on the Gaussian positions alone is not sufficient, and a single-seed test shows that the model degrades less when fewer Gaussians are kept at inference.

</details>


### [8] [LateMatch: Breaking the Interaction Bottleneck via Cacheable Matching for Efficient Visual Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25358)
*Mohammad Omama,Aeyan Ashraf,Sandeep Chinchali*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LateMatch 通过将图像匹配中的跨图像交互推迟到网络末层，实现可缓存的视觉定位匹配，大幅降低边缘设备上的计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准深度特征匹配器在每一层紧耦合图像内上下文构建与图像间匹配，导致单次查询对 K 张数据库图像需完整执行 K 次网络且无法缓存，成为边缘部署的严重交互瓶颈。

Method: LateMatch 重构现有匹配器，将图像内处理隔离为可离线缓存的 Context Blocks，仅在线执行少量 Interaction Blocks；并引入 E-SToRE，利用后段 Interaction Blocks 的香农熵下降趋势，在跨图像交互前动态减少 token 数量。

Result: 在 HPatches、ScanNet、MegaDepth、ETH-3D 和 Aachen 上，LateMatch 相比标准深度匹配器降低超过 2 倍有效计算成本，E-SToRE 额外节省 10% 至 14%，且精度下降可忽略。

Conclusion: 可缓存图像匹配范式能显著提升深度匹配器在边缘设备视觉定位中的部署效率，并在计算成本与精度之间取得良好权衡。

Abstract: Visual localization is critical for embodied agents, but deploying state-of-the-art deep feature matchers on edge devices is fundamentally limited by their massive computational cost. Standard deep matchers tightly interleave intra-image context building and inter-image matching at every layer, creating a severe interaction bottleneck. Localizing a single query against K database images forces the system to execute the full network K independent times with no capacity for caching. To break this bottleneck, we advocate for a paradigm shift toward cacheable image matching. We realize this paradigm with LateMatch, a versatile approach that restructures existing matchers by deferring inter-image interaction to their final layers. By isolating intra-image processing into offline-cacheable Context Blocks, only a small set of Interaction Blocks must be executed online. To further optimize LateMatch, we introduce E-SToRE (Entropy-guided Spatial Token Reduction and Expansion). E-SToRE leverages the decreasing Shannon entropy of the late Interaction Blocks to dynamically reduce token counts just prior to cross-image interaction. Evaluated across HPatches, ScanNet, MegaDepth, ETH-3D, and Aachen, LateMatch reduces effective computational cost by over 2x compared to standard deep matchers, while E-SToRE yields an additional 10% to 14% savings with negligible degradation in accuracy.

</details>


### [9] [Scene-Adaptive VLA: Efficient Autonomous Driving via Dynamic Layer Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24590)
*Yujia Yang,Quan Yuan,Jiawei Fan,Yang Li,Tiange Fu,Xiaoyuan Fu,Guiyang Luo,Jinglin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Scene-Adaptive VLA框架，利用动态层路由根据实时场景复杂度调整活跃参数，从而降低自动驾驶VLA模型的推理计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型参数规模大，车载实时推理受算力和能耗限制；现有高效VLA研究多集中在Chain-of-Thought和token调整等序列适配，忽视动态驾驶场景中的参数冗余和结构适配。

Method: 基于帧级时序场景变化和模型预测不确定性刻画场景复杂度，并将其编码为scene-aware tokens及两个专用query；采用预算条件层路由机制评估query，分配预算以确定活跃参数比例并选择层组合，整个框架端到端优化。

Result: 在Bench2Drive基准上适配先进VLA模型后，推理阶段计算开销降低53.5%，驾驶能力和语言能力几乎无退化。

Conclusion: 动态层路由的结构自适应能有效压缩VLA推理成本，在保持驾驶与语言能力的同时提升车载部署效率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models offer a promising paradigm for autonomous driving. However, the massive parameter scale of VLAs strains onboard computational resources during real-time inference. This directly increases energy consumption and limits vehicle battery range, making it critical to minimize computational overhead without compromising driving capability and explainability. Current research on efficient VLA-based driving focuses on sequential adaptation (\textit{e.g.}, Chain-of-Thought and token adjustment), leaving structural adaptation underexplored despite parameter redundancy across dynamic driving scenes. To address this gap, we propose Scene-Adaptive VLA, an efficient framework leveraging dynamic layer routing to adjust active parameters based on real-time scenes. Inspired by the temporal continuity of scenes and model predictive uncertainty, our approach characterizes model-perceived scene complexity via frame-level temporal scene variations and decision deviations. To uncover latent scene-to-parameter correspondences, we encode this characterization into scene-aware tokens, alongside two specialized queries. A budget-conditioned layer routing mechanism evaluates these refined queries to allocate a budget that determines the active parameter ratio and subsequently selects appropriate layer combinations. The entire framework is optimized end-to-end. Extensive evaluations on the Bench2Drive benchmark show that when adapted to a state-of-the-art VLA model, our approach significantly reduces inference-time computational overhead by 53.5\% with negligible degradation in driving and language capabilities. Code is available at https:github.com/yang-luvu/Scene-Adaptive-VLA .

</details>


### [10] [MOMO: Human Motion from Moving Sounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24522)
*Deli Cai,Shuyang Xu,Zhiyang Dou,Zitong Lan,Mingmin Zhao,Changxing Ding,Taku Komura*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出动态空间音频驱动人体运动生成新任务，构建DySAM与DySAL数据集，并提出扩散模型MOMO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频驱动运动生成主要关注非空间或静态声学条件，忽略了移动声源与听者之间动态空间关系及其时间演化，难以建模人类对动态空间音频的反应。

Method: 形式化动态空间音频驱动人体运动生成任务；构建DySAM数据集，提供同步双耳音频、3D人体运动和移动声源轨迹；构建DySAL辅助双耳定位数据集，配对声学事件、头部姿态和移动声源轨迹；提出MOMO扩散框架，从原始双耳音频估计头中心空间方向线索，提取逐帧音频内容表征，并以语义特征和动态空间线索为条件生成人体运动。

Result: 实验表明MOMO在运动保真度、音频-运动对齐和物理合理性方面优于现有基线。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了动态空间音频驱动人体运动生成的空白，提供了数据集、基准任务和有效的扩散生成方法，对角色动画、行为分析和机器人学具有意义。

Abstract: Human responses to sound depend not only on its semantic content, but also on the evolving spatial relationship between source and listener, conveyed through spatial audio and its temporal dynamics. Modeling human reactions to such dynamic spatial audio is important for character animation, human behavior analysis, and robotics. As of yet, existing audio-driven motion generation methods primarily focus on non-spatial or static acoustic conditions, leaving this important problem largely underexplored. To bridge this gap, we formulate the novel task of dynamic spatial-audio-driven human motion generation and introduce two motion-captured datasets. We introduce the Dynamic Spatial Audio and Motion dataset (DySAM) as the first dataset and benchmark for this task, providing synchronized binaural audio, 3D human motions, and moving sound source trajectories. Given the importance of dynamic spatial cues of sound sources, we further provide the Dynamic Spatial Audio Localization dataset (DySAL), an auxiliary binaural localization dataset that pairs diverse acoustic events with head poses and moving source trajectories to support spatial audio perception. For benchmarking, we propose MOMO for human Motion generation from Moving Sounds, a diffusion-based framework guided by three coupled principles: localization, understanding, and generation. MOMO performs localization by estimating head-centric spatial direction cues of the moving source directly from raw binaural audio, avoiding reliance on ground-truth source coordinates. It performs understanding by extracting frame-wise audio-content representations, and performs generation by conditioning motion diffusion on both semantic features and dynamic spatial cues. Experiments show that MOMO outperforms existing baselines in motion fidelity, audio-motion alignment, and physical plausibility.

</details>


### [11] [HAVANA: A Hierarchical Video-Action Model for Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24122)
*Dongzhihan Wang,Yi Du,Chen Wang,Xiuxian Li,Liang Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HAVANA 提出一种双系统视频-动作模型，将视觉-语言导航中的预测性推理与反应式局部规划解耦：慢系统生成语言条件潜在计划，快系统基于最新计划与当前观测生成局部轨迹，实现异步执行并避免推理时生成未来视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言导航（VLN）同时需要长时程、指令一致的规划与短时程、基于当前几何的局部规划。现有视频-动作模型（VAM）在单一过程中联合预测视觉未来与局部轨迹，但二者更新节奏不同：未来预测在多次更新中保持稳定，而局部轨迹需对最新观测快速反应。

Method: 提出 HAVANA 双系统 VAM，将预测性推理与反应式局部规划解耦。慢系统从观测历史生成紧凑的、语言条件化的潜在计划；快系统结合最新计划与当前观测生成局部轨迹。两系统异步执行，使机器人无需重复未来预测即可快速适应，且推理阶段不生成未来视频。

Result: 在 OmniNavBench 上取得优于已有基线的最先进导航性能；真实世界部署进一步表明其在不同机器人形态下对未见环境具有稳健泛化能力。

Conclusion: HAVANA 通过解耦慢速预测规划与快速反应规划，有效弥合 VLN 中长时程指令一致性与短时程局部反应性的节奏差异，并在基准与真实部署中验证了有效性与泛化性。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) requires long-horizon instruction-consistent planning and short-horizon local planning in current geometry. Video-action models (VAMs) offer a promising way to learn predictive representations for navigation. However, existing VAMs jointly predict visual futures and local trajectories in a single process, even though they operate at different paces: future prediction remains stable across multiple updates while local trajectories must react to fresh observations. To bridge this gap, we introduce HAVANA, a dual-system VAM that decouples predictive reasoning from reactive local planning. A slow system produces a compact language-conditioned latent plan from observation history, while a fast system combines the latest plan with the current observation to generate local trajectories. Their asynchronous execution enables rapid adaptation without repeated future prediction, and no future video is generated during inference. Experiments on OmniNavBench demonstrate state-of-the-art navigation performance over prior baselines, while real-world deployments further show robust generalization to unseen environments across different robot embodiments. Our code and checkpoints will be available at this https URL .

</details>


### [12] [IMPULSE: Push First, Then Predict in V-JEPA 2 Feature Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23968)
*Tingan Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 IMPULSE，在冻结的 V-JEPA 2 特征空间中，通过少量接触推挤推断物体物理响应，并让预测器利用这些经验提升未来特征预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在世界模型如 V-JEPA 2 能在视频编码器特征空间中预测，但仅看一眼物体无法知道其旋转、弹跳、滑动等物理属性；人类通过尝试和推挤来发现物体如何响应。

Method: 用贝叶斯接触记忆总结三次短校准推挤；将预测的物体位姿传入预测器；用轻量随机傅里叶位姿头使预测器利用位姿信息。

Result: 在 512 个新物体上，预注册留出确认显示：相比无经验预测器，记忆使 1 秒特征预测误差降低 11.6–12.8%，8 个训练种子均成立且 95% 区间排除零；对 128 个超出范围物理属性物体增益为 11.7–14.0%；完整扩展比普通预测器好 15.5–16.6%。信息阶梯论证表明物理信息仅通过运动进入特征；傅里叶头使完美记忆相对普通位姿 token 的增益约提升至 2.8–3.2 倍；原始推挤测量经同一通路也有类似增益，说明贝叶斯总结非必需，但将试验压缩为八个可解释数字；增益在接触开始时不存在，之后增长。

Conclusion: 少量校准推挤可使冻结 V-JEPA 2 预测器通过位姿通路适应物体物理属性；贝叶斯接触记忆提供可解释压缩，物理信息主要通过运动影响特征预测。

Abstract: Latent world models such as V-JEPA 2 forecast in the feature space of a video encoder, but one look at an object does not reveal how hard it is to rotate, how much it bounces or how much it slides. People find out by trying: a few pokes reveal how an object responds. IMPULSE, named after the impulse response it infers, gives a predictor in frozen V-JEPA 2 feature space the same ability. A Bayesian contact memory summarizes three short calibration pushes, a predicted object pose carries it into the predictor, and a lightweight random-Fourier pose head lets the predictor use the pose. On 512 fresh objects, in a pre-specified held-out confirmation with models sealed before the data existed, the memory lowers the 1-second feature-forecast error by 11.6-12.8% relative to the same predictor without experience, for all 8 training seeds, with a 95% seed-and-object interval excluding zero. The gain also holds for 128 objects with out-of-range physics (11.7-14.0%), and the full extension beats a plain predictor by 15.5-16.6%. An information-ladder argument, exact for ideal predictors, shows that physics reaches the features only through motion, so we measure how much the pathway lets pose help. On fresh objects, the Fourier head roughly triples the gain of a perfect memory over plain pose tokens (2.8-3.2x). Raw push measurements fed through the same pathway give a similar gain, so the Bayesian summary is not required; it condenses the trials into eight interpretable numbers. The gain is absent at contact onset and grows afterwards.

</details>


### [13] [EgoVine: Post-Training with Branching Experience Compiled from Real-World Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23907)
*Qinchuan Cheng,Bowen Lyu,Zhantao Gong,Angela Yao,Cheston Tan,Shijie Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoVine 是一个将真实自我中心视频演示转化为可交互符号环境的后训练框架，通过恢复决策状态探索替代动作与失败恢复，迭代训练紧凑的长期规划器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实自我中心视频虽包含多步家庭流程，但固定录制轨迹未充分覆盖替代动作和学习者自身错误后的恢复，限制长期规划学习。

Method: EgoVine 从真实视频标注编译可执行符号环境，在部分可观测和延迟后果下协调物体操作；通过恢复决策状态探索替代动作并比较最终结果；Union 用同一执行联合训练动作生成与后果预测，以监督候选比较和恢复；经核验的修复为失败尝试添加恢复目标，采样延续间的比较指导策略更新；更新后的规划器回到环境，用自身决策和错误塑造下一轮经验。

Result: 在共享基准上，EgoVine-1.5B 在 Main planning 任务成功率达 81.8%，在更长任务组合上达 66.7%，两项均优于所有评估的 Flash LLM 基线。

Conclusion: EgoVine 将真实演示连接到迭代学习过程，使紧凑规划器能够学习录制轨迹之外的选择与未来后果。

Abstract: Real-world egocentric videos capture multi-step household procedures, but their recorded trajectories leave alternative actions and recovery from the learner's own mistakes largely unexplored. We introduce EgoVine, a post-training framework that turns a fixed demonstration set into interactive experience for long-horizon planning. EgoVine operates in executable symbolic environments compiled from real-world video annotations, where an agent must coordinate object manipulations under partial observation and account for delayed consequences. Restoring a decision state allows the learner to explore alternative actions and compare their eventual outcomes. Union jointly trains action generation and consequence prediction in one model, using the same executions to supervise candidate comparison and recovery. Verified repairs add recovery targets from failed attempts, while comparisons among sampled continuations guide policy updates. The updated planner then returns to the environments, with its own decisions and mistakes shaping the next round of experience. On a shared benchmark, EgoVine-1.5B achieves 81.8% success on Main planning tasks and 66.7% on longer task compositions, outperforming all evaluated Flash LLM baselines on both. EgoVine connects real demonstrations to an iterative learning process that trains compact planners on choices and futures beyond the recorded trajectories.

</details>

### [14] [CROPS: Cosserat-Rod-Based Co-Design of Soft Slender Programmable Material Systems for Biomedical Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24209)
*Aaditya Chandrasekhar,Dex Doksoo Lee,Hyunwoo Kwon,Shicheng Li,Wei Chen*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Cosserat杆的可微分设计优化框架，用于自主设计可编程细长软材料结构，并在可膨胀支架和磁驱动导管两个生物医学案例中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刺激响应细长结构在软体机器人、微创手术、可穿戴电子和可展开结构中应用广泛；生物医学场景需要按患者定制材料布局与驱动方式，但几何与材料非线性使结构-驱动-响应关系高度非直观且计算昂贵，传统试错和常规设计方法难以满足患者个性化需求。

Method: 构建基于Cosserat杆表示的可微分端到端设计优化框架，同时考虑自接触及与周围结构的外部接触；涵盖凝胶-弹性体复合材料的溶胀驱动和硬磁复合材料的磁驱动两种机制，并用于优化材料布局与驱动参数。

Result: 在两个案例中展示有效性：一是设计可溶胀支架使其形貌匹配颈动脉血管；二是协同设计磁驱动导管的剩余磁化与应用磁场，以降低插入过程中对患者特定血管壁的作用力。

Conclusion: 该框架为支持大变形和多物理场驱动的软细长材料系统提供了面向个性化生物医学设计的自主设计平台。

Abstract: Slender structures made of stimuli-responsive materials represent an emerging class of programmable material systems, with applications in soft robotics, minimally invasive surgery, wearable electronics, and deployable structures. In biomedical applications, the on-demand design of slender structures, encompassing both their material layout and actuation, is essential to harnessing their large deformation and multiphysics actuation to meet patient-specific design requirements. However, owing to geometric and material nonlinearities, their structure-actuation-response relationship is highly nonintuitive and computationally demanding. This often makes trial-and-error and conventional design approaches challenging for addressing patient-specific needs. To this end, we present a Cosserat-rod-based design optimization framework for the autonomous design of programmable slender structures. Leveraging the Cosserat rod representation, the end-to-end differentiable framework is demonstrated on two case studies: (i) design of swellable stents that morph to the shape of carotid vessels; (ii) co-design of the remnant magnetization and the applied field of magnetically-active catheters to reduce the force exerted on the patient-specific vessel wall during insertion. Both self-contact and external contact with surrounding structures are accounted for. We consider two actuation mechanism, namely the swelling of gel-elastomer composites and the magnetic actuation of hard-magnetic composites. The proposed framework presents an autonomous design platform of soft slender material systems that support large deformations and multiphysics actuation toward personalized biomedical design.

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [15] [Finite-Time Capture by a Dubins Pursuer Against Unknown Evader Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25007)
*Dinesh Patra,Bhargav Jha,Ashish R. Hota*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 研究Dubins追捕者捕获速度较慢、可瞬时改变航向的一阶点质量逃逸者的平面追逃问题，提出带单增益的转向率反馈律并给出保证有限时间捕获的增益区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对追捕者受转向率约束、逃逸者速度较慢但航向可瞬时变化的情形，需要设计简单反馈律，并明确在转向能力限制下仍能保证捕获的条件。

Method: 提出仅含一个可调增益的转向率反馈律；推导保证对任意可行逃逸策略实现有限时间捕获的显式可行增益区间；给出捕获时间上界，并刻画使该增益区间非空所需的转向能力；进一步推导仅由追捕者参数计算的速度上限，以消除对逃逸者速度界的依赖。

Result: 得到保证有限时间捕获的显式增益区间及捕获时间上界；刻画了转向权限与可行增益区间非空性的关系；证明了在最大允许增益下，当逃逸者速度低于某一仅由追捕者参数确定的速度上限时，捕获保证仍成立。

Conclusion: 所提出的单增益转向率反馈律可在明确转向率约束下保证有限时间捕获，并通过速度上限条件降低对逃逸者速度信息的依赖；数值仿真验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: We consider a planar pursuit-evasion problem in which a Dubins pursuer seeks to capture a slower first-order point-mass evader that can change its heading instantaneously. First, we propose a turn-rate feedback law with a single tunable gain and derive an explicit admissible gain interval that guarantees finite-time capture against any admissible evader strategy while satisfying the turn-rate constraint. Next, we obtain an explicit capture-time bound and characterize the turning authority required for the gain interval to be non-empty. Finally, we show that the dependence on the evader's speed bound can be removed by deriving a speed ceiling, computable solely from the pursuer's parameters, below which the guarantees hold at the maximum allowable gain. Numerical simulations illustrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [16] [Transmission Error Compensation for Industrial Robots Using Fourier Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23882)
*Christian J. E. Bauer,Simon Nowinski,Valentin Kamm,Lukas Steinle,Armin Lechler,Alexander Verl*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出基于激光跟踪仪测量辨识机器人关节传动误差的速度相关傅里叶级数模型，并将其集成到虚拟调试软件中，通过修正CNC设定点实现误差补偿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业机器人因关节柔顺性和传动误差导致精度不足，离线仿真编程后通常仍需在线示教；其中传动误差表现为减速器负载侧角度与电机编码器角度之间的偏差。

Method: 利用激光跟踪仪测量数据辨识传动误差，建立速度相关傅里叶级数模型；将该模型作为联合仿真FMU集成到虚拟调试软件中，并用于修正CNC设定点以补偿传动误差；在Stäubli TX2-40工业机器人上开展实验评估。

Result: 模型平均绝对误差为0.114 mrad；补偿后轴2精度提升83.26%，轴3精度提升49.21%。

Conclusion: 所提传动误差辨识与补偿方法可有效提高工业机器人关节精度，并支持虚拟调试环境下的误差建模与补偿应用。

Abstract: Due to their low accuracy, industrial robots commonly require online teaching after the offline development of a robot program in simulation. The accuracy is limited by compliance and transmission errors in the robot joints. Transmission errors are defined as errors between the load-side angles of the gearboxes and the motor encoder angles. An approach to identify the transmission errors based on laser tracker measurements is presented. The developed velocity-dependent Fourier series model is integrated in a virtual commissioning software as a co-simulated functional mock-up unit. Additionally, it is used to compensate the transmission errors by correcting the setpoint from the computerized numerical control (CNC). The model identification and the compensation are experimentally evaluated with a Stäubli TX2-40 industrial robot. The model has a mean absolute error of 0.114 mrad, and the compensation improves the accuracy by 83.26 % for axis 2 and 49.21 % for axis 3.

</details>


### [17] [Lubricant Temperature Observer for Gearboxes in Industrial Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23881)
*Christian J. E. Bauer,Markus Neher,Valentin Kamm,Lukas Steinle,Armin Lechler,Alexander Verl*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 针对工业机器人因温度引起的定位与轨迹误差，提出用观测器估计减速器润滑脂温度，为温度相关误差补偿提供输入；基于双状态传热模型构建Luenberger观测器和Kalman滤波器，实验验证平均绝对误差为1.3 K。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业机器人定位与路径精度受温度显著影响，尤其是温度相关摩擦以及减速器齿轮箱中滞回和传动误差变化；标准工业机器人缺乏温度传感器，因此需要估计齿轮箱润滑剂温度以支持温度相关误差补偿。

Method: 建立并辨识描述润滑剂经齿轮箱壳体向环境传热的双状态传热模型，将其集成到Luenberger观测器和Kalman滤波器中，并利用壳体温度传感器测量对润滑剂温度估计进行校正；在填充润滑脂的摆线齿轮实验装置上进行温度测量与验证。

Result: 考虑润滑脂在不同剪切速率下粘度与热导率变化导致其热行为较齿轮油更不均匀，所开发观测器在验证测量中取得1.3 K的平均绝对误差。

Conclusion: 所提出的润滑剂温度观测器能够以较小误差估计齿轮箱润滑剂温度，可作为工业机器人减速器温度相关误差补偿的输入。

Abstract: The position and path accuracy of industrial robots is strongly affected by temperature. In particular, temperature-dependent friction and altered hysteresis and transmission errors in the gearboxes are the main causes of these deviations. Since standard industrial robots lack temperature sensors, it is proposed to estimate the gearbox lubricant temperature using observer structures. It is planned to apply the proposed lubricant temperature observer to provide the input for temperature-dependent error compensation for gearboxes in industrial robots. First, a two-state heat transfer model describing the heat transfer from the lubricant through the gearbox housing to the environment is described and identified. It is integrated into a Luenberger observer and a Kalman filter with a correction of the lubricant temperature estimation based on measurements from a housing temperature sensor. Temperature measurements were performed in an experimental setup with a cycloidal gear filled with grease, which is common for industrial robots. Due to its changing viscosity and changing thermal conductivity at different shear rates, it shows a less uniform thermal behavior than gear-oil. For the validation measurements, the developed observers showed a mean absolute error of 1.3 K.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [18] [DUET: Decoupled Upper- and Lower-Body Execution for Task-Agnostic Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23872)
*Kapalik Khanal,Biswash Khatiwada,Stephen Afrifa,Fan Wu,Ramesh Bahadur Bist*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DUET提出一种解耦执行框架，将人形机器人的运动控制与操作策略分离：下层运动策略维持平衡，上层操作策略仅通过机器人接口叠加命令，从而无需联合训练即可更换操作策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改变人形机器人的操作策略时，不应需要重新训练维持机器人平衡的控制器；目标是实现运动与操作的模块化并行执行，并支持即插即用的操作策略替换。

Method: 在23自由度Unitree G1上，使用强化学习运动策略控制12个腿部关节和1个腰部偏航关节，跟踪平面速度、转向率和骨盆高度；10个手臂关节不进入动作空间，仅将其位置和速度作为观测，使任意兼容的操作策略可生成手臂关节目标并控制上半身。硬件上同一运动检查点分别搭配GR00T N1.7和pi0.5两个视觉-语言-动作模型。

Result: 仿真中运动控制器保持下半身稳定且无跌倒，平面速度、偏航率和骨盆高度跟踪误差分别为0.186 m/s、0.142 rad/s和0.016 m。消融表明上半身姿态课程是主导因素，移除后回合存活时间从20.0秒降至7.4秒，跌倒率增至每环境分钟6.06次。鲁棒性测试中，训练负载范围内最高1.0 m/s推力下无跌倒；两倍负载上限下，低于0.7 m/s推力下也无跌倒。

Conclusion: 结果表明，操作策略可以在不重新训练、不重启且不修改维持机器人直立层的情况下进行交换，验证了运动与操作解耦框架的模块化和可替换性。

Abstract: Changing a humanoid robot's manipulation policy should not require retraining the controller that maintains the robot's balance. This study introduces DUET, a decoupled execution framework that enables locomotion and manipulation controllers to operate simultaneously by combining their commands only at the robot interface. A reinforcement-learning locomotion policy controls the 12 leg joints and a waist yaw joint, tracking planar velocity, turning rate, and pelvis height from deep crouching to upright standing on a 23-degree-of-freedom Unitree G1. The 10 arm joints are excluded from the action space, while their positions and velocities are included only as observations, so that any compatible manipulation policy that generates arm-joint targets can control the upper body, and the locomotion policy keeps the lower body stable without requiring shared training or direct coordination. This study demonstrates the modular approach on hardware by pairing the same locomotion checkpoint with two different vision-language-action models, GR00T N1.7 and pi0.5. In simulation evaluation, the locomotion controller kept the robot's lower body stable with no falls while tracking the commanded planar velocity, yaw rate, and pelvis height with errors of 0.186 m/s, 0.142 rad/s, and 0.016 m, respectively. Ablation studies identify the upper-body pose curriculum as the dominant component, and removing this training step reduces episode survival from 20.0 to 7.4 seconds and increases the fall rate to 6.06 per environment minute. Robustness tests show no falls under pushes of up to 1.0 m/s within the trained payload range, and no falls at twice the payload limit for pushes below 0.7 m/s. These results show that a manipulation policy can be exchanged without retraining, restarting, or otherwise touching the layer that keeps the robot upright.

</details>


### [19] [Vision as Proprioception: Estimating Humanoid Robot Pose from Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25288)
*Jiaxi Jiang,Matthias Heyrman,René Zurbrügg,Marco Hutter,Christian Holz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 HumanoidPose，将视觉作为本体感知，从单目视频中恢复人形机器人世界坐标系下的全身关节配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在交互中的内部状态信息常因隐私、所有权或通信限制而不可用；在线与 AI 生成机器人视频虽丰富，但缺少对应关节配置，难以直接用于机器人学习。

Method: 将预训练人体姿态特征与机器人运动学结合为统一模型，预测不同 embodiment 的关节配置；把人体动作重定向到 16 种人形机器人 embodiment，以合成数据实现可扩展学习；并利用无标注在线视频自训练 2D 机器人姿态检测器以提升真实世界泛化。

Result: 在三个真实人形机器人平台上，HumanoidPose 提升了关节角与关节位置精度，相较各平台最佳基线将关节角误差降低 18-32%；同时改善全局定位，并能泛化到未见 embodiment。

Conclusion: HumanoidPose 支持交互过程中对其他机器人的视觉理解，并可从在线及生成视频中提取机器人运动。

Abstract: Estimating a humanoid robot's internal state from video has a wide range of real-world applications but remains unexplored. During interactions with a robot, its onboard state information may be unavailable due to privacy, ownership, or communication constraints. Meanwhile, online and AI-generated robot videos provide rich sources of data but typically lack corresponding joint configurations, limiting their direct use in robot learning. To address these issues, we present HumanoidPose, a new framework that treats vision as proprioception to recover full-body joint configurations in world space from video alone. Specifically, we combine pretrained human-pose features with robot kinematics in a unified model that predicts each embodiment's joint configurations. We retarget human motions to sixteen different humanoid embodiments, enabling scalable learning entirely from synthetic data. To improve real-world generalization, we further leverage unlabeled online videos through self-training to develop a 2D robot-pose detector. In our evaluation, HumanoidPose improves joint-angle and joint-position accuracy over the evaluated baselines and reduces joint-angle error by 18-32% relative to the best-performing baseline on each of the three real humanoid platforms. We further show improved global localization and generalization to unseen embodiments. HumanoidPose enables visual understanding of other robots during interaction and extraction of robot motion from online and generated videos.

</details>


### [20] [The Bitter Lesson Comes to Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25287)
*Christopher Leet,Achyutha Menon,Sravanthi Machcha,Sven Koenig,Tzu Kit Chan,Jay Chooi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文首次系统比较多种前沿多模态语言模型在真实机械臂上直接执行操作任务的能力；当前模型能完成多种操作，但精度和效率仍不足以在实用动作预算内完成任务，且能力正快速提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估通用多模态语言模型直接控制机械臂的潜力与风险：一方面可提升工业自动化，另一方面可能被恶意用于制造危险化学或生物制剂；因此需要追踪其真实硬件操作能力。

Method: 选取7个前沿多模态语言模型，在多种推理努力设置下，基于RoboDojo基准的6个操作任务，在真实物理机械臂上进行系统评测；作者称这是首个在统一操作任务套件和真实硬件上对广泛前沿模型进行的系统比较。

Result: 当前前沿模型可直接控制真实机械臂完成多种操作任务，但尚不够精确或高效，无法在实用动作预算内完成。性能快速提升：2026年7月至9月（含）对应性能倍增时间44.5天；最佳执行率提高4.4倍，最低执行成本下降6.2倍。

Conclusion: 这些趋势提示“Bitter Lesson”可能正进入机器人学：具身通用语言模型的能力未来或可与更专用模型相当。

Abstract: General purpose multimodal language models (LMs) are increasingly capable of directly controlling robotic arms (e.g., by commanding end effector poses) to manipulate their environment. Tracking the development of these capabilities is important for understanding their potential benefits, such as improving industrial automation, and risks, such as enabling malicious users to produce dangerous chemical or biological agents. To this end, we investigate the ability of frontier multimodal LMs to directly control physical robotic arms. We evaluate seven models at multiple reasoning-effort settings on six manipulation tasks taken from the RoboDojo benchmark. To our knowledge, this is the first systematic comparison of a broad range of frontier LMs across a common suite of manipulation tasks on physical hardware. We find that current frontier models can directly control real robotic arms to perform a variety of manipulation tasks but are not yet precise or efficient enough to complete them within a practical action budget. However, these capabilities are improving rapidly: performance increases on our benchmark between July and September 2026 (inclusive) correspond to a performance doubling time of 44.5 days, while the best recorded execution rate increased by a factor of 4.4 and the lowest recorded execution cost fell by a factor of 6.2. These trends raise the possibility that the Bitter Lesson is coming to robotics; the capabilities of embodied general purpose LMs may one day be comparable to those of more specialized models.

</details>


### [21] [GOFusion: Real-Time Gaussian-Occupancy Submap Fusion for Fisher-Informed Autonomous Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25261)
*Boxin Bu,Simon Boche,Haoming Zhang,Jaehyung Jung,Jiaxin Wei,Hannah Schieber,Daniel Roth,Angela P. Schoellig,Stefan Leutenegger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GOFusion 是一个面向自主机器人导航的实时视觉建图系统，将高斯基元锚定到局部占据地图，同时提供路径规划所需的空间感知与高保真外观，并支持信息驱动的导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主导航既需要用于路径规划的占据/空间信息，也需要高保真外观以支持信息驱动决策；同时大规模3D高斯建图面临实时性、几何-视觉一致性和GPU显存限制。

Method: 利用视觉惯性SLAM得到的位姿估计与局部占据地图构建3D高斯地图，并通过位姿校正保持几何与视觉一致性；将3D高斯泼溅优化限制在活跃子图以约束显存；提出光栅化集成的Fisher近似在线量化每个高斯基元的敏感度，作为高斯剪枝与下一最佳视角规划的信息驱动启发式。

Result: 在公开数据集和真实无人机飞行中评估，GOFusion在建图质量上优于多个基线，并实现大规模实时、高保真且不确定性感知的建图；利用Fisher信息启发式展示了全自主无人机探索以细化高斯地图。

Conclusion: GOFusion将3D高斯表示、局部占据地图、视觉惯性SLAM与Fisher信息敏感度分析结合，实现了面向自主机器人导航的实时高保真建图与自主探索应用。

Abstract: In this letter, we present GOFusion, a real-time visual mapping system for autonomous robot navigation that anchors Gaussian primitives to local occupancy maps, providing both spatial awareness for path planning and high-fidelity appearance that enables information-driven robot navigation. GOFusion builds 3D Gaussian maps from pose estimates and local occupancy maps obtained via visual-inertial SLAM and enables pose corrections to maintain both geometric and visual consistency. Furthermore, it restricts the 3D Gaussian Splatting optimization to active submaps to bound GPU memory usage. Building on the appearance-rich Gaussian map, we introduce a rasterizer-integrated Fisher approximation that quantifies per-primitive sensitivity online, serving as an information-driven heuristic for Gaussian pruning and next-best-view planning. We evaluate GOFusion on public datasets and in real-world drone flights. It not only outperforms several baselines in mapping quality, but also achieves real-time, high-fidelity and uncertainty-aware mapping at scale. Finally, we demonstrate fully autonomous drone exploration for Gaussian map refinement using the proposed Fisher-informed heuristic. Code is available at this https URL . We provide a supplementary video demonstration at this https URL .

</details>


### [22] [AI-Enhanced Radiation Classification in ROS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25225)
*Youngseo Lee,Cesar Alan Contreras,Tianshu Ruan,Manolis Chiou,Rustam Stolkin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究开发了两个ROS节点，用于区分七种放射性同位素，并在Jackal无人车上进行实际机器人评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在实现基于机器人平台的放射性同位素自动识别与分类，并支持后续数据集扩展。

Method: 开发模型训练节点和预测节点：训练节点使用梯度提升分类器进行同位素识别，预测节点接收CSV数据并输出分类结果；将C12137辐射探测器模块安装于Jackal UGV，由操作员接近不同辐射源采集数据；同时开发将伽马能谱图像转换为CSV文件的方法，并在三个源位置开展机器人评估。

Result: 分类器在留出测试集上的总体准确率报告为95%；机器人评估中42次试验有30次正确分类，准确率为71.43%；Am-241、Co-60、Cs-137、Na-22和Ti-44各6次试验均正确，而Ba-133和Eu-152各6次试验均错误。

Conclusion: 机器人实际试验的准确率低于留出测试集，表明实际部署场景中的分类性能存在下降，需进一步改进和验证。

Abstract: This study presents the development of two Robot Operating System (ROS) nodes with a primary focus on differentiating seven radioactive isotopes. The first node, referred to as the model training node, uses a Gradient Boosting classifier to identify and classify these isotopes. The classifier achieved a reported overall accuracy of 95\% on the held-out test dataset. The second node, known as the prediction node, provides classification results when users input data in CSV format. For the practical demonstration, the C12137 radiation detector module was mounted on the Jackal UGV, and an operator approached different radiation sources to collect data. To support future dataset expansion, we also developed a method for converting gamma-spectrum graph images into CSV files. In a separate robotic evaluation across three source locations, 30 of 42 trials were correctly classified (71.43\%). Am-241, Co-60, Cs-137, Na-22, and Ti-44 were correctly classified in all six trials each, whereas Ba-133 and Eu-152 were incorrectly classified in all six trials each. These results show lower accuracy in robotic trials than in the held-out test set.

</details>


### [23] [Structure over Reasoning: Unified Zero-Shot Navigation with a Small Vision-Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25130)
*Zeqi Li,Xunyi Zhao,Zerui Li,Sihao Lin,Qi Wu,ShiJie Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作面向真实感物理仿真场景中的零样本视觉语言导航，将小型VLM的感知判断作为证据而非直接动作，结合鸟瞰语义场、骨架图前沿与测地距离、两阶段终止器，在VLNVerse子集上超过四个零样本基线，并部署于Unitree Go2四足机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人偏好小型模型，但小型VLM能力不均衡：Qwen3-VL-4B能判断单视角是否像目标，却缺乏可靠路径推理、距离估计近乎常数、停止投票不可靠；因此需要一种不依赖其直接输出动作的导航框架。

Method: 每步向VLM提出两个感知问题：哪个方向看起来像目标、当前视角是否足以停止；对含路径指令额外进行门控前沿查询。将回答视为证据：在鸟瞰地图上构建路线盲语义场，以温度化更新累积方向分数；用骨架图提供前沿和测地距离；用两阶段终止器将停止投票转化为终止点，包含轨迹一致性门控和预算强制返回。

Result: 在同一安装与同一预算下重跑所有基线，该智能体在VLNVerse子集的粗、细两种粒度平均成功率上均超过四个零样本基线；但与最强粗粒度基线的配对区间包含零。消融显示终止机制是主要增益杠杆，十三次让模型承担更多导航任务的尝试均未带来可靠提升；系统在Unitree Go2四足机器人上以离板推理运行。

Conclusion: 小型VLM更适合作为局部感知证据源，而非端到端导航决策器；通过地图语义累积、图结构前沿与稳健终止机制，可在零样本真实感VLN中取得优势，但相对最强粗粒度基线的统计显著性和模型能力扩展仍有限。

Abstract: Vision-language navigation is moving into photorealistic, physically simulated scenes, where the leading zero-shot agents put a vision-language model in the control loop to choose every move and stop. Robots favour small models, whose abilities are uneven: Qwen3-VL-4B can judge whether a single view looks like the goal, but its route reasoning is at chance, its distance estimates nearly constant, its stop votes unreliable. Our agent therefore asks it two perceptual questions per step, which direction looks like the goal and whether the view justifies stopping, plus one gated frontier query on route-bearing instructions, and treats the answers as evidence, not actions: a route-blind semantic field over a bird's-eye map accumulates the directional scores through a tempered update, a skeleton graph supplies frontiers and geodesic distances, and a two-stage terminator turns stop votes into termini through a trail-consistency gate and a budget-forced return. With every baseline re-run in one installation at one budget, the agent exceeds all four zero-shot baselines of the VLNVerse subset in mean success on both granularities, though the paired interval against the strongest coarse-grained baseline includes zero. Termination is the dominant lever in ablation, thirteen attempts to give the model more of the navigation establish no reliable gain, and the agent runs on a Unitree Go2 quadruped with off-board inference. Code, per-episode logs and a robot video will be released at this https URL .

</details>


### [24] [Compile Once, Run Cheap: Frozen, Portable Skills for Embodied Agents, and What Recursive Self-Improvement Adds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25114)
*Jingwen Bai,Guochao Bai,Canbo Zhang,Shuangqi Li,Minchao Hu,Weichun Huang,Shuang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 Skill-RSI：让强通用模型仅在离线阶段编写、运行、诊断并修复仿真中的操作技能，通过隐藏布局考试后冻结为代码，再由廉价调用器复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身 AI 缺乏语言模型式的大规模预训练数据，新操作能力通常需要每任务演示或每回合调用前沿模型，成本高且难扩展。

Method: 定义技能级递归自我改进与编译协议（隔离审计作者、隐藏验证布局、无反馈与仅继承对照、防篡改账本），以及跨模型族执行器的复用协议；在 RoboTwin 2.0 杂乱桌面任务上评估冻结技能的编译成功率、修复策略、继承效应和部署成本。

Result: 7 个任务中 4 个编译出 7 个冻结技能，作者成本每技能 4.29-8.22 美元，计入 13 次失败则约 55 美元；3 个任务 18 次 campaign 未编译成功，最难任务得分 78-95/100 但有 3-16 次安全中止，未达 95% 成功率且零中止门槛。隐藏布局上继承式修复 8/8 优于重写（p=0.004），仅继承血统解释大部分差距；写一次实验未检测到初稿练习效应（MDE=14 点）。冻结技能在新布局达 148/150（无模型）和 147/150（DeepSeek-flash 调用器，每回合 0.00028-0.00054 美元）。

Conclusion: Skill-RSI 可将部分操作任务编译为低成本、可复用的冻结技能，继承式修复关键；但高安全中止任务仍难通过门槛，且缺少直接控制基线，部署优势需谨慎解读。

Abstract: Embodied AI lacks the data-rich pretraining that gave language models their agents and skills, so new manipulation capability costs demonstrations per task or a frontier model in every episode. We study a third route: a strong general model acts only as an offline author that writes, runs, diagnoses and repairs a manipulation skill in simulation until it passes an exam on layouts drawn after freezing; the frozen skill then runs as code alone or under a cheap caller that reads only its description. We call this skill-level recursive self-improvement Skill-RSI and specify a compile protocol (isolated, audited author; hidden validation layouts; no-feedback and inherit-only controls; tamper-evident ledger) and a reuse protocol that runs the identical files with executors from another model family. On 7 RoboTwin 2.0 tasks with cluttered tables, Claude Code compiled 4 tasks into 7 frozen skills, 5 from a first draft needing only qualification, at USD 4.29-8.22 of author cost each (USD 55 per skill counting the 13 failed campaigns). The other three tasks did not compile in 18 campaigns, including five with GPT-6 and a pre-registered 150-layout run: on the hardest task every campaign scored 78-95 of 100 but had 3-16 safety aborts, failing the gate of 95% success with zero aborts; a 100-episode exam bounds an abort rate only to 3.6%. On hidden layouts, repair that inherits the previous version beat from-scratch rewriting in 8 of 8 controlled campaigns (sign test p = 0.004); an inherit-only lineage attributes most of the gap to inheritance, with development feedback adding a little (3 of 3, p = 0.125). Write-once campaigns found no detectable first-draft effect of practice (minimum detectable effect 14 points). The frozen skills kept 148/150 successes on new layouts with no model and 147/150 with a DeepSeek-flash caller at USD 0.00028-0.00054 per episode; no direct-control baseline was run.

</details>


### [25] [Localization and Neural Splatting on the Edge: An FPGA Scene Memory for Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25068)
*Que Haohua,Mingkai Liu,Handong Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LENS 是一个部署在 AMD KR260 FPGA/ARM 边缘平台上的场景记忆加速系统，将 ACEsplat 风格工作负载带到低功耗边缘设备，实现板载相机定位与近邻视图渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 资源受限机器人和自动驾驶需要无需桌面 GPU 的场景记忆来定位相机并渲染附近视图。SCR 对位姿敏感，3DGS 渲染不规则且对容量敏感，而 ACEsplat 式流水线以 GPU 为中心，难以迁移到边缘 FPGA。

Method: LENS 不提出新场景模型，而是将两个主导内核与板级数据通路协同设计：一是保持位姿精度的 INT16 SCR 加速器；二是位精确定点、有序的 3DGS 渲染器，将 alpha 透射率重写为 log-sum-exp 递推，并剔除可证明无用的流式高斯。所有延迟、功耗和质量结果均为板载实测。

Result: SCR 引擎在 25.42 ms 下保持 DSAC* 定位精度；板载渲染器在 Cambridge 上达到 34.11 dB，高于 ACEsplat 的 33.20 dB；RealEstate10K 新视图合成达到 27.84 dB，高于 26.11 dB；十个 Wayspots 场景均达到 ACEsplat 渲染目标。LENS 在所有部署任务上的每瓦性能均优于 Jetson Orin NX，约四分之一功耗下提升 1.9x 至 5.7x，并在定位和 Wayspots 渲染的绝对延迟上更快。设备端训练可达到 ACEsplat 级质量，但未达到 GPU 的时间和能耗水平，标明了未来训练加速器的剩余边界。

Conclusion: LENS 证明了 ACEsplat 风格场景记忆在低功耗 FPGA/ARM 边缘平台上的可行加速路径，同时指出设备端训练仍是未来训练加速器需要突破的边界。

Abstract: Resource-constrained robots and autonomous vehicles need scene memories that localize a camera and render nearby views without a desktop GPU. Scene-coordinate regression (SCR) and 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) fit because they avoid an SfM stage, but ACEsplat-style pipelines are GPU-centered, and moving them to an edge FPGA is hard because SCR is pose-sensitive and 3DGS rendering is irregular and capacity-sensitive. We present LENS, an edge scene-memory acceleration system on the AMD KR260 that brings an ACEsplat-style workload to a low-power FPGA/ARM platform. Rather than proposing a new scene model, LENS co-designs the two dominant kernels with the board data path: an INT16 SCR accelerator that preserves pose accuracy, and a bit-exact fixed-point ordered 3DGS renderer that rewrites alpha transmittance as a log-sum-exp recurrence and removes provably useless streamed Gaussians. All LENS latency, power, and quality results are board-measured. The SCR engine preserves DSAC* localization accuracy at 25.42 ms, the on-board renderer reaches 34.11 dB on Cambridge against ACEsplat's 33.20, novel-view synthesis on RealEstate10K reaches 27.84 dB against 26.11, and all ten Wayspots scenes reach the ACEsplat rendering target. LENS wins performance per watt over a Jetson Orin NX on every deployment task, by 1.9x to 5.7x at about a quarter of the power, and is faster in absolute latency on localization and Wayspots rendering. On-device training reaches ACEsplat-level quality but not the GPU's time or energy, marking the remaining boundary for a future training accelerator.

</details>


### [26] [Learning Waypoints and Passage Events for Decentralized Multi-Quadrotor Trajectory Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25024)
*Yunhan Shi,Jia Deng,Zehao Jia,Lin Cheng,Shengping Gong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出学习-优化框架，联合预测多四旋翼的空间航点与有序通过事件，并由可微序列凸规划在刚体动力学和推力约束下生成局部轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多四旋翼通道规划需同时协调空间路径与通过时间，且受动力学、执行器约束和车间交互影响，局部轨迹生成计算困难。

Method: 用网络预测空间航点和有序通过事件，以共同物理时间网格对齐各飞行器；中间事件由网络固定，终端进度局部优化以支持不同完成指标；采用可微序列凸规划融合刚体动力学与旋翼推力边界，并通过生成轨迹的碰撞和完成梯度训练两个网络。

Result: 在双四旋翼24种布局评估中，适配策略在全部5个留出布局通过物理可行性和采样分离检查；平均完成指标较继承策略降低1.52%；在匹配数值轨迹参考的留出交换布局上，前向时间中位数为21.01 ms。

Conclusion: 结果表明，学习到的空间与事件参数可协调独立的刚体规划器，并在评估场景中保持局部飞行器约束。

Abstract: Multi-quadrotor passage planning requires coordinated spatial routes and passage times under vehicle dynamics and actuation constraints. Optimizing these decisions jointly couples temporal allocation, nonlinear motion, and inter-vehicle interactions, creating a computational challenge for local trajectory generation. We propose a learning-optimization framework that predicts spatial waypoints and ordered passage events for independently solved quadrotor planners. A common physical time grid aligns the vehicles, with intermediate events fixed by the networks and terminal progress optimized locally to permit different completion metrics. Differentiable sequential convex programming incorporates rigid-body dynamics and rotor-thrust bounds, allowing collision and completion gradients to train both networks through the generated trajectories. In a two-quadrotor evaluation across 24 layouts, the adapted policy passes physical feasibility and sampled separation checks in all five held-out layouts. Their mean completion metric decreases by 1.52% relative to the inherited policy. The median forward time is 21.01 ms on the held-out exchange layout with matching numerical trajectory references. These results demonstrate that learned spatial and event parameters can coordinate independent rigid-body planners while preserving local vehicle constraints in the evaluated scenarios.

</details>


### [27] [Scalable, Intuitive, Mixed Reality-Based Semi-Autonomous Robotic Telemanipulation for the Execution of a Fine-Grained Bimanual Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25006)
*Shifei Duan,Zhe Wang,Francesco De Pace,Minas Liarokapis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于混合现实（MR）头显的可扩展、直观半自主机器人遥操作系统：机器人自主完成大部分任务轨迹，操作员仅在关键阶段进行精细调整；在高精度双手销孔装配任务上验证，以更低数据采集负担获得高质量示范并训练出全自主模仿学习策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人遥操作数据采集高度依赖人工，大规模采集数十至数百条示范耗时费力，操作员易疲劳，尤其灵巧高精度任务需持续集中注意力；半自主遥操作有望在保留人在回路控制的同时降低操作员负担。

Method: 构建基于MR头显的机器人本体数据采集系统，采用半自主执行策略：系统自主完成多数任务轨迹，仅在关键阶段要求操作员微调；在机器人上采集示范数据并训练模仿学习策略，以高精度、细粒度双手销孔装配任务进行验证。

Result: 以更低的数据采集投入获得高质量示范数据，并可据此训练完全自主的任务执行策略。

Conclusion: 半自主MR遥操作是一种可扩展且直观的高效数据采集方式，能够降低操作员负担并支持从人类示范学习全自主机器人策略。

Abstract: Nowadays, robotic telemanipulation enables humans to control robots so as to execute tasks in a manner similar to how they perform them, and serves as an essential approach for collecting on-robot data for robot learning by human demonstrations. The quality of collected demonstrations directly impacts the performance of the learned policy. However, existing telemanipulation systems rely heavily on human operation, particularly for large-scale data collection. Collecting dozens or hundreds of demonstrations is a demanding process, as operators tend to fatigue quickly, especially during the execution of dexterous tasks that require sustained concentration. Recently, semi-autonomous robotic telemanipulation has emerged as a promising direction that reduces operator workload while preserving human-in-the-loop control for task execution. We therefore introduce a scalable, intuitive, semi-autonomous robotic telemanipulation system that leverages a mixed reality (MR) headset for on-robot data collection. The system autonomously executes the majority of the task trajectories and only requires the operator to make fine adjustments at key stages. We validate the effectiveness of our system by collecting data and training imitation learning policies on the execution of a high-precision, fine-grained bimanual peg-in-hole task. With lower data collection effort, our system obtains high-quality demonstrations that can be used to train fully autonomous task execution policies.

</details>


### [28] [Dream What I Can: Sparse Visual Planning for Long-Horizon Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24981)
*Chongkai Gao,Wenlong Huang,Haonan Chen,Lin Shao,Yilun Du,Jiajun Wu,Ruohan Zhang,Li Fei-Fei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DreamCan 提出一种分层机器人操作策略，用稀疏视觉子目标将视频模型先验落地为可执行规划：高层规划器从预训练视频模型适配而来，低层为视觉运动策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频模型能提供丰富的可泛化机器人操作先验，但视觉上合理的未来并不一定可执行；需要弥合视频预测与真实可执行动作之间的差距。

Method: 高层规划器不预测稠密轨迹，而是生成兼顾可达性与任务效用的稀疏视觉子目标；利用离线、无动作机器人视频估计这些量，并通过动态规划自动选择子目标序列，无需人工标注。

Result: 所选子目标比均匀间隔子目标更可靠可达；系统优于语言、稠密视频和均匀子目标规划器，在 Language-Table 上取得高规划成功率，在 LIBERO-Long 上以 0.1B 参数低层策略达到 91.2% 成功率；额外次优视频和测试时计算可进一步提升成功率；并在真实双臂折箱和装箱任务中处理未见物体组合。

Conclusion: 稀疏视觉规划是 grounding 视频模型用于可执行机器人操作的有效方式，兼顾可达性、任务效用与自动监督，并展现出仿真与真实双臂任务上的强性能。

Abstract: Video models provide rich priors for generalizable robotic manipulation, but plausible visual futures are not always executable. We introduce DreamCan, a hierarchical policy that grounds video models through sparse visual planning: a high-level planner adapted from a pre-trained video model and a low-level visuomotor policy. Rather than predicting dense trajectories, the high-level planner generates sparse visual subgoals that balance reachability and task utility. From offline, action-free robot videos, we estimate these quantities and use dynamic programming to select subgoal sequences without manual annotations. The resulting subgoals are more reliably attained than uniformly spaced alternatives, and the full system outperforms language, dense-video, and uniform-subgoal planners, reaching high planning success rates on Language-Table tasks and 91.2% success on LIBERO-Long with a 0.1B-parameter low-level policy. Additional suboptimal videos and test-time compute further improve success. The system also performs challenging real-world bimanual tasks, including box folding and packing with unseen object combinations. More results are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [29] [Anticipatory Gait Switching for Quadrupeds via Look-Ahead MDP Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24895)
*Kelvin Aladum,Justin Peel,Nisar Ahmed*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种仅依赖高程图的轻量级步态监督器，将前瞻信息与运动约束统一到单一决策策略中，实现四足机器人约束感知的前瞻步态切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人常通过切换步态安全通过混合地形。现有监督器多用转换处理器满足硬件约束，但切换决策依赖近场阈值；当粗糙地形在局部高程图中临近接触才被检测时，会导致切换承诺延迟。

Method: 将机器人中心高程图划分为近场、中场和远场三个带，每带离散为地形类别，并将带演化建模为马尔可夫过程；把步态选择建模为前瞻马尔可夫决策过程，其转移模型和代价显式编码平台相关的切换约束。

Result: 在Unitree Go2平台的室内外实验表明，与调参的反应式基线相比，该方法减少了晚切换、降低了粗糙地形上无效步态的使用时间，并改善了横滚稳定性。

Conclusion: 该MDP建模实现了前瞻且约束感知的步态切换，无需针对特定场景重新调参即可提升鲁棒性。

Abstract: Quadruped robots often switch gaits to traverse mixed terrain safely. Many practical supervisors enforce hardware constraints (e.g., dwell or guard intervals) using a transition handler. However, the switch decision itself is often driven by near-field thresholds, which can delay commitment when rough terrain is only detected shortly before contact in the local height map. We propose a lightweight height-map-only gait supervisor that unifies look-ahead and locomotion constraints in a single decision policy. A robot-centered height map is partitioned into near-, mid-, and far-field bands, each discretized into a terrain class, and band evolution is modeled as a Markov process. Gait selection is cast as a look-ahead Markov Decision Process whose transition model and cost explicitly encode platform-specific switching constraints. Indoor and outdoor experiments on a Unitree Go2 platform show reduced late switching, lower time spent using ineffective gaits on rough terrain, and improved roll stability compared to tuned reactive baselines. Overall, the MDP formulation yields anticipative constraint-aware gait switching that improves robustness without course-specific retuning.

</details>


### [30] [DiPLO: Self-distilled Diffusion Planning for Loco-manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24847)
*Shafeef Omar,Sebastian Sanokowski,Elham Daneshmand,Dian Yu,Daniel Cremers,Cordelia Schmid,Majid Khadiv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DiPLO 将自蒸馏扩散规划器与低层全身跟踪策略结合，用于人形机器人 loco-manipulation：规划器先在人类到人形重定向动作上监督预训练，跟踪器用 RL 训练执行；再通过 SD:DPO 去除对强任务条件的依赖，并用 DA:PPO 以最大熵 RL 微调扩散规划器，使其对齐冻结跟踪器能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人 loco-manipulation 需同时协调移动、平衡与物体交互，仅靠少量示范或从零 RL 学习都很困难；同时，强任务条件对预训练有效，但下游微调难以在省略该条件时恢复同等性能，因此需要一种能利用示范预训练、再用 RL 提升任务成功并对齐执行能力的方法。

Method: 提出 DiPLO 框架：扩散规划器基于 human-to-humanoid retargeting 得到的动作进行监督预训练，低层全身跟踪器通过 RL 学习执行规划动作。后训练包含两阶段：SD:DPO 进行自蒸馏扩散策略优化，移除规划器对强任务条件的依赖；随后用 DA:PPO（面向扩散模型的最大熵 RL）微调，使生成计划更符合冻结跟踪器的执行能力，并仅依靠状态历史和目标条件生成动作。

Result: 在 pick-carry-place 任务中达到 99% 成功率，而预训练模型仅 45%；规划器还能面向未见过的物体目标生成动作，并在 kicking、pushing/pulling 任务上进一步评估。最终在 Unitree G1 硬件上部署 DA:PPO 规划器-跟踪器流程，无需真实世界微调即可实现多次成功执行。

Conclusion: DiPLO 表明，自蒸馏扩散规划与 RL 后训练相结合，可有效利用重定向动作预训练并对齐低层跟踪器，从而提升人形机器人 loco-manipulation 的任务成功率、目标泛化能力和硬件部署可行性。

Abstract: Humanoid loco-manipulation is particularly challenging because it requires coordinating locomotion, balance, and object interaction, making it difficult to learn from limited demonstrations or from scratch through Reinforcement Learning (RL). We introduce DiPLO (self-distilled Diffusion Planning for Loco-manipulation), a framework that combines a self-distilled diffusion planner with a low-level whole-body tracking policy. Our planner is pretrained via supervised learning on motions derived from human-to-humanoid retargeting, while the tracker is trained via RL to execute them. DiPLO centers around two complementary post-training stages. First, we introduce Self-Distillation Diffusion Policy Optimisation (SD:DPO) to remove the planner's dependence on strong task conditioning, which we identify as a crucial ingredient for effective pretraining, as downstream fine-tuning does not recover comparable performance when it is omitted.. We then fine-tune this distilled model with Diffusion-Augmented Proximal Policy Optimization (DA:PPO), a maximum-entropy RL method for diffusion models, to improve task success and align generated plans with the frozen tracker's execution capabilities. Together, these stages enable motion generation through state-history and goal conditioning alone, enabling 99% task success in pick-carry-place tasks, compared to just 45% for the pretrained model. The resulting planner also generates motions for unseen object goals, with further evaluations on kicking and pushing/pulling tasks. Finally, we deploy our pipeline on the hardware, using our DA:PPO planner-tracker on a Unitree G1, achieving repeated successful executions without any real-world fine-tuning.

</details>


### [31] [Escape Without a Recipe: Maneuver-Agnostic Mars Rover Recovery from Granular Entrapment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24827)
*Meraj Hossain Promit,Chandak Chakma,Md Jubair Ahmed Sourov,Sejuti Rahman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种无需预定义动作的循环策略，使轮式行星巡视器能从松散风化层陷困中自主脱困，并在MPM仿真中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轮式行星巡视器陷入松散风化层后缺乏有原则的恢复控制器：脚本化机动依赖地形，简单驱动会加深陷困。

Method: 在连续物质点法（MPM）仿真中确认z/r=2.0的陷困构型；用81,771个窗口（覆盖3种材料、6个下沉深度）训练冻结陷困检测器，向策略提供三状态信念；以独立仿真移动锁存门控训练奖励；训练循环策略学习脱困，不预设机动。

Result: 5个训练种子平均脱困率62.5%（范围28.1%–81.3%，每种子32次试验），最强脚本控制器为7/32，动作噪声为0/32；数值审计发现耦合缓冲区故障在16个并行环境下静默丢弃9.7%接触节点。

Conclusion: 结果仅在所测试的仿真工况中确立脱困恢复能力，物理迁移尚未验证。

Abstract: Wheeled planetary rovers immobilised in loose regolith have no principled recovery controller: scripted maneuvers are terrain-specific, and naive driving deepens entrapment. We introduce a recurrent policy that learns to escape without a predefined maneuver. The framework rests on a verified trap: in a continuum material point method (MPM) simulator we identify a configuration at sinkage-to-radius ratio z/r = 2.0 where the strongest of ten scripted controllers succeeds in only 7/32 trials, yet the regolith retains traction and continues to shear, confirming granular entrapment rather than a numerical artifact. A frozen entrapment detector, trained on 81,771 windows spanning three materials and six sinkage levels, supplies a three-state belief to the policy observation. A separate simulator mobility latch gates training rewards. Across five training seeds, mean action evaluation achieves 62.5% escape (range 28.1-81.3%; 32 trials per seed) versus 7/32 for the strongest scripted controller and 0/32 for action noise. A numerical audit identifies a coupling-buffer fault that silently discarded 9.7% of contact nodes at 16 parallel environments. Results establish recovery in the tested simulation regime; physical transfer remains unverified.

</details>


### [32] [SARAM: Sparse Adapter Allocation with Reuse and Merging for Lifelong Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24804)
*SeongJun Jeong,Chungwoo Lee,Gi-Cheon Kang,Byoung-Tak Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SARAM 是一种面向预训练 VLA 骨干的终身机器人学习框架：复用冻结的旧任务适配器，仅在稀疏选层新增适配器，并通过任务签名与权重合并，在保持性能的同时限制存储增长，使 GPU 内存和每步推理成本不随任务流长度增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 终身机器人学习需要顺序学习新任务且不遗忘旧任务。基于大型预训练 VLA 骨干的适配器方法虽能缓解遗忘，但任务专用适配器与选择模块会随任务流累积，在固定板载资源预算下带来磁盘存储和部署成本的可扩展性问题。

Method: SARAM 对每个新任务复用先前任务的冻结适配器，仅在稀疏选择的层引入新适配器，并使用重复惩罚鼓励新适配器捕获适配器库尚未表示的信息。部署时，用骨干激活统计得到的紧凑签名在每个 episode 识别一次任务，并将对应任务更新合并进骨干，使控制回路中仅保留骨干。

Result: 在四个 LIBERO 套件上，SARAM 匹配或超过基于适配器的基线，同时每个任务增加的磁盘状态最多少 4.5 倍；随着任务流增长，GPU 内存占用和控制频率与骨干相当。在采用不同骨干的真实 UR5 机械臂实验中，也展示了顺序任务流中的保持能力。

Conclusion: SARAM 通过复用与稀疏新增适配器、任务签名识别和权重合并，在有限板载资源下实现可扩展的终身机器人学习，兼顾旧任务保持、部署效率与存储增长控制。

Abstract: Lifelong robot learning requires acquiring new tasks sequentially without forgetting earlier ones. With large pre-trained vision-language-action (VLA) backbones, adapter-based methods effectively mitigate this challenge. However, their task-specific adapters and selection modules accumulate with the task stream, creating a scalability challenge in on-disk storage and deployment cost under fixed onboard resource budgets. We propose SARAM, a lifelong learning framework that limits storage growth while keeping GPU memory and per-step inference cost independent of the task stream length. For each new task, SARAM reuses frozen adapters from previous tasks and introduces new adapters only at sparsely selected layers, with a duplication penalty encouraging them to capture information not already represented by the adapter library. At deployment, a compact signature derived from backbone activation statistics identifies the task once per episode, and the corresponding task update is merged into the backbone, leaving only the backbone in the control loop. Across four LIBERO suites, SARAM matches or exceeds adapter-based baselines while adding up to 4.5x less on-disk state per task. GPU memory usage and control frequency remain comparable to those of the backbone as the task stream grows. Experiments on a real UR5 arm with a different backbone further demonstrate retention throughout a sequential task stream.

</details>


### [33] [When Better Policies Leave Harder Failures: Coverage Shift in VLM Success Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24801)
*Isabel Guan,Dingyuan Zhang,Shipeng Lyu,I-Ming Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究固定VLM成功判别器下机器人策略改进是否反而更难监控，提出“覆盖偏移”（coverage shift）：学习改变到达固定判别器的失败类型，使绝对漏检下降但条件漏检上升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人策略性能提升可能掩盖失败检测能力退化，需区分任务改进与失败可检测性，避免仅凭成功率判断监控可靠性。

Method: 在20个随机种子的托盘放置实验中，仅用模拟器验证成功进行训练，并由固定VLM判断成功；比较训练前后成功率、hover失败接受率，并用边界探针测试托盘边缘上下hover；进一步评估9个判别器和1,392个刺激，并在8种设置下做判别器过滤实验。

Result: 成功率从66.8%升至97.0%，被接受的hover失败从5.0%降至0.9%（按执行计）；但剩余hover中判别器接受率为50.0%，高于未训练采样的31.1%。剩余失败更多位于托盘边缘下方，可掩盖抓取立方体下方间隙；边界探针接受148/160个边缘下方hover、0/160个边缘上方hover。九判别器与八设置实验显示状态依赖接受效应具有异质性。

Conclusion: 策略改进不等同于失败更可检测；学习会导致条件监控敏感性发生覆盖偏移，应同时报告绝对漏检、条件漏检与审计覆盖率。

Abstract: Can a better robot policy become harder to monitor? We study a fixed vision-language model (VLM) that judges success while a policy improves. In a controlled 20-seed tray-placement experiment, training selects only simulator-verified successes. Success rises from 66.8% to 97.0%, and accepted hover failures fall from 5.0% to 0.9% of executions. Yet the judge accepts 50.0% of remaining hovers, versus 31.1% under untrained sampling. These residual failures more often lie below the tray rim, which can conceal the gap beneath a grasped cube. A separate boundary probe accepts 148/160 below-rim hovers and 0/160 above-rim hovers. We call this change in conditional monitoring sensitivity coverage shift: learning changes which failures reach a fixed judge. A broader diagnosis covers nine judges and 1,392 stimuli. Judge-filtered experiments across eight settings further show heterogeneous effects of state-dependent acceptance. The results distinguish task improvement from failure detectability and motivate reporting absolute misses, conditional misses, and audit coverage together.

</details>


### [34] [Physical Words: A Vocabulary for Structuring Latent Reasoning in Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26854)
*Jiaming Liu,Xinghao Zhu,Yiming Zhang,Shanghang Zhang,Yinxi Wang,Yichu Yang,Zhuoyang Liu,Dantong Niu,Soha Pouya,Juekun Li,Yan Chang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 PhysWord，一种以原子动作动词为“Physical Words”共享语义锚点的“先推理后行动”机器人基础模型，将语言语义与未来观测驱动的潜空间动态对齐，并通过自回归预测未来物理词序列为动作生成提供结构化多模态上下文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言推理能表达任务语义但难以刻画细粒度物理动态；潜空间推理能建模未来状态演化但缺乏可复用、可组织的推理原语。二者需要桥接，以形成既有语义结构又能表达物理动态的推理框架。

Method: PhysWord 预测即将发生的原子动作词，并将其上下文隐状态与对应未来观测导出的潜目标直接对齐；在此基础上构建时间结构化的 PhysWord 空间，为每个动作词提供视觉及可选触觉变体；通过自回归预测未来时间步的 Physical Words 捕捉执行阶段转换，为动作生成提供结构化多模态上下文。

Result: 在 UniVTAC 视触觉任务上达到 90.3% 成功率，在 LIBERO-Long 视觉任务上达到 99.6%；在真实世界双臂夹爪和灵巧手任务上优于对比方法；进一步评估表明该结构化潜推理框架可泛化到未见场景并支持长程操作。

Conclusion: PhysWord 通过原子动作词语义锚定连续物理动态，将语言推理的结构性与潜空间推理的物理表达能力结合，形成可复用、时间结构化且多模态的推理原语，有效提升机器人操作性能、泛化性与长程任务能力。

Abstract: Multimodal reasoning provides robotic foundation models with predictive context needed for reliable action generation. However, language-based reasoning captures task semantics but struggles to express fine-grained physical dynamics, while latent reasoning models future state evolution but offers limited structure for organizing these dynamics into reusable reasoning primitives. To bridge this gap, we introduce PhysWord, a reasoning-before-acting foundation model built on Physical Words, which use atomic action verbs as shared semantic anchors for continuous representations of physical dynamics. Specifically, PhysWord predicts upcoming atomic action words and directly aligns their contextual hidden states with latent targets derived from the corresponding future observations. Building on these primitives, we construct a temporally structured PhysWord space with visual and optional tactile variants of each action word. Autoregressively predicting Physical Words across future timesteps captures transitions between execution stages, providing structured multimodal context for action generation. PhysWord achieves state-of-the-art success rates of 90.3\% on visuotactile UniVTAC and 99.6\% on vision-based LIBERO-Long, and outperforms competing methods on real-world tasks using bimanual grippers and dexterous hands. Further evaluations demonstrate that our structured latent reasoning framework generalizes to unseen scenarios and enables long-horizon manipulation.

</details>


### [35] [CoopUQ: Robust Multi-Robot Social Navigation via Uncertainty-Aware Cooperative Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26852)
*Zehao Wang,Jianpeng Yao,Yuping Wang,Yiping Li,Jiaqi Li,Zhixu Li,Kanghoon Lee,Jinfeng Wang,Cong Liu,Zhaowei Tan,Jinkyoo Park,Jiachen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CoopUQ多机器人社会导航框架，将不确定性感知的跨机器人合作占用预测与约束多智能体强化学习决策相结合，以提升遮挡和分布外场景下的安全性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将单机器人强化学习导航直接扩展到多机器人场景难以保证安全与效率；在遮挡和OOD场景中，单个机器人易忽视潜在风险或预测不准，如何有效利用合作同时服务于预测与导航仍是挑战。

Method: CoopUQ通过跨机器人时空特征融合预测未来占用栅格及其不确定性，并利用预测结果和不确定性通过约束多智能体强化学习引导策略；成本函数惩罚进入预测占用或高不确定区域，使策略在分布偏移下自适应调整风险敏感度。

Result: 在多个Unity3D仿真环境及ID/OOD设置（不同布局、行人密度和急促行为）中，CoopUQ在预测性能与安全性上优于代表性基线；真实机器人实验也显示其在多种场景下具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CoopUQ有效桥接了不确定性感知的合作占用预测与鲁棒决策，提升了多机器人社会导航的安全性和OOD鲁棒性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning enables mobile robots to make fast and scalable navigation decisions in constrained human-shared environments. However, directly extending single-robot approaches to multi-robot settings often fails to ensure safety and efficiency, especially under occlusions and out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios, where individual robots may overlook potential risks or make inaccurate predictions. Leveraging cooperation effectively for both prediction and navigation remains a major challenge. We propose CoopUQ, a multi-robot social navigation framework that bridges uncertainty-aware cooperative occupancy forecasting and robust decision making. CoopUQ predicts future occupancy grid maps through cross-robot spatiotemporal feature fusion and further leverages forecasts and their uncertainty to guide policy via constrained multi-agent reinforcement learning, where the cost penalizes navigation through regions predicted to be occupied or highly uncertain. This design enables the policy to adapt its risk sensitivity under distribution shifts. Experimental results across multiple Unity3D-based simulation environments with both in-distribution and OOD settings, including diverse layouts, pedestrian densities, and rushing behavior, demonstrate improved prediction performance and safety over representative baselines. Real-robot experiments further demonstrate its robustness across various scenes. Our code and videos are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [36] [Where is Physical RSI capability from? An Observational Scaling Law of LLM-as-policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26848)
*Zekai Li,Jiayi Yu,Jinlu Zhang,Luyang Niu,Zekun Qi,Jun Zheng,Xuchuan Chen,Dairu Liu,Yintianrun Zhang,He Wang,Li Yi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 系统研究物理递归自我改进（RSI）的机制、缩放与泛化，提出PhysRSI-eval五轴指标和Physical Barrel Hypothesis，并通过VLM操作任务与干预实验分析改进速度、幅度及遗留效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RSI评估多依赖数字环境或公开基准平均分，难以刻画物理智能体在固定交互预算下如何改进、受何能力瓶颈约束，以及经验能否复用。

Method: 提出PhysRSI-eval，用首次尝试性能、效率、成功率、预算和失败后改进五轴量化物理RSI；在18个操作任务和固定交互预算下评测不同VLM；从公开能力预测相对表现、从早期物理反馈预测后续进展两方面研究观测缩放；并开展工作区干预，包括移除历史、冻结工作区和跨任务迁移。

Result: 五轴指标能区分公开基准平均分接近的系统，且高分未必伴随早期或高效增益；移除历史降低后期成功率，冻结工作区在未见布局上仍有用，三个跨任务案例实现更早成功。

Conclusion: 物理RSI可能由不同能力分别约束不同改进环节；场景特定适应与可复用经验可以共存，评估应关注改进轨迹而非仅看平均分。

Abstract: In this paper, we systematically investigate the mechanisms, scaling behavior, and generalization of physical Recursive self-improvement.
