We introduce PhysRSI-eval to quantify physical RSI through a five-axis metric: First-Attempt Performance, Efficiency, Success, Budget and Improvement from failure. Different VLMs are evaluated on 18 manipulation tasks under a fixed interaction budget. The analysis of experimental results motivates the Physical Barrel Hypothesis: different capabilities may constrain different parts of improvement. The five-axis metrics also distinguish systems with close public benchmark averages and show that high scores need not come with early or efficient gains. We study observational scaling from two perspectives: predicting the relative performance of VLM agents from their public capabilities, and forecasting later progress from early physical feedback. Together, these analyses ask how quickly and how far an agent can improve. Moreover, we conduct workspace interventions and then analyze what improvement leaves behind. Removing history reduces late-round success, frozen workspaces remain useful on unseen layouts, and three cross-task cases achieve success earlier. The results suggest that scene-specific adaptation and reusable experience can coexist.

</details>


### [37] [GPT-6 Astra Is a Native Visuo-Tactile Policy for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26846)
*Chen Yang,Linzhe Shi,Hang Zhang,Mu Xu,Jiansheng Fan,Chen Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文首次展示多模态模型 GPT-6 Astra 可直接解释基于视觉的触觉图像，并通过其现有视觉接口利用接触线索完成机器人操作；在 UniVTAC 与 OpenViTac 的 19 个仿真任务上进行少样本闭环控制，性能超过最强专科策略基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富控制与触觉感知通常被视为专科策略的优势领域，因此需要检验通用多模态基础模型能否直接理解并使用触觉观测，并挑战专科策略在接触丰富操作中的排他性。

Method: 将触觉 RGB 图像作为视觉输入送入 Astra 现有视觉接口，在 UniVTAC 和 OpenViTac 的 19 个仿真任务、每设置 210 个场景种子上进行少样本闭环控制；比较视觉-触觉与纯视觉输入，并分析操作轨迹与行为模式。

Result: 视觉-触觉输入下平均成功率分别为 93.8% 和 81.8%，比对比中最强已报告专科基线高 22.9 和 13.1 个百分点；纯视觉也达 76.3% 和 80.6%。轨迹分析显示主动探测、谨慎抓取、接触引导对齐与恢复，但在切向加载和纠正性执行上仍有困难，并出现基准饱和迹象。

Conclusion: 研究表明基础模型可直接利用基于视觉的触觉传感作为接触理解接口，细粒度视觉可部分补偿缺失触觉；结果支持联合视觉-触觉预训练、更难的接触丰富评测以及真实机器人验证。

Abstract: Recent advances in multimodal models have enabled GPT-6 Astra to perform robotic manipulation through in-context learning. Yet contact-rich control and tactile perception are often regarded as strengths of specialist policies, leaving open whether Astra can directly understand and use tactile observations. We provide, to our knowledge, the first demonstration that Astra directly interprets vision-based tactile images and uses their contact cues for manipulation. Tactile RGB images enter through its existing visual interface, enabling few-shot closed-loop control on nineteen simulated UniVTAC and OpenViTac tasks with 210 sampled scene seeds per setting. With visuo-tactile input, Astra achieves mean success rates of 93.8% and 81.8%, exceeding the strongest reported specialist baselines in our comparison by 22.9 and 13.1 percentage points, respectively. Vision-only performance also leads these baselines, reaching 76.3% and 80.6%. Trajectory analysis reveals active probing, cautious grasping, contact-guided alignment, and recovery, alongside difficulties with tangential loading and corrective execution. Our findings challenge the exclusivity of specialist policies in contact-rich manipulation and show that fine-grained vision can partially compensate for missing touch. They also identify vision-based tactile sensing as an interface for scaling contact understanding with foundation models and reveal signs of benchmark saturation. These results motivate joint visual-tactile pretraining, harder contact-rich evaluations, and physical-robot validation.

</details>


### [38] [Bio-inspired Thrust-Assisted Trotting on a legged-Aerial Robot via Real-Time QP-MPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26810)
*Shreyansh Pitroda,Eric Sihite,Alireza Ramezani,Morteza Gharib*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文在Harpy推力辅助双足机器人上实现了基于二次规划（QP）的控制器，用于推力辅助trotting，并验证了实时性与跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受石鸡等鸟类翼辅助运动启发，利用推力矢量调节地面反作用力，以提升双足机器人运动控制能力。

Method: 将推力辅助弹簧负载倒立摆动力学建模为紧凑的QP模型预测控制问题，以500 Hz同时调节地面接触力与主动推力矢量；采用解耦架构：矢状面用QP-MPC，额状面用级联姿态稳定控制器；开展0 m/s和0.1 m/s前向参考速度实验。

Result: 平均速度可跟踪各自参考值，瞬时速度存在振荡；关节跟踪误差保持在1 deg以内；QP-MPC每次求解耗时0.18 ms，远低于2 ms计算预算，并满足GRF摩擦锥约束。

Conclusion: 结果表明该QP-MPC控制框架可在Harpy硬件上实时实现，并有效完成推力辅助trotting控制。

Abstract: This paper presents the hardware implementation of a quadratic program-based controller on Harpy: thruster-assisted bipedal robot to perform thruster-assisted trotting. This is inspired by wing-assisted locomotion observed in birds such as the chukar partridge, which employ thrust vectoring to manipulate the ground reaction force. The proposed controller formulates the thruster-assisted spring-loaded inverted pendulum dynamics as a condensed QP-based model predictive control problem, enabling simultaneous regulation of ground contact force and active thrust vectoring at 500 Hz. A decoupled control architecture is used, where the robot's sagittal dynamics are controlled using QP-MPC while the frontal dynamics is regulated using a cascade orientation stabilization controller. Two experiments were conducted with different forward reference velocities of 0 m/s and 0.1 m/s. While the instantaneous velocity oscillates, the mean velocities can track their respective reference. Additionally, joint tracking error remains within 1 deg while tracking the desired leg length given by the leg trajectory controller and desired swing trajectory given by Raibert's heuristic. The QP-MPC problem is consistently solved with 0.18ms, which is well within the 2ms computation budget while satisfying the GRF friction cone constraint.

</details>


### [39] [HandsOn: A Large-Scale Simulation Benchmark for Generalizable Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26765)
*Tianwei Luo,Yiming He,Hongjin Wu,Ting Pan,Debin Ren,Haorui Guo,Junlin Yang,Yuxin Zuo,Zhehao Kou,Kaisen Yang,Zhuoqun Lai,Yi Ding,Jiale Yu,Yuchen Zhang,Zijie Qiu,Haozhan Li,Ning Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HandsOn 是一个面向跨具身灵巧操作的可组合仿真平台与基准，包含多机械臂、多灵巧手、约200种资产和66个任务，用于评测操作泛化与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用操作需要在不同具身、物体和任务需求下保持可靠行为，因此需要一个共享的跨具身灵巧操作仿真平台与基准来系统评估泛化能力和物理交互极限。

Method: 构建 HandsOn：支持5种机械臂、6种灵巧手和约200个刚性、铰接及可变形资产，场景随机化；提供18,000条精选人类示范和66个人工验证任务。Core-50 支持基于示范的学习与执行测试；Transfer-16 评估资产与关系迁移、技能组合和无可训练示范的可变形操作。还进行真机定性 rollout，并评测扩散模型、视觉-语言-动作模型、世界-动作模型和提示式 VLM 方法。

Result: 最佳训练多任务策略在 Core-50 上成功率为29.1%；未在基准上微调的 GPT-6 使用 move_eef 进行手掌与手指控制，在660次试验中 Core-50 为5.2%、Transfer-16 为1.9%。诊断显示失败主要涉及接触建立、动作协调、已达成目标维持，以及组合任务中的序列错误。

Conclusion: HandsOn 为研究操作泛化和可靠物理交互的边界提供了共享基础，并揭示了当前方法在跨具身灵巧操作中的显著局限。

Abstract: General-purpose manipulation requires reliable behavior across embodiments, objects, and task demands. We introduce HandsOn, a composable simulation platform and benchmark for cross-embodiment dexterous manipulation. It supports five robotic arms, six dexterous hands, and approximately 200 rigid, articulated, and deformable assets in randomized scenes. The benchmark pairs 18,000 curated human demonstrations with 66 human-verified tasks: Core-50 supports demonstration-based learning and execution tests, while Transfer-16 evaluates asset and relation transfer, skill composition, and deformable manipulation without target-task training demonstrations. Qualitative real-robot rollouts illustrate sim-to-real transfer. Across diffusion, vision-language-action, world-action, and prompted VLM-based methods, the best trained multi-task policy achieves 29.1% Core-50 success. Without benchmark finetuning, GPT-6 using move_eef for palm-and-finger control achieves 5.2% on Core-50 and 1.9% on Transfer-16 over 660 trials. Trained-policy stage diagnostics expose failures in contact establishment, action coordination, and maintaining achieved goals, with additional sequencing errors in composed tasks. HandsOn provides a shared basis for investigating manipulation generalization and the limits of reliable physical interaction.

</details>


### [40] [DexOAK: Object-Aware Kinematic Retargeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26762)
*Federica Spinola,Robert Katzschmann,Cordelia Schmid*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexOAK 是一个基于学习的物体感知运动学重定向框架，用于从人体动作捕捉数据生成接触一致的机器人手部参考，并在双臂刚体/铰接物体任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接从人体手部姿态重定向到机器人手容易丢失物体相对交互几何与接触一致性，且逐帧优化成本高；目标是生成稳定、可用于物理细化与硬件回放的机器人手参考，并支持跨数据集泛化。

Method: DexOAK 首先在联合手-物体图上最小化交互图拉普拉斯目标，使机器人复现演示中的物体相对交互几何而非仅人体手姿；逐帧解作为种子参考，训练以人体关键点和物体点云为条件的前馈网络学习重定向函数；随后用接触、非穿透和自碰撞损失进行物体感知微调，使手指落在演示接触上并减少破坏物理细化的伪影；训练后仅推理即可迁移到未见数据，无需逐帧优化。

Result: 在双臂刚体与铰接物体任务上提升动态重定向：Inspire 手上 OakInk-v2 成功率从 39.5% 升至 48.4%，ARCTIC 上 ADD-AUC 从 62.7% 升至 77.9%；消融表明增益来自物体感知运动学重定向与接触损失微调的摊销网络；OakInk-v2 训练可零样本迁移到 ARCTIC，微调后接近域内性能；硬件上五个任务中 46/56 次开环回放成功。

Conclusion: DexOAK 通过物体感知的运动学重定向与接触约束学习，实现高效、接触一致的机器人手参考生成，并支持从人体数据生成灵巧机器人数据及跨数据集迁移。

Abstract: We introduce DexOAK, a learning-based object-aware kinematic retargeting framework for generating contact-consistent robot hand references from human motion-capture data. DexOAK first minimizes an interaction-graph Laplacian objective over a joint hand-object graph, so the robot reproduces the demonstrated object-relative interaction geometry rather than the human hand pose alone. These per-frame solutions form seed retargeted references, from which a feed-forward network conditioned on human keypoints and object point clouds learns the retargeting function. Object-aware finetuning then refines the network under contact, non-penetration, and self-collision losses that position the fingers on the demonstrated contacts and reduce artifacts destabilizing physics-based refinement. Once trained, the network transfers to unseen data by inference alone, without per-frame optimization. DexOAK references improve dynamic retargeting over the state-of-the-art across bimanual tasks with rigid and articulated objects. On the Inspire hand, success rises from 39.5% to 48.4% on OakInk-v2, and ADD-AUC rises from 62.7% to 77.9% on ARCTIC. Ablations trace the gains to object-aware kinematic retargeting and the amortized network refined with contact losses. Trained on OakInk-v2, it transfers to ARCTIC zero-shot and nears in-domain performance after fine-tuning. On hardware, 46/56 open-loop replays succeed across five tasks, enabling dexterous robot data generation from human data. Visualizations and interactive results are at: this https URL .

</details>


### [41] [Tactile In-Hand Object Rotation: A Comparative Study of Hand Morphology in Simulation and Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26759)
*Wenhao He,Max Yang,Chenghua Lu,Yijiong Lin,David A.W. Barton,Nathan F. Lepora*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究比较两种低成本LEAP Hand手指布局（拟人式与对称式）在集成TacTip视觉触觉传感器和共享强化学习框架下的连续手内旋转表现，分析触觉反馈、手指参与和指尖载荷的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续手内旋转需要协调维持与更新接触，操作性能可能受手部几何结构和触觉反馈共同影响；作者希望厘清手指排列与触觉信息如何影响旋转进度、手指参与和指尖载荷。

Method: 构建两种LEAP Hand版本（拟人式与对称式手指排列），均集成TacTip视觉触觉传感器并使用共享强化学习框架。仿真在12个未见物体上评估全触觉、二值触觉和仅本体感觉观测；硬件在10个物体及多种手部姿态下评估全触觉策略，并进行接触与力测量。

Result: 仿真中，全触觉相比仅本体感觉使每回合平均归一化旋转分别提升94.8%（拟人手）和66.9%（对称手），同时回合更长、平均总指尖载荷更低。硬件中，拟人手手指参与更分化，对称手累积载荷更均匀；对称手平均旋转次数高32.0%，总旋转率高25.8%，操作时长更长，平均总指尖载荷低19.9%。

Conclusion: 手指排列与接触组织和载荷分担相关；更丰富的触觉信息在两种仿真 embodiment 下均支持更大的任务进展并降低平均载荷；对称布局在旋转次数、旋转率和载荷分布方面表现更优。

Abstract: Continuous in-hand rotation requires coordinated contact maintenance and renewal, linking manipulation performance to both hand geometry and tactile feedback. We investigate these relationships through rotational progress, finger participation, and fingertip loading on two versions of the low-cost LEAP Hand with anthropomorphic and symmetric finger arrangements. Both hands are integrated with TacTip vision-based tactile sensors and use a shared reinforcement learning framework. Simulation evaluates full tactile, binary touch, and proprioception-only observations on 12 unseen objects; hardware experiments evaluate full tactile policies on ten objects under multiple hand orientations. In simulation, full tactile observations increase mean normalized rotation per episode over proprioception alone by 94.8% for the anthropomorphic hand and 66.9% for the symmetric hand, with longer mean episode durations and lower mean total fingertip loads. Hardware contact measurements reveal more differentiated finger participation in the anthropomorphic hand, while cumulative loading is more evenly distributed across the symmetric hand's fingers. Overall, the symmetric hand achieves a 32.0% higher mean rotation count and a 25.8% higher aggregate rotation rate, together with longer manipulation duration. Additional hardware force recordings show a 19.9% lower mean total fingertip load for this layout. These findings relate finger arrangement to contact organization and load sharing, and show that richer tactile information supports greater task progress at lower mean loading in both simulated embodiments.

</details>


### [42] [Robot Learning with Vision-Language-Action Models for Agricultural Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26717)
*Hamid Hafezi,Javad Mohammadpour Velni,Mohammadreza Davoodi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作提出面向农业采摘到达与抓取的仿真到真实机器人学习框架，结合任务特定示教采集与预训练通用策略适配，并比较 Octo 与 π0.5 在分布偏移和真机实验中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业机器人操作受植物几何变化、遮挡和相机视角变化影响，预训练视觉-语言-动作（VLA）及通用机器人策略有潜力，但适配农业任务需要昂贵且耗时的领域示教数据。

Method: 构建基于 MuJoCo 的农业环境，包含多样化番茄植株结构，用于示教采集与受控评估；通过摇杆遥操作采集专家示教，并用底层控制器执行动作；将 Octo 和 π0.5 两个预训练通用策略用仿真示教适配，再在物理 Franka Research 3 机械臂上用真机示教微调；在仿真中评估分布内、视角/空间偏移和分布外条件，并进行真机实验，同时研究本体感知信息对策略性能的影响。

Result: π0.5 的任务性能得分高于 Octo，且在分布偏移下性能退化更小；研究还考察了本体感知信息对策略性能的影响。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将预训练通用策略与任务特定示教学习相结合，在农业机器人操作中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation in agricultural environments remains challenging due to variations in plant geometry, occlusion, and camera viewpoints. Pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) and generalist robot policies offer a promising approach to addressing these challenges by learning manipulation behaviors from multimodal observations and language instructions. However, adapting these policies to agricultural tasks requires domain-specific demonstrations, which are costly and time-consuming to collect on physical robots. In this work, we develop a simulation-to-real robot-learning framework for agricultural reaching and grasping that integrates task-specific demonstration collection and pretrained policy adaptation. A MuJoCo-based agricultural environment is developed with diverse tomato plant structures to support demonstration collection and controlled evaluation. Expert demonstrations are collected through joystick teleoperation, with a low-level controller used to execute robot actions. Two pretrained generalist robot policies, Octo and $\pi_{0.5}$, are adapted using simulated demonstrations, followed by further fine-tuning on a physical Franka Research 3 manipulator using real-robot demonstrations. The adapted policies are evaluated under in-distribution, viewpoint/spatial-shift, and out-of-distribution conditions in simulation, followed by physical-robot experiments. The influence of proprioceptive information on policy performance is also investigated. Results show that $\pi_{0.5}$ achieves higher task-performance scores than Octo and exhibits less degradation under distribution shifts. These findings demonstrate the potential of integrating pretrained generalist policies with task-specific demonstration learning for agricultural manipulation.

</details>


### [43] [Robotic Physical Comparison of Biologically Motivated and Counterfactual Sensory Arrangements using Biomimetic Skin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26680)
*Akihiro Miki,Shun Hasegawa,Yoshimoto Ribayashi,Yuta Sahara,Kento Kawaharazuka,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究开发分层仿生机器人皮肤，通过嵌入受体模拟传感器比较生物启发与反事实的触觉感受器空间排布，发现生物启发排布在三项被动触觉任务中表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人体皮肤机械感受器具有特定空间组织，但其功能意义难以在活体受控条件下实验检验，尤其难以物理比较生物学中不存在的替代排布。

Method: 构建模拟人皮肤组织关键结构的分层仿生机器人皮肤，将受体模拟传感器嵌入柔性软介质，实现生物启发和反事实的感觉排布，并定量评估弱接触检测、类两点辨别和纹理识别三项被动触觉任务。

Result: 反映生物启发感觉组织关键特征的排布在所考察的替代配置中表现出更高性能。

Conclusion: 该仿生机器人方法可在难以活体研究的条件下物理和实证比较生物启发与反事实感觉排布，从而探讨空间组织如何影响触觉功能。

Abstract: Human skin contains diverse mechanoreceptors, including Merkel cells and Pacinian corpuscles, each localized in specific regions. Although their spatial organization is known to be shaped by molecular signaling and neural induction during development, experimentally examining its functional significance under controlled conditions in living systems remains challenging. In particular, physically comparing alternative receptor arrangements that do not occur biologically is difficult. In this study, we developed a layered biomimetic robotic skin designed to mimic key structural features of human skin tissue by embedding receptor-analog sensors within compliant soft media. Using this platform, we implemented both biologically motivated and counterfactual sensory arrangements and quantitatively evaluated their performance across three passive tactile tasks: detection of weak contacts, a two-point discrimination-like assessment, and texture identification. Arrangements reflecting key features of the biologically motivated sensory organization exhibited higher performance than the alternative configurations examined. This study addresses a biological question concerning how spatial organization influences tactile function by presenting a biomimetic robotic approach that enables physical and empirical comparison of biologically motivated and counterfactual sensory arrangements that are difficult to investigate in living systems.

</details>


### [44] [RoboValue: A Fine-Grained Sim-and-Real Benchmark for Unified Evaluation of Robotic Value Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26663)
*Shengbang Liu,Zhengye Du,Zhilong Wan,Honghao Su,Chenxiang Xia,Chang Ge,Jinyang Xiao,Nan Wang,Zhipeng Hu,Huining Yuan,Shu'ang Yu,Yue Chen,Tianxing Chen,Chuankang Li,Maoqing Yao,Wenbo Ding,Yu Wang,Guanghui Ren,Chao Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RoboValue细粒度仿真-真实基准，统一评估机器人价值模型的四维能力，并揭示其在细粒度指令接地、历史依赖进度跟踪和恢复评估上的可靠性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人价值模型评估仅覆盖执行理解的若干方面，无法充分检验价值反馈的可靠性，尤其是任务要求与执行变化下的细粒度判断。

Method: RoboValue通过共享接口评估任务状态理解、时间进度监控、失败与恢复推理、价值一致性四个互补维度，并用诊断轨迹和指令变体测试价值判断是否准确反映任务要求及执行变化。RoboValue-Dataset覆盖15个仿真和20个真实世界操作任务，提供专家示范用于训练、标注轨迹用于细粒度评估；在零样本和单样本设置下，以及机器人具身和环境偏移条件下评测代表性机器人价值模型。

Result: 结果表明，即使结果分数和前进进度分数较强的模型，仍在细粒度指令接地、历史依赖的进度跟踪和恢复评估上存在错误；同一语义子任务在不同有效执行顺序下可获得显著不同的价值增益。

Conclusion: 这些发现为价值监督指明具体改进目标，数据集和公开榜单可支持对机器人价值反馈可靠性的持续评估。

Abstract: Robotic value models assess task execution from visual observations and instructions, providing feedback for policy learning and execution monitoring. However, existing evaluations cover only selected aspects of execution understanding, leaving the reliability of value feedback insufficiently tested. We introduce RoboValue, a fine-grained sim-and-real benchmark for unified evaluation of robotic value models. Its framework evaluates four complementary capability dimensions through shared interfaces: task-state understanding, temporal progress monitoring, failure and recovery reasoning, and value consistency. Diagnostic trajectories and instruction variations test whether value judgments accurately reflect task requirements and changes throughout execution. RoboValue-Dataset covers 15 simulation and 20 real-world manipulation tasks, providing expert demonstrations for training and annotated trajectories for fine-grained evaluation. We evaluate representative robotic value models in zero-shot and one-shot settings under standard conditions and separate shifts in robot embodiment and environment. Our results reveal errors in fine-grained instruction grounding, history-dependent progress tracking, and recovery assessment, even in models with strong outcome and forward-progress scores. The same semantic subtask can also receive substantially different value gains across valid execution orders. These findings identify concrete targets for value supervision, while the dataset and public leaderboard support continued assessment of feedback reliability. The website is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [45] [EgoRayFormer: Ray-Space Stereo Refinement for Egocentric 3D Human Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26641)
*Haozhe Wang,Songyuan Wu,Jihai Xu,Jie Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoRayFormer 是一种在射线空间中耦合立体特征采样与几何精化的因果姿态估计器，用于头戴式立体鱼眼相机下的自中心 3D 人体姿态估计，并在 UnrealEgo2/UnrealEgo-RW 上取得更低 MPJPE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 头戴式立体鱼眼相机可紧凑观测佩戴者身体，但强畸变、局部可见性和短基线使 3D 姿态估计困难；现有精化解码器在各视图中独立采样图像特征，不要求采样对应同一 3D 假设，缺乏显式立体约束。

Method: EgoRayFormer 在射线空间中联合立体特征采样与几何精化。对每个关节，解码器将共享 3D 假设沿其视射线及射线周围投影到两幅图像；几何分支校正投影关节位置并相交对应射线；学习门控将几何估计与学习到的姿态更新融合，从而引入显式立体约束，同时在射线深度约束较弱时保留灵活性。

Result: 在官方 device-relative 测试划分上，三随机种子平均 MPJPE 为 UnrealEgo2 17.64 mm、UnrealEgo-RW 49.55 mm；使用 UnrealEgo2 预训练后 RW 降至 47.57 mm。配对验证消融中，移除几何分支在两个输入分辨率、三个种子下均使 MPJPE 增加。相同 ResNet-18 编码器和输入分辨率下，相较 device-relative EgoPoseFormer 基线，RW 测试 MPJPE 降低 9.09 mm。另有协议分析量化 device-relative 与 oracle-root 评估差异。

Conclusion: EgoRayFormer 通过射线空间中的立体约束特征采样与几何精化有效提升自中心立体鱼眼 3D 姿态估计精度；几何分支对性能至关重要，且评估协议差异需被明确考虑。

Abstract: Head-mounted stereo fisheye cameras provide a compact view of the wearer's body, but strong distortion, partial visibility and a short baseline make 3D pose estimation difficult. Existing refinement decoders sample image features independently in each view, without requiring the samples to correspond to a common 3D hypothesis. We introduce EgoRayFormer, a causal pose estimator that couples stereo feature sampling and geometric refinement in ray space. For each joint, the decoder projects shared 3D hypotheses along and around its viewing ray into both images. A geometric branch then corrects the projected joint locations and intersects the resulting rays; a learned gate blends this geometric estimate with a learned pose update. This formulation introduces an explicit stereo constraint while retaining flexibility when the rays provide weak depth constraints. In local evaluations on the official device-relative test splits, EgoRayFormer achieves three-seed mean MPJPEs of 17.64 mm on UnrealEgo2 and 49.55 mm on UnrealEgo-RW, improving to 47.57 mm with UnrealEgo2 pre-training. In paired validation ablations, removing the geometric branch increases MPJPE in all three seeds at both input resolutions. With a common ResNet-18 encoder and input resolution, our system reduces RW test MPJPE by 9.09 mm relative to the device-relative EgoPoseFormer baseline. A separate protocol analysis quantifies the difference between device-relative and oracle-root evaluation.

</details>


### [46] [CAOS-SLAM: Class-Agnostic Object-Aware Real-Time vSLAM for Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26633)
*Gaia Borghi,João Carlos Virgolino Soares,Angelo Bratta,Shahab Heshmati-Alamdari,Claudio Semini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CAOS-SLAM 在 ORB-SLAM3 基础上引入混合感知流水线，实时检测、跟踪并过滤已知和未知动态物体，从而提升动态环境下的 vSLAM 定位鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态环境中的运动物体会引入错误观测、破坏地图重建并降低定位精度；现有 vSLAM 方法常依赖预定义语义类别或昂贵的逐像素分类，限制其鲁棒性和实际适用性。

Method: 提出 CAOS-SLAM：按需执行全景分割进行物体发现，并通过未分类区域的深度聚类实例化未知候选物体；在全景更新之间，利用轻量检测或基于世界坐标系卡尔曼预测、深度一致性和外观相似性的时序掩码传播维护持久物体轨迹；随后利用估计的物体运动剔除 SLAM 流水线中的动态特征。

Result: 在多个动态 RGB-D 数据集和真实实验中，CAOS-SLAM 相比最先进动态 vSLAM 方法取得更高定位精度：未知动态物体场景定位误差最多降低 59%，严重遮挡场景最多降低 88%，并在笔记本电脑上达到 30 FPS 的中位处理速度；跟踪模块还可输出物体轨迹和速度估计。

Conclusion: CAOS-SLAM 能实时处理已知和未知运动物体，提升动态环境下定位与建图鲁棒性，并为感知、导航和运动规划等未来应用提供物体轨迹与速度信息；代码和数据集将在接收后发布。

Abstract: Robust visual Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (vSLAM) in dynamic environments faces challenges due to the presence of moving objects that may introduce erroneous observations, corrupt the map reconstruction and overall degrade localization accuracy. Existing vSLAM approaches often rely on predefined semantic classes or costly pixel-wise classification, limiting their robustness or applicability in real-world scenarios. This paper presents CAOS-SLAM, a dynamic vSLAM framework that extends ORB-SLAM3 with a hybrid perception pipeline to detect, track, and filter both semantically known and previously unseen moving objects in real-time. On-demand panoptic segmentation is performed for object discovery, with candidate unknown objects instantiated through depth-based clustering of unclassified regions. Between panoptic updates, persistent object tracks are updated using lightweight detection when available or temporal mask propagation based on world-frame Kalman prediction, depth consistency, and appearance similarity. The estimated object motion is then used to reject dynamic features from the SLAM pipeline. Experimental results on multiple dynamic RGB-D datasets and real-world experiments demonstrate improved localization accuracy over state-of-the-art dynamic vSLAM approaches, achieving up to 59% lower localization error in scenes characterized by unknown dynamic objects and up to 88% lower error in heavily occluded scenes, while achieving a median processing rate of 30 FPS on a laptop. Furthermore, the tracking module provides estimates of object trajectories and velocities, supporting potential future applications in perception, navigation, and motion-planning tasks in dynamic environments. Code and dataset will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [47] [A Probabilistic, Rotation-Preserving Kernel Operator Surrogate for Continuum-Robot Design Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26605)
*Branden Frieden,Alan Kuntz,Varun Shankar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向腱驱动连续机器人（TDCR）的不确定性感知算子学习代理模型，可跨机器人设计与驱动条件泛化，并预测完整平衡构型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TDCR代理模型难以同时实现跨设计泛化、数据高效训练和可靠不确定性量化；需要减少对仿真器采样的依赖并识别不支持的设计区域。

Method: 采用非平稳高斯过程核，其度量由浅层神经网络从力学感知的设计坐标中学习，用于定义设计到位姿的算子、后验不确定性和序贯仿真器采集准则；以空间核表示 backbone 位置与旋转应变，并在 SO(3) 上直接重建姿态以保持材料标架和腱几何；使用 ASkotch 与 Nystrom 预条件实现可扩展核算子求解，并用独立 Nystrom 近似后验协方差。

Result: 实验表明，基于核覆盖的采集相较 IID 采样将平均轨迹误差降低约15%；对越来越不支持的设计，后验方差增大；相较已有神经算子TDCR模型，方法在约少5倍训练设计的情况下精度提高30-50%，并额外提供后验不确定性估计。

Conclusion: 该方法通过力学感知的非平稳GP算子学习，在TDCR平衡构型预测中实现了更高精度、更高数据效率和可用的不确定性量化，并支持跨设计与驱动条件泛化。

Abstract: We develop an uncertainty-aware, operator-learning-based surrogate for tendon-driven continuum robots (TDCRs) that generalizes across robot design and actuation while predicting the complete equilibrium configuration. Our central idea is a nonstationary Gaussian-process (GP) kernel whose metric is learned by a shallow neural network from mechanics-aware design coordinates. The network learns how similarity changes across design space, while the resulting kernel defines the design-to-pose operator, its posterior uncertainty, and a criterion for sequential simulator acquisition. We represent backbone position and rotational strain with spatial kernels and reconstruct orientation directly on SO(3), preserving valid material frames and tendon geometry. We use ASkotch with Nystrom preconditioning for scalable kernel operator solves and a separate Nystrom approximation for posterior covariance. Experiments show that kernel-coverage acquisition reduces mean trajectory error by approximately 15% relative to IID sampling, while posterior variance increases for increasingly unsupported designs. Compared with prior neural-operator TDCR models, the method is 30-50% more accurate using approximately 5x fewer training designs while additionally providing posterior uncertainty estimates.

</details>


### [48] [HAF-VLA: Hierarchical Action Flow Framework for Humanoid Vision-Language-Action Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26584)
*Langzhe Gu,Chengkai Hou,Meng Li,Xinhua Wang,Xinyuan Lv,Shuanghao Bai,Guangrun Li,Jingyang He,Ziluo Ding,Zhiyuan Xu,Ning Ding,Kuan Cheng,Jian Tang,Shanghang Zhang,Zhengping Che*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 HAF-VLA，一种面向人形机器人全身 loco-manipulation 的分层动作流 VLA 控制框架，通过分阶段去噪与跨阶段 KV 缓存条件增强现有 VLA 模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将通用视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略迁移到真实世界人形机器人 loco-manipulation 仍具挑战：人形机器人需在高维全身动作空间中协调移动、姿态、 gaze 与双臂操作，且基座或腰部动作不准确易引发不稳定且激进的上半身补偿。

Method: HAF-VLA 基于预训练 flow-matching VLA，继承视觉-语言前缀编码器与动作专家权重，并通过可学习阶段嵌入和跨阶段 KV-cache conditioning 适配人形全身 loco-manipulation；其不一次性生成完整动作块，而是在共享全身动作空间中渐进去噪：先生成移动与头部指令，再细化腰部，最后生成全身操作；跨阶段 KV 缓存使后续阶段基于前序动作表示进行细化与增强。

Result: 在六个真实世界任务上的实验表明，该框架显著提升现有模型在人形机器人 loco-manipulation 任务中的性能。

Conclusion: HAF-VLA 为将强移动操作 VLA 适配到人形机器人全身 loco-manipulation 提供了一条有效路径。

Abstract: Humanoid robots hold great promise as general-purpose agents in human-centered environments, yet adapting generalist vision-language-action (VLA) policies to real-world humanoid loco-manipulation remains challenging. Unlike fixed-base or wheeled manipulators, humanoids must coordinate locomotion, posture, gaze, and dual-arm manipulation over a high-dimensional whole-body action space, where inaccurate base or waist motions can easily induce unstable and aggressive upper-body compensation. We propose HAF-VLA, a hierarchical action-flow framework for humanoid VLA control. Built upon a pretrained flow-matching VLA, HAF-VLA inherits the vision-language prefix encoder and action expert weights, and adapts them to humanoid whole-body loco-manipulation with learnable stage embeddings and cross-stage KV-cache conditioning. Instead of generating the full action chunk in a single pass, HAF-VLA progressively denoises a shared whole-body action space: first locomotion and head commands, then waist refinement, and finally full-body manipulation. To preserve structural dependencies across stages, we introduce a cross-stage KV cache conditioning mechanism, enabling subsequent action generation stages to refine and augment their outputs based on the action representations from preceding stages. Experimental results across six real-world tasks demonstrate that our framework significantly enhances the performance of existing models in humanoid loco-manipulation tasks, demonstrating an effective pathway for adapting strong mobile-manipulation VLAs to humanoid whole-body loco-manipulation.

</details>


### [49] [Learning Wheel Motion Uncertainty for Degeneracy-Aware LiDAR-Inertial Odometry on Quadrupedal Wheel-Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26550)
*Hairuo Wei,Yunfan Ren,Renda Yang,Qiangsheng Huang,Zhongyu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种紧耦合LiDAR-惯性-运动学里程计框架，学习每个轮子、与运动方向相关的过程协方差，并结合退化感知投影，提高四足轮腿机器人在LiDAR几何约束弱及轮滑/接触丢失情况下的里程计鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轮式运动学可为四足轮腿机器人提供里程计支持，尤其在LiDAR几何提供弱运动约束时；但轮滑和接触丢失会使滚动预测不可靠，且仅凭接触状态无法判断不同运动方向上的可靠性。

Method: 保留解析滚动预测，利用本体感知历史学习逐轮、方向相关的过程协方差；估计器维护移动轮心状态，通过前向运动学校正让学习到的不确定性调节滚动预测影响；退化感知投影（DAP）在几何弱方向抑制LiDAR约束；协方差网络（CovNet）通过可微滤波器、以仿真速度监督训练，并零微调部署到真实机器人。

Result: 在室内数据集中退化率最高的六个房间序列上，相比FAST-LIO2平均每序列位置RMSE降低66.2%，相比固定协方差变体降低31.0%；在严重退化走廊运行中端点位置误差为0.198 m，而FAST-LIO2为7.531 m。

Conclusion: 结果表明该方法在挑战性几何与轮式运动条件下具有鲁棒里程计性能；作者计划公开代码和多模态数据集。

Abstract: Wheel kinematics can support odometry for quadrupedal wheel-legged robots when LiDAR geometry provides weak motion constraints. However, slip and contact loss make rolling predictions unreliable, and contact state alone cannot determine their reliability across motion directions. We propose a tightly coupled LiDAR-inertial-kinematic odometry framework that retains analytical rolling predictions and learns per-wheel, direction-dependent process covariances from proprioceptive history. The estimator maintains moving wheel-center states, allowing the learned uncertainty to regulate the influence of rolling predictions through forward-kinematic corrections. Degeneracy-aware projection (DAP) complements this mechanism by suppressing LiDAR constraints along geometrically weak directions. The learned covariance network (CovNet) is trained through a differentiable filter using simulated velocity supervision and deployed on the real robot without fine-tuning. On the six room sequences with the highest degeneracy ratios in our indoor dataset, the method reduces mean per-sequence position root mean square error (RMSE) by 66.2% relative to FAST-LIO2 and by 31.0% relative to a fixed-covariance variant. In a severely degenerate corridor run, it achieves an endpoint position error of 0.198 m, compared with 7.531 m for FAST-LIO2. These results demonstrate robust odometry under challenging geometric and wheel-motion conditions. We plan to publicly release our code and multimodal dataset. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [50] [ReInsVLN: Revisiting Historical Evidence for Training-Free Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26545)
*Siwei Cheng,Hao Wu,Zitong Qi,Xinming Zhang,Haoyu Xi,Peiran Yin,Haozhe Hu,Xudong Wang,Xiaoyu Shen,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出诊断基准ReInsBench，发现历史复习式重指令可利用现有任务上下文恢复导航失败，并提出免训练框架ReInsVLN提升冻结VLN导航器的记忆构建与利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLN依赖保留和使用相关历史观察，但历史增长带来冗余，且保留信息可能未被充分利用；需要诊断历史利用情况并改进冻结导航器的记忆机制。

Method: ReInsBench通过受控延续试验检验历史利用；ReInsVLN为免训练框架，包含Coverage- and Instruction-Aware Compression（时序冗余缩减、覆盖感知保护、指令引导token选择）构建紧凑视觉上下文，以及Forgetting-Inspired History Reminders按预定义计划引导导航器回访保留观察且无需额外模型调用。

Result: 在R2R Val-Unseen上，ReInsVLN相对原生StreamVLN和InternVLA-N1成功率分别提升8.48和3.37个百分点，达到64.27%和64.44%；视觉压缩在匹配观察负载下基本保持性能，并将prefill延迟和KV cache内存最多降低21.77%和32.25%；人形与四足机器人真实试验支持其用于顺序指令跟随和序数导航。

Conclusion: 历史复习重指令与紧凑记忆利用能有效提升冻结VLN导航器的成功率和效率，并具备真实机器人应用潜力。

Abstract: Vision-and-language navigation (VLN) relies on retaining and using relevant observations. Yet growing histories introduce redundancy, and retained information may remain underused. We introduce ReInsBench, a diagnostic benchmark that examines history utilization through controlled continuation trials. Its results show that history-review reinstruction can recover navigation failures using the existing task context. Motivated by this finding, we propose ReInsVLN, a training-free framework supporting memory construction and utilization in frozen navigators. Coverage- and Instruction-Aware Compression constructs compact visual context through temporal redundancy reduction, coverage-aware protection, and instruction-guided token selection. Forgetting-Inspired History Reminders guide navigators to revisit retained observations according to a predefined schedule, without additional model calls. On R2R Val-Unseen, ReInsVLN improves success rates by 8.48 and 3.37 percentage points over the native StreamVLN and InternVLA-N1, reaching 64.27% and 64.44%, respectively. Visual compression largely preserves navigation performance while reducing prefill latency and KV cache memory by up to 21.77% and 32.25%, respectively, on matched observation workloads. Real-world trials on humanoid and quadruped robots further support the applicability of ReInsVLN to sequential instruction following and ordinal navigation.

</details>


### [51] [Learning Geometry-Aware Planning Representations for Monocular Flight with Cross-Modal Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26508)
*Wencan Feng,Bailing Tian,Yulin Hui,Zhiyu Li,Junjie Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种几何感知的单目视觉规划框架，通过跨模态蒸馏将深度专家中与规划相关的几何特征迁移到RGB规划器，使MAV无需显式深度重建即可实现高速避障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目相机相比深度相机、立体视觉和LiDAR更轻量低成本，但RGB图像缺乏直接度量线索且外观随环境变化大，导致单目高速飞行与避障仍然困难。

Method: 方法从两帧图像、相对位姿和MAV状态预测轨迹；不显式重建深度，而是先训练基于深度的几何专家，再利用配对RGB-D观测将其规划相关特征通过跨模态蒸馏迁移到单目分支，并面向下游轨迹规划目标进行适配；同时使用3D Gaussian Splatting仿真器渲染观测，用于数据采集、配对RGB-D监督和仿真评估。

Result: 在仿真中训练的规划器无需真实世界微调即可部署到真实环境，实现最高6.5 m/s速度下的障碍物规避。

Conclusion: 结果表明，特征级几何监督与跨模态蒸馏能够赋予单目规划器规划相关的几何表征，结合3DGS仿真可实现轻量、低成本且高速的MAV单目避障。

Abstract: Existing high-speed navigation systems for micro aerial vehicles (MAVs) commonly rely on depth cameras, stereo vision, or LiDAR to obtain explicit geometric information, while monocular cameras offer a lighter and lower-cost alternative. However, monocular flight remains challenging because RGB images offer no direct metric cues and visual appearance varies considerably across environments. We propose a geometry-aware monocular visual planning framework that predicts trajectories from two image frames, their relative pose, and the MAV state. Without explicitly reconstructing depth, the proposed method complements the downstream planning objective with feature-level geometric supervision by transferring the planning-relevant representation learned by a privileged depth expert to the RGB planner through cross-modal distillation. The framework first trains a depth-based geometric expert, then distills its planning features into a monocular branch using paired RGB-D observations, and finally adapts the planner to the downstream trajectory planning objective. This design encourages the policy to learn planning-relevant geometric features, enabling collision avoidance. We further use a 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) simulator to render visual observations for data collection, paired RGB-D supervision, and simulation evaluation. The planner trained in simulation is then deployed in real-world environments without real-world fine-tuning, achieving obstacle avoidance at speeds of up to 6.5 m/s.

</details>


### [52] [Learn the Task, Not the Schedule: Reducing Spurious Cross-Arm Coupling in Bimanual Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26507)
*Shuyuan Hu,Deng-Ke Wu,Yifan Jin,Qianhang Luo,Mingchen Ju,Kai Ye,Bo Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出约束保持时间重组（CTR），一种训练时数据干预方法：保留双臂示范中各臂局部传感器运动内容，对片段重新定时以生成可行调度，同时保持任务必需的跨臂约束，从而减少双臂模仿学习中的虚假跨臂耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂示范同时包含机器人应做什么以及各臂何时行动的特定调度。标准模仿学习不区分任务必需的跨臂约束与偶然的调度选择，可能利用对侧臂进度作为捷径线索，在当前任务阶段相对时序无关时产生虚假跨臂耦合。

Method: CTR在训练时保留记录的臂局部传感器运动内容，将对应片段重定时为实现可行调度的段，并确保保留任务必需的跨臂约束。作者用CTR训练Diffusion Policy、Action Chunking with Transformers和π0.5，并在四个仿真任务和四个真实机器人任务上评估。

Result: 在六种真实机器人双臂进度状态初始化下，CTR将最差状态成功率从最强非CTR调度混合的56%提升到76%。在π0.5的Independent任务中，Concurrent调度分布下4 s暂停向对侧臂转移2.56 s延迟，而CTR仅转移0.25 s。真实机器人上，CTR仅在0–4%的试验中违反任务约束，窄调度基线为36–98%。

Conclusion: CTR能够将偶然调度选择与任务必需约束解耦，减少虚假跨臂耦合，并提升双臂策略在进度错位初始化和真实机器人环境中的成功率与约束满足率。

Abstract: A bimanual demonstration specifies both what the robot should do and one particular schedule for when each arm should act. Standard imitation learning does not distinguish task-required cross-arm constraints from incidental scheduling choices, and may therefore exploit opposite-arm progress as a shortcut cue, creating spurious cross-arm coupling even where relative timing is irrelevant for the current task phase. We introduce Constraint-preserving Temporal Recombination (CTR), a training-time data intervention that preserves recorded arm-local sensorimotor content while retiming the corresponding segments to realize admissible schedules that preserve task-required cross-arm constraints. We use CTR to train Diffusion Policy, Action Chunking with Transformers, and $\pi_{0.5}$, and evaluate them on four simulated and four real-robot tasks. Across six real-robot bimanual progress-state initializations, CTR raises worst-state success from 56% for the strongest non-CTR schedule mixture to 76%. With $\pi_{0.5}$ on an Independent task, a 4 s pause transfers 2.56 s of delay to the opposite arm under the Concurrent schedule distribution, but only 0.25 s with CTR. On real robots, CTR violates task constraints in only 0--4% of trials, versus 36--98% for narrow-schedule baselines. Code will be released at this https URL .

</details>


### [53] [SPAR: Structural Pruning and Action Recovery for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26487)
*Shenli Zheng,Zhou Fang,Yuan Du,Yijiang Liu,Li Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SPAR，通过结构化剪枝与强化学习恢复，将VLA模型π0.5大幅压缩并保持/提升操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人端载计算资源有限，VLA模型推理成本高；但激进压缩会显著降低操作性能，仅靠监督微调难以恢复。

Method: SPAR对LLM主干的隐藏维和FFN中间维同时进行结构化剪枝，采用剪枝感知调优，并在剪枝后用强化学习恢复策略性能；在π0.5上验证。

Result: 语言骨干从2.34B降至0.09B，全模型从3.37B降至1.12B，分别约去除96%和67%参数；LIBERO四个套件平均成功率从84.55%提升至93.45%（+8.90个百分点）；压缩策略相比未剪枝模型获得1.98×推理加速。

Conclusion: 结果表明LLM主干中间维存在大量冗余，剪枝后强化学习可有效恢复紧凑VLA策略的操作性能。

Abstract: The limited onboard computing resources of robots make the inference demands of vision-language-action (VLA) models costly to accommodate. Reducing these demands through aggressive compression, however, can degrade manipulation performance beyond what supervised fine-tuning alone recovers. We present SPAR (Structural Pruning and Action Recovery), which simultaneously prunes the hidden and feed-forward intermediate dimensions of the large language model (LLM) backbone through pruning-aware tuning, then applies reinforcement learning (RL) to recover policy performance. Applied to $\pi_{0.5}$, SPAR reduces the language backbone from 2.34B to 0.09B parameters and the full model from 3.37B to 1.12B, removing approximately 96% and 67% of their parameters, respectively. At this compression level, RL raises average success across the four LIBERO suites from 84.55% to 93.45%, an 8.90-percentage-point gain. The compressed policy achieves a $1.98\times$ full-policy inference speedup over the unpruned model. These results identify substantial intermediate-dimension redundancy and show that post-pruning RL can recover manipulation performance in compact policies.

</details>


### [54] [ALIGN: Aligning Condition Estimation and Expression in Advantage-Conditioned Behavior Cloning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26455)
*Zechen Wang,Zhenqi Qiu,Houlong Xiong,Suohang Zhang,Yiyu Ren,Wanting Xu,Hongfei Niu,Chengyang He,Ge Sun,Ran Cheng,Qian Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ALIGN：一种面向视觉-语言-动作策略的条件对齐 Advantage-Conditioned Behavior Cloning（AC-BC）框架，用于从混合质量的真实机器人数据中学习鲁棒操作策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类采集的机器人操作数据常同时包含成功、重试、暂停、恢复和失败；AC-BC 可利用混合质量数据，但现有流程通过阈值化估计优势分数产生噪声条件，并通过 prompt 弱影响动作向量场，导致条件信号被削弱。

Method: ALIGN 直接在策略面向的标签空间中预测类别式成对条件，并将条件通过 AdaRMS 调制注入 DiT 动作专家，且与 flow time 融合；在 ARX X5 叠碗和 Franka 挂钥匙的真实机器人混合数据上评估，数据包含标准演示与重试密集轨迹。

Result: ALIGN 提升了条件保真度和条件向量场可分性，闭环成功率分别达到 90.0% 和 56.7%，优于最强 AC-BC 基线的 56.7% 和 33.3%。

Conclusion: 将条件从估计阶段到动作预测阶段进行对齐，是从成功、恢复与错误共存的数据中提取鲁棒操作策略的有效方法。

Abstract: Human-collected robot manipulation data often mix successful behavior with retries, pauses, recoveries, and failures. Advantage-Conditioned Behavior Cloning (AC-BC) can exploit such mixed-quality data, but only if the conditioning signal is both accurately constructed from offline trajectories and effectively incorporated into the policy's action-vector-field prediction. Existing pipelines can weaken this signal by thresholding estimator-derived advantage scores into noisy conditions, and by routing conditions through prompts that weakly affect the action vector field. We introduce ALIGN, a condition-aligned AC-BC framework for vision-language-action policies. ALIGN directly predicts categorical pairwise conditions in the policy-facing label space and injects them into a DiT action expert through AdaRMS modulation fused with flow time. We evaluate ALIGN on deliberately mixed real-robot datasets for ARX X5 bowl stacking and Franka key hanging, each combining standard demonstrations with retry-heavy trajectories. ALIGN improves condition fidelity, strengthens conditional vector-field separability, and achieves 90.0% and 56.7% closed-loop success, compared with 56.7% and 33.3% for the strongest AC-BC baseline. These results indicate that aligning conditions from estimation to action prediction is an effective way to extract robust manipulation policies from data in which success, recovery and error coexist.

</details>


### [55] [A Human-in-the-Loop Comparison of Proportional Control and Adaptive Gravity Compensation in Elbow EMG-Driven Exoskeletons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26445)
*Francesco Pascucci,Eldison Dimo,Davide Costanzi,Mario Sedilesu,Andrea Calanca*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究在人机闭环条件下系统比较了上肢肘关节外骨骼的五种肌电驱动控制策略，发现模型型自适应重力补偿（尤其基于递归最小二乘在线负载估计的AGC-RLS）在负载下相较比例肌电控制能同时降低身心负荷并提升运动表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上肢外骨骼多采用比例肌电控制（PMC），其放大用户努力但在高辅助增益下可能损害稳定性；肌电驱动自适应重力补偿（AGC）可抵消重力矩并随神经肌肉活动持续调整，被视为有前景的替代方案，但上述策略尚未在人机闭环环境中得到系统比较。

Method: 14名健康受试者佩戴主动肘关节外骨骼完成目标到达任务，在0 kg与2 kg负载条件下比较五种控制算法：两种不同辅助水平的PMC、纯积分AGC（AGC-PI）、融合运动学与加速度信息的模型型AGC（AGC-AW），以及基于递归最小二乘（RLS）在线估计负载的新型模型型AGC（AGC-RLS）。评价指标包括肌肉活动、运动准确性、任务效率与主观工作负荷。

Result: PMC呈现体力付出与任务表现之间的权衡：低辅助水平在负载下需更大体力付出，而高增益损害任务执行；AGC-PI降低了主动肌活动，却诱发拮抗肌激活。相比之下，两种模型型AGC均能有效补偿外部负载，降低体力与认知需求，并改善运动准确性与任务完成时间。

Conclusion: 模型型肌电驱动AGC，尤其是基于递归最小二乘在线负载估计的AGC-RLS，在负载条件下优于PMC，可在降低身心负荷的同时改善上肢外骨骼的任务表现；结果支持对自适应重力补偿策略开展进一步的人机闭环优化研究。

Abstract: Electromyography (EMG)-driven control enables wearable robots to assist users based on their neuromuscular activity. Upper-limb exoskeletons typically rely on proportional myoelectric control (PMC), which amplifies user effort but may compromise stability at high assistance levels. EMG-driven adaptive gravity compensation (AGC) offers a promising alternative, counterbalancing gravitational torques while continuously adapting to neuromuscular activity. However, these approaches have not been systematically compared in a human-in-the-loop setting. This study evaluates five control algorithms: two PMC strategies at different assistance levels, a purely integral AGC (AGC-PI), a kinematics- and acceleration-aware model-based (MB) AGC (AGC-AW), and a novel MB AGC approach based on Recursive Least Squares (AGC-RLS) for online payload estimation. Fourteen healthy participants performed target-reaching tasks using an active elbow exoskeleton under 0 kg and 2 kg loading conditions. Performance metrics included muscular activity, movement accuracy, task efficiency, and subjective workload. PMC exhibited a trade-off between physical effort and task performance: low assistance levels required greater physical effort under load, whereas high gains impaired task execution. AGC-PI reduced agonist activity but induced antagonist activation. In contrast, both model-based AGC approaches effectively compensated for external payloads, reducing physical and cognitive demands while improving movement accuracy and completion time.

</details>


### [56] [RoboMMT: Learning Stable Tracking of Modulated Expressive and Interactive Robot Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26414)
*Till Hielscher,Kai O. Arras*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 RoboMMT，一种模块化控制架构，将参数化表现性动作生成、基于物理的角色控制和面向空间交互目标的反应式适应相耦合，使动态不稳定平台上的机器人既能执行表现性动作又能保持稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表现性机器人动作可提升人机交互的自然性、有效性和亲和力，但在动态不稳定平台上，若表现性与物理控制解耦，反应式执行表现性身体动作可能使机器人接近稳定性极限。

Method: RoboMMT 训练一个以参考动作为条件的跟踪策略，覆盖广泛表现性域，以忠实实现指令动作并维持动态稳定；同时训练轻量、任务特定的交互策略来调制参考生成器的空间目标吸引点，并将稳定化跟踪策略保留在闭环中。

Result: 在四足机器人和两个人形机器人上的评估表明，学习到的跟踪器在整个调制域内保持稳定和准确，而缺乏该覆盖训练的跟踪器会失去平衡、降低跟踪精度或两者兼失。将闭环目标适应叠加于该基础后，可实现交互式面向和指向行为，同时保持稳定执行和相当的参考跟踪保真度；机器人学会主动重分配体重并采取纠正步。四足机器人重扰动真实实验展示了零样本 sim-to-real 迁移。

Conclusion: RoboMMT 通过将表现性动作生成、稳定跟踪控制与闭环目标适应分层结合，为动态不稳定平台上的表现性人机交互提供了一种兼顾稳定性、跟踪精度和交互反应性的控制框架，并具备零样本仿真到现实迁移能力。

Abstract: Expressive robot motion can improve naturalness, effectiveness, and perceived likability in human-robot interaction. When deployed on dynamically unstable platforms, however, reactive execution of expressive body motion may bring the robot to its stability limits, especially when expressivity is decoupled from physical control. In this paper, we present RoboMMT (Robot Modulated Motion Tracking), a modular control architecture that couples parameterized expressive motion synthesis, physics-based character control, and reactive adaptation to spatial interaction goals. Our approach trains a reference-conditioned tracking policy across a broad domain of expressivity, enabling it to faithfully realize the commanded motions while maintaining dynamic stability. Goal reactivity is achieved by training lightweight, task-specific interaction policies that modulate the reference generator's spatial goal-attraction point, with the stabilizing tracking policy kept in the loop. Evaluations on a quadruped and two humanoid robots show that the learned tracker remains stable and accurate across the entire modulation domain, whereas trackers trained without this coverage lose balance, tracking accuracy, or both. We show that layering closed-loop goal adaptation over this foundation enables interactive facing and pointing behaviors, while maintaining stable execution and comparable reference-tracking fidelity. The robot learns to proactively redistribute its weight and take corrective steps. Zero-shot sim-to-real transfer is demonstrated through real-robot experiments on a quadruped under heavy disturbances.

</details>


### [57] [ForagerSim: Evaluating Embodied Map-Based Reasoning in Large-Scale Dynamic Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26395)
*Tomáš Musil,Martin Kotík,Michal Vavrečka,Karla Štěpánová,Sebastian Scherer,Martin Saska*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 ForagerSim，一个用于研究具身智能体是否以及如何学习并使用类地图表征的跨学科仿真平台与基准，覆盖从机器人搜救到动物觅食的多种地图推理任务，并通过多难度轴实验揭示现有强化学习方法在复杂/大规模环境中的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动物能构建并灵活使用认知地图进行导航和任意任务空间推理，而现有具身 AI 要么依赖易失效的人工地图管线且局限于度量空间，要么学习到的策略很少形成类地图表征。核心困难在于缺少真正要求地图推理的具身 AI 基准。

Method: 构建 ForagerSim：高度模块化仿真环境，可独立调节感知（2D 抽象传感至 RGB/LiDAR）、尺度（房间级至多公里级）、拓扑（开阔地至复杂迷宫）、动态（烟雾、碎片、传感器/运动故障等）和任务（2D 覆盖至觅食启发的井交替任务）。在 8 个示例世界中比较强化学习基线、简单工程化机器人栈和人类玩家，分析不同难度轴对方法的影响。

Result: 当感知简化为 2D 时，学习策略在简单拓扑中的多目标搜索和井交替任务上表现良好；但在更大规模或更复杂拓扑环境中，其表现落后于人类，而基于地图的基线表现良好，表明地图推理是这些场景中的瓶颈。

Conclusion: ForagerSim 提供了面向地图推理的具身任务研究平台，并指出当前学习型策略在复杂、大规模环境中仍缺乏可扩展的类地图表征与推理能力；地图式方法在这些场景中更有效，未来工作需要将学习与地图构建/使用更紧密结合。

Abstract: Animals build cognitive maps of vast environments, use them to perform map-based navigation, and might even be reusing the same circuitry used in spatial reasoning for creating and using maps of arbitrary task-spaces. In contrast, current embodied AI systems either rely on hand-engineered mapping pipelines, which break in the presence of unmodeled dynamics and are restricted to metric space, or on learned policies that rarely form map-like representations at all. Developing architectures that could learn to build and use map-like representations as flexibly as animals is, however, difficult because few embodied AI benchmarks actually require map-based reasoning. To address this, we present ForagerSim --- an interdisciplinary research platform and simulation environment focused on embodied tasks that require map-based reasoning, with scenarios ranging from mobile-robot search and rescue to animal foraging in both complex mazes and vast, potentially infinite open areas. ForagerSim is highly modular and allows modifying independent axes of difficulty related to perception (abstract 2D sensing to full RGB or LiDAR), scale (room-scale to multi-km scale), topology (open-fields to complex mazes), dynamics (smoke, debris, sensorimotor failures and others) and tasks (from simple 2D coverage to foraging-inspired well-alternation tasks). We compare reinforcement learning baselines with a simple engineered robotics stack and a human player on 8 example worlds which show how different difficulty axes affect existing methods. Our results show that, when sensing is simplified to 2D, learned policies can perform multi-object search and well-alternation tasks surprisingly well in simple topologies, but they fall short of human performance in larger or more complex-topology environments where map-based baselines perform well, suggesting that map-based reasoning is the bottleneck in these scenarios.

</details>


### [58] [Cartender: Verifiable Language-Conditioned Driving by Learning the Drives Not Taken](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26390)
*Zhiwen Chen,Benjin Zhu,Yan Xie,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Cartender 利用反事实分支从真实驾驶记录中构造可行/不可行指令-动作对，学习场景约束下的语言条件轨迹生成，从而提升指令跟随与场景可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶场景是一对多的：场景决定可行行为，指令从中选择，但日志只记录已执行动作，缺少对替代可行动作及不可支持指令的直接监督；因此忽略语言或无视场景约束的策略都可能拟合同一数据。

Method: 提出 Cartender：用视觉语言模型提出替代未来及其选择指令，并以不支持指令作负标签，构建匹配/不匹配的指令-动作对；学习场景-语言兼容性分数调节轨迹生成中的语言条件，并用奖励训练的语言残差对候选轨迹基于场景分数重排序；训练用分支，评估则在相同记录输入下交换指令并测行为而非与生成目标一致。

Result: 在 WOD-E2E 上，四个提案中最佳 RFS 为 8.642（3,603 个可行指令条件）；8,974 个准入提案平均 RFS 8.203，高于匹配日志轨迹的 7.906，67% 高于对应日志轨迹；同骨干仅匹配模仿相比，同场景配对指令成功率从 23.67% 提升到 57.00%，RFS 从 6.32 提升到 7.10；门控将不可行指令执行率从 53.36% 降至 14.18%。

Conclusion: Cartender 通过“未走的驾驶”进行反事实监督，有效学习场景-语言兼容性，显著改善同场景指令跟随并抑制不可行指令执行。

Abstract: Every driving scene is logged once, but driving is one-to-many: the scene determines which behaviors are feasible, while an instruction selects among them. A log records only the action taken, leaving no direct supervision for alternative feasible actions or instructions the scene cannot support. Consequently, a policy that ignores language and one that follows it without regard to scene constraints can fit the same data. We present Cartender, which learns from the drives not taken. Counterfactual Branching constructs matched and mismatched instruction-action pairs from real recordings without a simulator: a vision-language model proposes alternative futures and their selecting instructions, while unsupported instructions provide negative labels. A learned scene-language compatibility score regulates language conditioning in trajectory generation, and a reward-trained language residual re-ranks generated candidates over a scene-based score. The branches are used for training; instruction following is evaluated by swapping instructions under identical recorded inputs and measuring behavior rather than agreement with generated targets. On WOD-E2E, the best of four proposals achieves an RFS of 8.642 across 3,603 feasible instruction conditions. Across 8,974 admitted proposals, mean RFS is 8.203 versus 7.906 for matched logged trajectories, and 67% score higher than their logged counterpart. Compared with matched-only imitation using the same backbone, Cartender improves same-scene paired instruction success from 23.67% to 57.00% and RFS from 6.32 to 7.10; its gate reduces infeasible-instruction execution from 53.36% to 14.18%.

</details>


### [59] [Robotic Photography System based on Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26287)
*Song-Li Wu,Chubin Zhang,Wenbo Ding,Yansong Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出统一的视觉-语言-动作框架RoboPhoto-VLA，用于自主机器人摄影，联合建模感知、推理与相机控制，并通过结构化规划与美学偏好对齐提升拍摄效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器人摄影需要在复杂环境中理解拍摄意图、规划相机运动并生成符合人类审美的高质量图像，现有方法难以统一处理这些需求。

Method: RoboPhoto-VLA采用PhotoCoT进行结构化动作规划，并引入美学直接偏好优化（ADPO）使相机行为对齐人类审美偏好；同时构建OpenPhotography数据集，包含语言引导的机器人摄影演示用于训练与评估。

Result: 真实世界实验表明，RoboPhoto-VLA生成的 photographs 更符合人类审美偏好，并能有效泛化到未见主体、视角、姿态及复杂摄影指令。

Conclusion: RoboPhoto-VLA验证了统一视觉-语言-动作框架在自主机器人摄影中的有效性，可实现意图理解、相机运动规划与美学图像生成的协同优化。

Abstract: Modern robotic photography requires understanding photographic intent, planning camera motions, and producing aesthetically pleasing images in complex environments. We present RoboPhoto-VLA, a unified Vision-Language-Action framework that jointly models perception, reasoning, and camera control for autonomous robotic photography. RoboPhoto-VLA incorporates PhotoCoT for structured action planning and Aesthetic Direct Preference Optimization (ADPO) to align camera behaviors with human aesthetic preferences. To facilitate training and evaluation, we construct OpenPhotography, a robotic photography dataset with language-guided demonstrations. Extensive real-world experiments demonstrate that RoboPhoto-VLA produces photographs better aligned with human aesthetic preferences while generalizing effectively to unseen subjects, viewpoints, poses, and complex photographic instructions.

</details>


### [60] [AnchorFlow-VIO: Fixed-Template Monocular Visual-Inertial Odometry on LaMAria](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26210)
*Sudhir Pratap Yadav*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出并开源基于 Basalt 的单目视觉惯性里程计 AnchorFlow-VIO，在 LaMAria 上用统一配置评测 63 条测试记录，揭示冻结系统的类别性能差异、启动敏感性和评测证据不足问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在提供可复现的单目 VIO 系统与评测工具，并刻画固定配置系统在真实测试条件下的性能边界，说明仅凭完整输出、特征数量或挑选成功运行不足以证明定位鲁棒性。

Method: 系统仅用一个灰度相机和一个 IMU，采用出厂 IMU 校正、固定时间偏移调整、窗口化初始化和固定模板 patch 跟踪；在 LaMAria 上进行服务器统一配置评测，并开展固定模板与滚动模板、特征密度、不同起始时间偏移等消融/对比实验。

Result: 63 条测试记录的控制点得分为 Short 47.2、Medium 37.1、Long 24.8、Low light 35.1、Moving platform 15.6；四类密集召回类别的 5 m 位姿召回率分别为 84.6%、69.1%、53.9%、62.9%。10 条训练记录平均控制点得分 41.38，265,694 个图像时间戳均获得位姿（含 204 个填充位姿），但一条训练平台记录发散且得分为 0。固定模板在 4 条配对记录中 3 条优于滚动模板、1 条相反；提高特征密度结果混合。固定 30 s 与 120 s 起始偏移的 10 次新运行显示明显启动敏感性：低光记录早启动得分 6.14、晚启动 42.00。

Conclusion: AnchorFlow-VIO 的冻结配置在 LaMAria 上呈现明显的场景类别差异和起始时刻敏感性；完整位姿输出、特征计数及挑选的成功运行不能作为鲁棒定位的充分证据，需报告统一配置下的完整评测与可复现实验。

Abstract: We present AnchorFlow-VIO, an open-source Basalt-derived monocular visual-inertial odometry system, and evaluate a uniform configuration on LaMAria. The system uses one grayscale camera and one IMU, combining factory IMU correction, a fixed timing adjustment, windowed initialization and fixed-template patch tracking. Server evaluation of all 63 test recordings yields control-point scores of 47.2, 37.1, 24.8, 35.1 and 15.6 for Short, Medium, Long, Low light and Moving platform. Test pose recalls at 5 m are 84.6%, 69.1%, 53.9% and 62.9% for the four categories reporting dense recall. On ten training recordings, mean control-point score is 41.38 and all 265,694 image timestamps receive poses, including 204 filled poses. One training platform recording diverges and scores zero. A paired four-record comparison favors fixed over rolling templates on three recordings, while reversing that ranking on the fourth. Increasing feature density also yields mixed results. Ten fresh runs at prospectively fixed 30 s and 120 s offsets expose substantial start sensitivity: a low-light recording scores 6.14 at the earlier start and 42.00 at the later start. These results characterize the limits of the frozen system and show why complete output, feature counts and selected successful runs are insufficient evidence of robust localization. Source code, frozen configuration and reproducible submission tools are publicly available; training ablations are reported separately from the submitted test evaluation.

</details>


### [61] [K-AoI: A Spatio-Temporal Synchronization Metric for Digital Twin-based Teleoperation in Dynamic Network Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26201)
*Lu Tian,Zhenhui Yuan,Zexin Deng,Wenjuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出运动学信息年龄（K-AoI），以瞬时笛卡尔速度加权AoI，将时间陈旧度转化为空间偏差，并在6-DOF机械臂数字孪生遥操作测试床上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有同步度量多基于时间，难以刻画运动过程中延迟信息造成的空间后果，进而影响操作员态势感知与任务性能。

Method: 构建由6-DOF机械臂、Unity数字孪生和tc netem网络损伤组成的遥操作测试床，在七种网络条件下逐样本分析K-AoI与AoI的秩相关性，并使用ULAoI分析AoI尾部行为及GPD拟合。

Result: K-AoI与AoI的秩相关在所有网络下ρ≤0.65，卫星连接下降至ρ=0.22，表明K-AoI并非AoI的简单变换，而能捕捉速度相关的退化效应；AoI尾部拒绝常用GPD模型，反映确定性转发和有界抖动违反标准极值假设。

Conclusion: K-AoI是一种互补且具有物理意义的同步度量，可将网络延迟与数字孪生遥操作中任务相关的空间误差联系起来；数据集和测试床软件已公开。

Abstract: Synchronization between a digital twin (DT) and its physical robot is critical for effective teleoperation, as mismatches directly degrade operator awareness and task performance. Existing synchronization metrics are predominantly time-based and rarely capture the spatial consequences of delayed information during motion. In this paper, we introduce Kinematic Age of Information (K-AoI), a spatio-temporal metric that weights AoI by instantaneous Cartesian velocity, translating temporal staleness into physically meaningful spatial divergence. We implement and evaluate K-AoI on a DT teleoperation testbed comprising a six-degrees-of-freedom (6-DOF) manipulator, a Unity-based twin, and controlled network impairments using tc netem across seven network conditions. Per-sample analysis shows that K-AoI exhibits rank-correlations with AoI at $\rho \leq 0.65$ across all networks, dropping to $\rho = 0.22$ under satellite connections. This shows that K-AoI is not a simple transformation of AoI, but instead captures velocity-dependent degradation effects that time-only metrics cannot resolve. We further analyze AoI tail behavior using Ultra-Low AoI (ULAoI) and find that the commonly assumed Generalized Pareto Distribution (GPD) model is rejected. This reflects the impact of deterministic forwarding and bounded jitter, which violate standard extreme-value assumptions. Overall, K-AoI provides a complementary and physically grounded synchronization metric, linking network delay to task-relevant spatial error in digital-twin teleoperation systems. The dataset and testbed software are publicly available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [62] [Data-Efficient Learning from Demonstration for Humanoid Robots via Feature-Level Perception and Prompt-Based Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26170)
*Keyhan Rayati,AliReza Beigy,Anoosheh Saadati,Pourya Shahverdi,Mehdi Tale Masouleh,Ahmad Kalhor,Wing-Yue Geoffrey Louie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种数据高效的人形机器人示教学习框架，从演示视频中提取紧凑的任务特定特征，训练时序模型预测关节轨迹，并以少量演示实现操作技能学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多基于视觉的示教学习方法依赖大规模数据集和资源密集的端到端训练，难以满足数据高效的人形机器人技能获取需求。

Method: 集成分层视觉预处理、基于Kinect的人体关节估计、提示式目标检测和深度信息，从演示视频中提取人体运动特征与三维物体位置；用这些紧凑特征训练时序深度学习模型预测机器人关节角轨迹，并在NAO人形机器人上实现，进行仿真定量评估和实物演示。

Result: 仿真中，拾放任务仅用16次演示达到78%成功率，使用100次演示提升至86%；推动任务使用8次演示达到71%成功率。

Conclusion: 结果表明该框架能有效支持数据高效的人形机器人操作技能学习，并具备从仿真到实物硬件的可行性。

Abstract: Learning from Demonstration (LfD) allows robots to learn new skills from human examples; however, many vision-based methods require large datasets and resource-intensive end-to-end training. This paper introduces a data-efficient LfD framework for humanoid robots that learns manipulation skills from compact, task-specific features instead of raw images. The proposed approach integrates layered visual preprocessing, Kinect-based human joint estimation, prompt-based object detection, and depth information to extract human motion features and three-dimensional object locations from demonstration videos. These features are used to train a temporal deep learning model that predicts robot joint-angle trajectories for execution. The framework is implemented on a NAO humanoid robot, evaluated quantitatively in simulation, and demonstrated on physical hardware. In simulation, the robot achieves a 78% success rate on a pick-and-place task using only 16 demonstrations, which increases to 86% when trained with 100 demonstrations. The framework also achieves a 71% success rate on a pushing task using eight demonstrations, highlighting its effectiveness for data-efficient humanoid skill acquisition.

</details>


### [63] [Compositional Humanoid Manipulation through Isomorphic Teleoperation and Independent Skill Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26169)
*Yiming Wang,Peixian Yang,Lifeng Zhuo,Lai Wei,Xinling Du,Kun Song,Weiming Wang,Daolin Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HS-MoDP，通过语义路由在共享专家池上组合独立演示的手臂技能，并结合G1Exo外骨骼遥操作提升人形机器人接触操作技能学习与柜体工作流成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人操作需要可复用的手臂技能，但逐一演示所有技能组合不现实；同时，全身接触操作的高质量示范获取困难，现有VR-IK末端执行器遥操作可靠性不足。

Method: HS-MoDP对分别演示的手臂技能进行独立语义路由，路由到共享专家池，在保留单臂技能控制的同时共享动作生成参数；语义调度器交替执行操作与重新定位阶段，将学习策略与现有运动控制器集成。为采集全身接触操作示范，开发同构外骨骼G1Exo，实现手臂关节级直接控制。

Result: 在Unitree G1上，HS-MoDP的技能组合强于基线；G1Exo比末端执行器VR-IK接触遥操作更可靠，其示范在四种策略架构上均改善接触技能学习；HS-MoDP将VR-IK示范切换为G1Exo示范后，完整柜体工作流成功率从35%提升到65%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，语义专家路由与关节级外骨骼示范可有效支持人形机器人可复用手臂技能组合和接触操作学习；项目硬件设计与源码将在评审后开源。

Abstract: Humanoid manipulation requires reusable arm skills, yet demonstrating every possible skill combination is impractical. We present Humanoid Semantic Mixture-of-Experts Diffusion Policy (HS-MoDP), which composes separately demonstrated arm skills through independent semantic routing over a shared expert pool. This design preserves arm-specific skill control while sharing action-generation parameters across arms. A semantic scheduler integrates the learned policy with an existing locomotion controller through alternating manipulation and repositioning stages, extending the effective workspace and enabling skill reuse across location changes. To acquire demonstrations of whole-body contact manipulation, we develop G1Exo, an isomorphic exoskeleton that provides direct joint-level control of arm configurations. Experiments on a Unitree G1 show stronger skill composition than the baselines. G1Exo enables more reliable contact teleoperation than end-effector VR-IK, and its demonstrations improve contact-skill learning across four policy architectures. For HS-MoDP, switching from VR-IK to G1Exo demonstrations raises full cabinet workflow success from 35% to 65%. All project hardware designs and source code will be released as open source after the review process.

</details>


### [64] [A GPU-Resident Pipeline for Multi-Robot 3D Inspection with Limited Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26106)
*Kryštof Pšenička,Stefan Edelkamp*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一套完整的GPU驻留管线，用于多机器人水下结构3D巡检；将联合6-DOF覆盖路径规划分解为五个核心阶段并端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人联合覆盖与路径规划是NP-hard问题，需同时处理有限视场可见性、表面覆盖比例、makespan/总成本权衡以及机器人间连续时间冲突，且缺乏端到端GPU集成方案。

Method: 输入三角网格和K台带短距有限视场相机的机器人，输出联合覆盖目标表面比例的6-DOF轨迹并最小化线性插值的makespan/总成本目标。管线分解为候选视点生成、可见性计算、集合覆盖、以混合整数规划求解的min-max车辆路径问题（采用提升的Miller-Tucker-Zemlin子tour消除）、以及带连续时间机器人间冲突检查的优先级多智能体路径规划；前后接网格加载/表面采样预处理与轨迹平滑后处理。贡献包括五阶段GPU加速集成及kernel设计、Lien ε-可见性的GPU批处理适配（四个自定义CUDA kernel）、基于插值目标的β感知单车tour上界并补充Desrochers-Laporte系数提升与forbidden-pair cuts、Zhou-Zeng GPU并行前沿A*的4D时空扩展。

Result: 在14个网格（最多720万面）上实验，消融路由cuts和并行前沿搜索，在轨迹平滑后审计无碰撞性与航点到达情况，并测量端到端解相对解析下界和LP松弛下界的gap；代码与原始结果公开。摘要未给出具体数值结果。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了端到端GPU加速的多机器人水下3D巡检规划集成方案，并通过消融、平滑后审计和与下界的gap评估验证各组件及整体解质量，具备可复现性。

Abstract: We present a complete, GPU-resident pipeline for multi-robot 3D inspection of underwater structures. Given a triangle mesh and a fleet of $K$ robots, each carrying a short-range, limited field-of-view camera, the pipeline computes 6-DOF trajectories that jointly cover a target fraction of the surface while minimizing a linearly interpolated makespan/total-cost objective. The joint problem is NP-hard, so we decompose it into five core stages - candidate viewpoint generation, visibility computation, set cover, min-max vehicle routing solved as a mixed integer program (MIP) with lifted Miller-Tucker-Zemlin subtour elimination, and priority-based multi-agent path finding with continuous-time inter-robot conflict checking - flanked by mesh loading and surface sampling as preprocessing and by trajectory smoothing as postprocessing. Our contributions are: an end-to-end GPU-accelerated integration of the five stages, with its kernel design documented; a GPU batch adaptation of Lien's $\varepsilon$-visibility with four custom CUDA kernels; a $\beta$-aware per-vehicle tour upper bound derived from the interpolated objective, supplementing the Desrochers-Laporte coefficient lift with forbidden-pair cuts; a 4D space-time extension of Zhou and Zeng's GPU parallel-frontier A*; and an experimental evaluation on 14 meshes (up to $7.2$M faces) that ablates the routing cuts and the parallel-frontier search, audits collision freedom and waypoint attainment \emph{after} trajectory smoothing, and measures the end-to-end gap against analytical and LP-relaxation lower bounds. Code and raw results are publicly available.

</details>


### [65] [OmniInteract: Towards Unified Whole-Body Interaction Control for Dual Humanoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26043)
*Yinghao Li,Tianyu Zhang,Zhuohao Li,Yi-Lin Wei,Fa-Ting Hong,Qing Zhang,Wei-Shi Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OmniInteract 通过表面感知重定向与可扩展残差策略学习，实现多样化双人形机器人物理交互，在保持运动控制保真度的同时提升接触与协调能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双人形机器人协作需要协调全身运动与精确接触；但人机形态差异会在重定向中破坏接触界面和空间关系，且联合学习运动技能与伙伴协调会随交互多样性增加而难以保持控制保真度。

Method: 提出 OmniInteract：一是 Surface Contact-Aware Retargeting，利用稠密人-机器人表面对应关系联合重定向两个人形机器人，保留显式交互接触界面及近场几何上下文；二是 Scalable Flow-based Interaction Policy，将双人形控制分解为可复用的运动基础与交互特定适配，由伙伴感知专家在冻结的全身运动先验上学习残差修正，并用条件流匹配在共享残差动作空间中统一。

Result: 实验表明 OmniInteract 提升了重定向保真度和双人形协调性，并在技能多样性增长时更好地保持交互性能；真实部署实现了成功接触建立、稳定全身协调以及外部扰动恢复。

Conclusion: OmniInteract 通过表面感知建模和可扩展残差策略学习，有效缓解形态差异与交互多样性带来的挑战，支持可扩展的双人形机器人物理交互技能整合与真实世界部署。

Abstract: Physical interaction is a key capability for humanoid collaboration, requiring coordinated whole-body motion and precise contact. However, learning diverse dual-humanoid interactions faces two major challenges: (1) human-robot morphological differences disrupt contact interfaces and spatial relationships during retargeting, and (2) jointly learning motor skills and partner coordination makes it difficult to retain control fidelity as interaction diversity grows. In this paper, we propose OmniInteract to address these challenges through surface-aware modeling and scalable residual policy learning. First, we introduce Surface Contact-Aware Retargeting, which leverages dense human-robot surface correspondences to jointly retarget both humanoids, preserving explicit interaction contact interfaces and their near-field geometric context. Second, we propose a Scalable Flow-based Interaction Policy for partner-conditioned residual generation. It factorizes dual-humanoid control into a reusable motor foundation and interaction-specific adaptations: partner-aware experts learn corrections over a frozen whole-body motion prior, while conditional flow matching unifies them in a shared residual action space. This enables scalable skill consolidation while retaining pretrained motor capabilities. Extensive experiments demonstrate that OmniInteract improves retargeting fidelity and dual-humanoid coordination while better retaining interaction performance as skill diversity grows. Real-world deployment further demonstrates successful contact establishment, stable whole-body coordination, and recovery from external perturbations.

</details>


### [66] [A Spiking Ring-Attractor Architecture for Representation and Control of Revolute Robot Joints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26027)
*Aung Htet,Alejandro Rodriguez Jimenez,Ruidong Ma,Alessandro Di Nuovo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种全脉冲神经控制架构，用环形吸引子编码旋转关节状态，使表示、调节与动作统一于符合SO(2)/SO(3)几何的神经形态控制框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转关节系统天然处于圆形状态空间，但多数控制架构用欧氏变量表示状态，忽略其拓扑几何，导致表示与系统几何不一致。

Method: 以环形吸引子编码关节状态；用稳态调节在分布式表示上计算目标状态失配；由对手增益调制群体将失配转化为活动波包的受控相移，实现环上的设定点调节。推导波包动力学遵循SO(2)几何及增益不对称产生有符号相速度；设计解析Fourier相位解码器，并用推挽脉冲群体实现其有符号分量。多关节时，将局部环形Fourier特征组合为乘积表示，通过正运动学线性解码SO(3)姿态。

Result: 在NEST中验证完整SO(2)调节机制及针对lift、pitch、yaw的脉冲乘积特征解码器；在Tiago机械臂实验中，解码后的环形动力学生成关节速度指令，产生有向运动，精度随关节而异。

Conclusion: 结果表明可构建一种神经形态控制框架，在环形吸引子基底中统一表示、调节和动作，并与旋转关节系统的几何结构相一致。

Abstract: Robotic systems with revolute joints evolve on circular state spaces, yet most control architectures represent these states using Euclidean variables that ignore this underlying geometry. We propose a fully spiking neural control architecture in which ring attractors encode joint states, enforcing representations consistent with the topology of revolute systems. Homeostatic regulation computes goal-state mismatch directly over these distributed representations, and opponent gain-modulated populations convert this mismatch into controlled phase shifts of the activity bump, implementing set-point regulation as motion on the ring. We show that the bump dynamics follow an SO(2) geometry, and that gain asymmetries induce a signed phase velocity on the ring. To interface distributed activity with scalar regulation signals, we derive an analytic Fourier phase decoder and implement its signed components using push-pull spiking populations. For multi-joint systems, we demonstrate that local ring-based Fourier features compose into product representations that linearly decode SO(3) orientations through forward kinematics. Experiments in NEST validate the full SO(2) regulation mechanism and the spiking product-feature decoder for lift, pitch, and yaw. Robot experiments on a Tiago arm show that decoded ring dynamics generate joint velocity commands, producing directed motion with joint-dependent accuracy. Together, these results establish a neuromorphic control framework in which representation, regulation, and action are unified within a ring-attractor substrate aligned with the geometry of revolute systems.

</details>


### [67] [Self-Supervised Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25988)
*Yuheng Qiu,Yutian Chen,Carlton Cort,Weison Huang,Shibo Zhao,Pieter Abbeel,Rocky Duan,Koushil Sreenath,C. Karen Liu,Carmelo Sferrazza,Junyi Geng,Sebastian Scherer,Guanya Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种无需真值轨迹的惯性里程计训练方法：先用短窗独立积分IMU的“近视教师”监督学生学习直接预测速度，再用位姿图蒸馏提供跨多窗的序列一致目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为新平台训练惯性里程计通常依赖真值轨迹，采集成本高；作者希望仅通过IMU标定和正常操作数据即可训练模型。

Method: 构造短窗独立积分IMU的教师模型，仅用短窗速度变化监督学生直接预测速度；随后通过位姿图蒸馏，用多窗位姿图产生的序列一致目标精炼模型。

Result: 在行人、无人机、四足机器人和人形机器人等六个数据集上，无速度或位置标签训练的模型平均速度误差与同架构监督基线仅差0.07 m/s；学生模型速度预测精度高于其积分教师。

Conclusion: 新平台惯性里程计训练不再需要采集真值轨迹，只需标定IMU并记录正常运行数据，即可获得接近监督基线的性能。

Abstract: Learning inertial odometry for a new platform usually requires ground-truth trajectories. We show that an inertial odometry model can instead be trained from scratch by a myopic teacher that integrates the IMU over each short window independently, a scheme we call integration this http URL , although supervised only by short-window velocity changes, the student learns to directly predict velocity, and does so more accurately than its integration teacher. Pose graph distillation then refines the model with sequence-consistent targets from a pose graph over many windows. Training on a new platform therefore no longer requires collecting ground-truth trajectories, but only calibrating the IMU and recording data during normal operation. Across six datasets spanning pedestrians, drones, quadrupeds, and a humanoid, the same recipe with no velocity or position labels trains models that come within $0.07$~m/s average velocity error of a same-architecture supervised baseline.

</details>


### [68] [FR-VLA: Implicit Force Reasoning VLA for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25981)
*Jiaju He,Zhiyuan Yang,Ruyang Han,Jiahong Tang,Zhiping Lin,Haiyue Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FR-VLA提出视觉-语言力推理模型，通过可学习的力查询token从视觉语言上下文生成力接纳信号，并用该信号调制外部token化力通路来生成动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA通常以固定路径注入力信息，未显式判断当前动作是否需要力反馈，因此需要在力进入动作通路前引入中间力相关性机制。

Method: 使用可学习力查询token根据视觉语言上下文估计物理反馈对当前任务的相关性，产生力接纳信号，并以此调制外部tokenized力通路进行动作生成。

Result: 在两种机器人平台、五个接触丰富任务的六个评估阶段中取得最高平均成功率；轨迹可视化显示力接纳随交互阶段变化。

Conclusion: 显式力相关性门控与接纳机制能有效融合力感知，并提升接触丰富机器人操作任务的表现。

Abstract: Integrating force awareness into Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models involves not merely encoding wrench measurements as an additional modality, but determining when and how strongly physical interaction should influence robot action generation. Existing VLAs that use force typically inject it through a fixed pathway, without explicitly determining when it is relevant to the current action. This calls for an intermediate force relevance mechanism inside the VLA, in which visual-language context estimates the relevance of physical feedback to the current task before force enters the action pathway. FR-VLA addresses this gap with a Vision-Language Force-Reasoning Model that uses learnable force query tokens to derive a force admission signal grounded in visual-language context, and uses this signal to modulate an external tokenized force pathway for action generation. In real-world experiments on five contact-rich tasks across two robotic platforms, FR-VLA achieves the highest mean success rate over six evaluated stages, and trajectory visualizations show that its force admission varies with the interaction phase.

</details>


### [69] [Reconstruction and Discrete-Event Simulation Assessment of an Analytical](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25967)
*Mohsen Reda Mahmoud Moursi,Ahmed Stohy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文构建了自动化仓储巷道内机器人拣选的分析-DES基准，在相同货架几何、穿梭车运动、搬运与拣选条件下比较Lerher等解析模型与仿真结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解析模型需要独立仿真校验，但缺乏在统一参数与周期时间定义下的系统性分析-DES对照基准。

Method: 研究循环包含水平穿梭车行驶、料箱搬运和存储位拣选。按相同定义复现解析表需采用dES=1.5 m、dTB=0 m。基准含180个场景：3种货架长度、2种成对运动剖面、n=1至30个访问拣选位，每场景10次仿真重复；n=1的6个无行驶间分量场景单独报告。

Result: 其余174个场景中，周期时间平均绝对相对偏差为0.8655%，中位数0.6758%，最大值7.0370%；MAE为2.411 s，RMSE为2.744 s。所有174个主场景的仿真场景均值周期时间均低于解析值。

Conclusion: 在测试条件下二者数值吻合较好，但并非完全一致，也未证明统计等价性或针对实际运行仓库的验证。

Abstract: This article presents an analytical-DES benchmark for intra-aisle robotic picking in automated warehousing. The investigated cycle combines horizontal shuttle travel, tote handling and item picking at storage locations. Simulation results are compared with the analytical formulation of Lerher et al. under the same rack geometry, shuttle-motion, handling and picking conditions, and the same definitions of cycle time and derived item throughput. Reproducing the published analytical tables requires the distance interpretation dES = 1.5 m and dTB = 0 m. The analytical-DES benchmark comprises 180 scenarios formed from three rack lengths, two paired motion profiles and n = 1 to 30 visited picking locations, with ten simulation replications per scenario. The six n = 1 cases are reported separately because no travel-between component is present. Across the remaining 174 scenarios, the mean absolute relative cycle-time discrepancy is 0.8655%, the median is 0.6758%, and the maximum is 7.0370%. The mean absolute error is 2.411 s and the RMSE is 2.744 s. The simulated scenario-mean cycle time is below the analytical value in all 174 principal scenarios. The results therefore show close numerical correspondence under the tested conditions, but not exact agreement, formal statistical equivalence, or validation against an operating warehouse.

</details>


### [70] [SkateNav: Learning Energy-Efficient Navigation through Adaptive Skating on Passive-Wheel Quadrupedal Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25962)
*Jeil Jeong,Taeyun Kim,Sangjin Kim,Jinyoung Choi,Sung-Eui Yoon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SkateNav：一种基于强化学习的被动轮四足机器人滑行导航统一框架，可联合学习转向、推进及其时间分配，并在仿真与真实环境中实现高效导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动轮四足机器人可通过腿驱动推进与动量驱动滑行实现节能运动，但导航需协调转向与间歇推进，并按任务需求调整推-滑时序；传统速度跟踪目标可能抑制滑行固有的速度变化，不利于高效滑行。

Method: 设计带时间预算的目标到达目标，使策略联合学习转向、推进及二者时间分配；采用自适应滑行周期组织滑行、推进和恢复阶段，并在每个控制步通过频率调制调整相位时长以适配导航需求。

Result: 仿真实验中平均成功率高、运输成本低于所评估的规划器-控制器基线；消融实验验证周期结构与频率自适应的贡献；真实实验在杂乱室内、坡道和室外铺装路面实现从仿真到被动轮Mini Cheetah的零样本迁移。

Conclusion: SkateNav通过自适应滑行周期与频率调制实现高效滑行导航，兼顾任务需求与能量效率，并具备仿真到现实的迁移能力。

Abstract: Passive-wheel quadrupedal robots offer energy-efficient locomotion through leg-driven propulsion and momentum-driven gliding. However, navigation requires coordinating steering with intermittent propulsion and adapting push-glide timing to task demands. Velocity-tracking objectives may suppress the speed variations inherent in skating, discouraging efficient gliding. To address these challenges, we propose SkateNav, a unified skating-navigation framework based on reinforcement learning. A time-budgeted goal-reaching objective allows the policy to jointly learn steering, propulsion, and their temporal allocation. An adaptive skating cycle structures gliding, pushing, and recovery, while frequency modulation at every control step adjusts phase durations to navigation demands. Simulation experiments demonstrate higher average success rates and lower cost of transport than the evaluated planner-controller baselines, with ablations confirming the contributions of cycle structure and frequency adaptation. Real-world experiments demonstrate zero-shot transfer from simulation to a passive-wheel Mini Cheetah across cluttered indoor spaces, slopes, and paved outdoor surfaces. Supplementary videos are available on the project page: this https URL

</details>


### [71] [PaceVLA: Adaptive Temporal Action Quantization in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25961)
*Hojin Jeon,Jimin Lee,Taekwan Kim,Nayoung Oh,Taeyoung Kim,Myungkyu Koo,Jinmo Koo,Joonwoo Ahn,Jinwoo Shin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PaceVLA 提出一种面向 VLA 的自适应时间执行框架，通过多粒度动作解码与模式路由，在保持任务成功率的同时提升机器人执行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型虽可作为通用机器人策略，但从遥操作演示中学习细粒度时序模式，执行时需大量低层动作步骤，导致时间效率低。直接进行时间动作量化会引入速度-精度权衡：粗粒度动作可减少冗余步骤，但在精度敏感交互中可能丢弃关键运动细节。

Method: PaceVLA 集成两个耦合组件：一是基于共享动作专家的模式专用动作解码器，支持多种时间粒度和预测视界；二是量化可行性引导的模式选择机制，用于判断粗粒度执行是否合适，并结合上下文条件路由器在每个推理步选择执行模式。
