Result: 在三个多任务仿真基准和六个真实世界任务上的实验表明，PaceVLA 在多种机器人 embodiment（包括长时程和灵巧操作）中提升了执行效率，同时保持任务成功率。

Conclusion: 自适应选择时间粒度可缓解粗粒度加速与精细控制之间的速度-精度权衡，为 VLA 策略的实用化部署提供高效且可靠的执行方案。

Abstract: Fast and reliable manipulation is crucial for practical robot deployment. Although Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have recently shown remarkable progress as generalist robot policies, their execution often remains temporally inefficient. A key source of this inefficiency is that policies learn fine-grained temporal patterns from teleoperated demonstrations, requiring many low-level action steps. Temporal action quantization offers a simple way to accelerate execution by aggregating consecutive low-level actions into temporally coarser actions. However, applying action quantization without considering when fine-grained control is required introduces a speed-accuracy trade-off: accelerated actions may efficiently reduce redundant steps in some phases, while discarding critical motion details in precision-sensitive interactions. To address this, we propose PaceVLA, a unified framework for adaptive temporal execution in VLAs that integrates two coupled components: (1) mode-specific action decoders built on a shared action expert, supporting multiple temporal granularities and prediction horizons, and (2) a quantizability-guided mode selection scheme which decides whether coarse execution is appropriate, and a context-conditioned router that selects an execution mode at each inference step. Experiments across three multi-task simulation benchmarks and six real-world tasks show that PaceVLA improves execution efficiency while maintaining task success rate across multiple embodiments, including long-horizon and dexterous manipulation.

</details>


### [72] [Policy Search in Code Space for Multi-Agent Pathfinding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25946)
*Matthias Eder,Pantelis Emmanouil,Vivek Farias,Fabian Fischer,Adam Jozefiak,Tianyi Peng,Thomas Ulz,Raphael Watschinger,Fangyu Wu,Andrew Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出以编码智能体在代码空间中进行策略搜索，将自动优化与显式可解释性结合，用于复杂多智能体协调策略设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂多智能体系统的协调策略传统上依赖高人力成本的算法设计，或使用不透明且任务特定的强化学习，缺乏兼顾自动化与可解释性的路径。

Method: 采用人类引导的工作流，让编码智能体基于仿真反馈和显式正确性验证，迭代地提出、实现、评估、诊断并改进算法；通过两个案例研究验证：2024 League of Robot Runners 竞赛地图和模拟真实仓库环境。

Result: 在竞赛挑战地图上，两天搜索得到的策略在最密集实例上超过本地复现的更强竞赛方案35.0%，并能泛化到未见地图实例；在具有不规则通道、连续机器人几何和异步运动的模拟仓库中，一周搜索发现一类新的多智能体路径规划算法，吞吐量超过校准的专有方案58.9%，并在给定假设下证明其无碰撞且能在有限时间内完成所有未被撤回或替换的已接受目标。

Conclusion: 由先进编码智能体驱动的代码空间策略搜索是构建实际工程解决方案的有竞争力路径，可同时实现高效性、可解释性和分析可处理性，并稳健优于人工设计解决方案。

Abstract: Developing coordination policies for complex multi-agent systems relies on either labor-intensive algorithm design or opaque task-specific reinforcement learning. With recent advances in autonomous coding agents, we show that policy search directly in code space offers a compelling third path that combines automated optimization with explicit interpretability. We investigate a human-directed workflow in which coding agents iteratively propose, implement, evaluate, diagnose, and eventually refine algorithms guided by simulation feedback and explicit correctness verification. We conducted two case studies and found that this workflow robustly outperformed any manually engineered solutions. On a challenging map in the 2024 League of Robot Runners competition, a two-day search discovered a policy that exceeds the stronger locally reproduced competition entry by 35.0% on the densest instance and generalizes to unseen map instances. In simulated real-world warehouses with irregular lanes, continuous robot geometry, and asynchronous motion, a week-long search discovered an entirely new class of multi-agent pathfinding algorithm that outperforms a calibrated proprietary solution in throughput by 58.9%. Under stated assumptions, we further prove that the new algorithm is collision-free and completes, in finite time, every accepted goal that is not withdrawn or replaced. Together, the studies show that policy search in code space powered by state-of-the-art coding agents offers a highly competitive path to building real engineering solutions that are both effective and analytically tractable.

</details>


### [73] [Vision-Language-Delta-Action: Learning Compact Deltas as Actionable Transition Interfaces for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25874)
*Lingsen Zhang,Wei Li,Jie He,Henghua Zhang,Kai Mo,Shiyong Li,Yanpeng Wang,Rui Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLDA将完整未来预测重构为紧凑的可操作状态转移建模，以Delta作为视觉语言理解与动作生成之间的接口，提升机器人操作的长期一致性、鲁棒性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA方法直接学习观测到动作的映射，语义理解与动作执行之间的差距带来较大学习负担；WAM虽通过未来预测改善控制，但建模完整未来状态会在静态外观和动作无关变化上浪费容量。

Method: 提出Vision-Language-Delta-Action（VLDA）范式，核心是捕获潜在世界状态变化的紧凑Delta变量。通过由Local Transition Filter（LT-Filter）和Global Transition Anchor（GT-Anchor）组成的统一目标学习Delta：LT-Filter提取任务相关、以操作为中心的变化并抑制静态重建偏差，GT-Anchor提供粗粒度未来级正则以保持状态变化整体方向。Delta-Driven Action Refinement（DDAR）进一步将Delta grounding到动作流形，并作为结构化种子注入以引导动作去噪。VLDA在LoongForge开源训练框架中实现，利用其模块化与分布式基础设施进行统一两阶段优化。

Result: 实验表明，VLDA提升了长时程一致性、鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 可操作的Delta可作为直接动作预测和完整未来状态建模的替代方案，验证了VLDA范式的有效性。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation is not merely observation-to-action generation, but the realization of task-specified state changes in the physical world. Existing vision-language-action (VLA) methods directly learn observation-to-action mappings, imposing a substantial learning burden due to the gap between semantic understanding and action execution. Although world-action models (WAMs) improve control through future prediction, modeling complete future states wastes capacity on static appearance and action-irrelevant changes. We propose Vision-Language-Delta-Action (VLDA), a paradigm that reformulates full future prediction as compact actionable transition modeling. Its core is a compact Delta variable that captures latent world-state changes, serving as an interface between vision-language understanding and action generation. VLDA learns Delta through a unified objective composed of the Local Transition Filter (LT-Filter) and Global Transition Anchor (GT-Anchor). LT-Filter captures task-relevant manipulation-centric changes while suppressing static reconstruction biases. GT-Anchor provides coarse future-level regularization to preserve the overall direction of state changes. Delta-Driven Action Refinement (DDAR) further grounds Delta in the action manifold and injects it as a structured seed to guide action denoising. We implement VLDA within LoongForge, an open-source training framework for large-scale foundation and embodied models, using its modular and distributed infrastructure for unified two-stage optimization. Experiments show that VLDA improves long-horizon consistency, robustness, and generalization, validating actionable Delta as an alternative to direct action prediction and full future-state modeling.

</details>


### [74] [AeroIntent: A Synchronized Multimodal Benchmark for UAV Mission Intent Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25869)
*Yutong Chen,Jingxin Mao,Zhiwei Wei,Bing Li,Jianzhong Ni,Rongqing Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出多模态无人机任务意图识别基准AeroIntent与结构化推理模型AeroMIR，并在未见轨迹程序等严格协议下验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机多模态数据集主要面向检测、跟踪和定位，缺乏对跨未见过轨迹程序及雷达历史不完整条件下任务级意图识别的评估支持。

Method: 构建AeroIntent基准，包含22个轨迹程序、5类任务意图的990条同步多模态序列，提供雷达、RGB、红外和深度观测及程序不相交、雷达缺失、观测前缀三类评估协议；提出AeroMIR，融合雷达轨迹、派生行为特征、精选视觉证据与任务知识进行候选式意图识别。

Result: 在严格程序不相交协议下，AeroMIR三个随机种子的平均准确率为71.56%、平均Macro-F1为69.84%，较最强纯轨迹基线提升7.79个Macro-F1点，较传统雷达-RGB后融合基线提升19.32点；在受控雷达缺失和早期意图识别设置下也表现良好。

Conclusion: AeroIntent与AeroMIR为多模态无人机意图识别提供了可复现的基准与方法基础。

Abstract: Reliable recognition of mission-level UAV intent from multimodal observations is essential for low-altitude airspace surveillance and security. Existing multimodal UAV datasets primarily support detection, tracking, and localization, leaving limited support for evaluating mission-intent recognition across held-out trajectory programs and incomplete radar histories. We introduce AeroIntent, a simulation-based benchmark comprising 990 synchronized multimodal sequences generated by 22 trajectory programs across five mission intents. The benchmark provides radar, RGB, infrared, and depth observations, together with program-disjoint, radar-gap, and observation-prefix evaluation protocols. Building on this benchmark, we propose AeroMIR, a structured Multimodal Intent Reasoner that integrates radar trajectories, derived behavioral features, selected visual evidence, and task knowledge for candidate-based UAV intent recognition. Under a strict program-disjoint protocol evaluating unseen tactical realizations, AeroMIR achieves a mean accuracy of 71.56% and a mean Macro-F1 of 69.84% across three seeds, outperforming the strongest trajectory-only baseline by 7.79 Macro-F1 points and a conventional radar-RGB late-fusion baseline by 19.32 points. AeroMIR also demonstrates strong performance under controlled radar gaps and early intent recognition settings. AeroIntent and AeroMIR provide a reproducible foundation for multimodal UAV intent recognition. The benchmark is available at: this https URL

</details>


### [75] [OV-PEAR: Observation-Verified Pose-Scale Estimation and Reconstruction for Unknown Industrial Manipulation Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25840)
*Guoqin Tang,Qingxuan Jia,Yichen Tan,Zihang Lv,Ruiquan Wang,Zeyuan Huang,Gang Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OV-PEAR 是一个面向未知工业物体的观测验证式三维重建与位姿-尺度估计框架，可在无需 CAD 或物体专属训练数据的条件下支持精细机器人操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精细工业操作需要未知物体的 6D 位姿、尺度及可操作三维模型；现有工业位姿估计依赖 CAD、物体专属训练数据或稳定工作单元，零样本方法又受单视角遮挡、生成偏差和尺度不确定性问题限制。

Method: 提出三阶段以物体为中心的流程：1）分割 RGB-D 实例并生成物体级三维假设；2）通过外观感知视角检索、显式尺度搜索和可见性敏感配准将假设锚定到机器人坐标系；3）将多视角 RGB-D 证据反投影到物体规范坐标系，验证体素占用、抑制幻觉并生成可复用的可信物体模型。

Result: 在 BOP-Industrial 上取得 0.657 平均 AR，并提升真实场景中的位姿与体素级几何一致性；真实机器人连接器装配达到 99.67% 抓取成功率和 84.00% 装配成功率；模型库与位姿-尺度日志支持 0.02 ms/帧的短期物体级状态维护。

Conclusion: OV-PEAR 通过观测验证和多视角证据融合，实现未知工业物体的可信重建、位姿-尺度估计与可复用模型生成，并在真实装配任务中验证了有效性与高效性。

Abstract: Fine industrial robotic manipulation requires 6D pose, scale, and an operation-ready 3D model. Existing industrial pose estimation relies on CAD models, object-specific training data, or stable workcells, limiting use with unseen objects; zero-shot methods suffer from single-view occlusion, generative bias, and scale uncertainty. We present OV-PEAR, an observation-verified framework for 3D reconstruction and pose-scale estimation of unknown industrial objects. Its three-stage object-centric pipeline: (1) segments RGB-D instances and generates object-level 3D hypotheses; (2) anchors them to the robot frame via appearance-aware view retrieval, explicit scale search, and visibility-sensitive registration; (3) back-projects multi-view RGB-D evidence into the object-canonical frame to verify voxel occupancy, suppress hallucinations, and produce reusable trusted object models. On BOP-Industrial, OV-PEAR achieves 0.657 average AR and improves pose and voxel-level geometric consistency in real scenes; real-robot connector assembly reaches 99.67% grasp and 84.00% assembly success. The model library and pose-scale log enable short-term object-level state maintenance at 0.02 ms/frame.

</details>


### [76] [Long-Horizon Multi-Object Rearrangement via Distilled Tree Search over Continuous Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25785)
*Kallol Saha,Yining Hong,Amber Li,Yejin Choi,Maxim Likhachev,David Held*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DISCO 将基于物理仿真的渐进扩宽 MCTS 与学习到的策略-价值模型结合，通过周期性蒸馏把连续动作空间中的长时程搜索能力迁移到可直接部署的策略中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程操作中，学习策略会累积误差且难以保证未来可行性；搜索规划能显式考虑未来后果，但在高维连续动作空间中代价高昂。核心问题是：如何把搜索得到的长时程推理迁移到连续动作空间策略中。

Method: DISCO 在物理仿真中使用渐进扩宽 MCTS 生成长时程计划；搜索时由学习策略提出连续动作，学习价值函数按任务完成进度的期望评分；利用 GPU 并行仿真，周期性地将 MCTS 生成计划蒸馏到策略-价值模型中，以改进后续搜索的动作提议和价值估计；测试时直接部署学习模型，无需基于仿真器的搜索。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界的重排任务中，DISCO 优于行为克隆、强化学习和基于搜索的基线方法。

Conclusion: DISCO 表明可通过搜索蒸馏将长时程搜索推理有效迁移到连续动作空间策略中，从而在部署时避免昂贵的在线搜索并提升长时程操作表现。

Abstract: Despite recent advances in policy learning, long-horizon manipulation remains difficult because learned policies must avoid compounding errors while preserving future feasibility. While search-based planning can explicitly reason over such future consequences, it quickly becomes expensive in high-dimensional continuous action spaces. This raises a central question: how can long-horizon reasoning from search be transferred into policies in continuous action spaces? To this end, we propose DISCO: Distilled Tree Search over Continuous Actions. DISCO uses progressive-widening MCTS in physics simulation to generate long-horizon plans. During search, a learned policy proposes continuous actions, while a learned value function scores actions by their expected progress toward task completion. Using GPU-parallelized simulation, DISCO periodically distills MCTS-generated plans into the policy-value model, improving the action proposals and value estimates used in subsequent searches. Finally, the learned policy-value model can be directly deployed without the need for simulator-based search at test time. Across simulated and real-world rearrangement tasks, DISCO outperforms behavior cloning, reinforcement learning, and search-based baselines.

</details>


### [77] [TrajWAM: An Efficient Trajectory-Guided World Action Model for Dynamic Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25763)
*Haohan Geng,Sha Zhang,Weishuo Sun,Guanting Ye,Wenhao Yu,Yuang Xie,Qicheng Zeng,Peng Ye,Jiajun Deng,Jianmin Ji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TrajWAM 是一种轨迹引导的世界动作模型，用目标物体未来轨迹显式约束未来视觉想象，并据此生成动作；通过解耦蒸馏实现高效单步视频-动作推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态物体操作要求机器人预测目标运动并及时重规划，避免执行过时动作。现有方法要么仅做快速反应控制而缺乏显式未来目标状态，要么隐式编码预期运动；世界动作模型虽能联合未来视觉预测与动作生成，但场景级视频目标可能弱化目标动力学，且迭代视频-动作去噪带来显著延迟。

Method: 给定短历史物体掩码，历史条件运动预测器预测编码目标轨迹的未来掩码；轻量 Trajectory ControlNet 引导预训练视频模型生成轨迹一致未来；未来表示条件化动作专家预测可执行动作块。为实现不破坏该接口的单步推理，提出解耦蒸馏：视频骨干分布匹配、Trajectory ControlNet 重对齐、动作 rollout 蒸馏，支持基于压缩未来表示的准确单步动作预测。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界的综合评估中，TrajWAM 在动态物体操作任务上取得最先进性能，并实现高效的单步视频-动作推理。

Conclusion: 显式将未来视觉想象锚定到预测的目标物体运动，并在加速后保留该预期通路，是提升动态机器人操作性能与推理效率的有效方案。

Abstract: Dynamic object manipulation requires robots to anticipate target motion while replanning fast enough to avoid executing outdated actions. Existing approaches either rely on fast reactive control without an explicit future target state or encode anticipated motion only implicitly. World Action Models (WAMs) offer a promising alternative by coupling future visual prediction with action generation, but scene-level video objectives may underrepresent target dynamics that are critical for manipulation, while iterative video-action denoising introduces substantial latency. We present TrajWAM, a trajectory-guided world action model for efficient dynamic robotic manipulation. Our key insight is to explicitly ground future visual imagination in predicted target-object motion and use the resulting future representation to condition action generation, while preserving this anticipation pathway after acceleration. Given a short history of object masks, a history-conditioned motion predictor forecasts future masks encoding the target object's trajectory. A lightweight Trajectory ControlNet then steers a pretrained video model toward trajectory-consistent futures. The resulting future representation conditions an action expert to predict executable action chunks. To enable one-step inference without breaking this interface, we introduce a decoupled distillation scheme that combines distribution matching for the video backbone, Trajectory ControlNet realignment, and action rollout distillation to support accurate one-step action prediction conditioned on the compressed future representation. Comprehensive evaluations in simulation and the real world show that TrajWAM achieves state-of-the-art performance on dynamic object manipulation tasks while enabling efficient one-step video-action inference. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [78] [Learning from Fleet Experience: A Collective Learning Paradigm for Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25736)
*Chao Yu,Shu'ang Yu,Hao Lin,Hongzhi Zang,Yuxiang Yan,Chenxi Jiang,Zhen Guo,Chuankang Li,Yihao Chen,Xin Xu,Xu Fu,Jiakai Zhou,Liangzhi Shi,Hongyi Ren,Yiwei Liu,Jing Wu,Yuanqing Wang,Chen Wu,Bo Dai,Chunyu Yang,Hongliang Huang,Shuoyu Yue,Weiqi Yin,Yinuo Chen,Ruihua Chen,Chunyang Zhu,Han Xiao,Yanghao Li,Ruipu Wu,Zhikai Cai,Xiu Chen,Xu Tan,Yuchen Yan,Jialin Zhang,Wen Zeng,Yiming Zhou,Zixiao Huang,Guanghui Ren,Quanlu Zhang,Boxun Li,Maoqing Yao,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出集体经验学习（CEL）及其实例FERT/FATS，让部署中的机器人共享执行经验，在不额外收集专家示范或纠正动作的情况下提升公共策略可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人部署执行经验可用于改进策略，但依赖人类纠正需要操作员持续监控并在失败时干预，随着机器人集群扩大难以满足。

Method: 提出CEL范式，使已部署机器人共享执行经验以改进公共策略；通过FERT利用机器人执行中的失败上下文，从集群共享成功经验中检索相关轨迹片段，并以这些动作作为监督进行策略更新；用FATS容错异步训练系统协调经验收集、策略优化和策略分发，以应对集群局部中断。

Result: 在五个真实操作任务中，策略成功率达93.3%–99.2%，每任务在未参与训练数据收集的机器人上评估120次；固定每机器人收集时间时，更大集群提供更多经验且FERT总体成功率更高；在多阶段手机包装任务中，96台机器人学习使成功率从79.2%提升至98.3%。

Conclusion: 集体经验可作为可扩展机器人学习的实用基础，并能在部署期间无需额外专家纠正动作的情况下提升策略可靠性。

Abstract: Experience generated during deployment offers an opportunity for robots to improve policy reliability through learning from their own executions. However, learning through human corrections requires teleoperators to monitor execution and intervene when policies fail, a demand that is difficult to meet as robot fleets grow. We introduce collective experience learning (CEL), a learning paradigm in which deployed robots share their execution experience to improve a common policy. We realize CEL through failure-guided experience retrieval fine-tuning (FERT), which uses failure contexts from robot executions to retrieve relevant trajectory segments from the fleet's shared successful experience and uses their actions as supervision for policy updates, without collecting additional expert demonstrations or corrective actions. A fault-tolerant asynchronous training system (FATS) coordinates experience collection, policy optimization and policy distribution despite local interruptions within the robot fleet. Across five real-world manipulation tasks, the resulting policies achieve success rates of 93.3-99.2%, evaluated over 120 trials per task on robots excluded from training data collection. With collection time per robot held fixed, larger fleets provide more experience and FERT generally achieves higher success rates across the evaluated sizes. On multi-stage phone packaging, learning with 96 robots increases success from 79.2% to 98.3%. These results support collective experience as a practical basis for scalable robot learning and a path towards improving policy reliability during deployment without collecting additional expert corrective actions.

</details>


### [79] [Enhancing Lunar PNT Performance Through Surface Station Enabled Radiometric Observables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25720)
*Toshiki Tanaka,Satoshi Ikari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种由月面站辅助的辐射测量导航架构，通过双差卫星观测与月面站—巡视器测距提升月面巡视器相对定位精度，尤其改善垂直方向定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来月面任务需要自主巡视器具备可靠定位能力；仅依赖月球导航卫星时，受卫星可用性和观测几何限制，垂直定位精度可能不足，早期部署阶段尤为明显。

Method: 构建月面站（SS）与巡视器之间的双差伪距和Doppler相对定位，并引入双单向测距（DOWR）作为附加距离观测，再与巡视器航位推算信息融合；采用5颗代表性LCRNS星座卫星和月球南极Connecting Ridge附近巡视器轨迹进行Monte Carlo仿真，并提出DOWR硬件偏差估计方法。

Result: 仿真中，99.7%垂直定位误差均值在无月面站时为29.9 m，采用双差伪距和Doppler后降至5.5 m，加入DOWR后降至4.8 m；未校正DOWR硬件偏差会显著恶化定位，而所提偏差估计方法的最终估计误差为0.058 ± 0.057 m；DOWR收益还取决于观测几何。

Conclusion: 月面站可通过直接辐射测量测距改善巡视器垂直相对定位，补充其通信与导航支持作用，展现出提升月面自主导航定位性能的潜力。

Abstract: Future lunar surface missions require reliable positioning for autonomous rover operations. Lunar navigation satellite systems may provide limited vertical positioning accuracy due to satellite availability and observation geometry, particularly during early deployment. This study investigates a radiometric navigation architecture in which a lunar surface station (SS) improves rover positioning through double-differenced satellite observations and additional surface-to-surface ranging. Pseudorange and Doppler measurements from lunar navigation satellites are used for relative positioning between the SS and rover, while dual one-way ranging (DOWR) provides an additional range observable. The radiometric solution is integrated with rover dead-reckoning information. Monte Carlo simulations were conducted using five satellites representative of NASA's Lunar Communications Relay and Navigation Systems (LCRNS) constellation and a rover trajectory near Connecting Ridge at the lunar south pole. The results show that the SS improves horizontal and vertical relative positioning, with further gains from DOWR. In the simulated scenario, the mean 99.7% vertical positioning error decreased from 29.9 m without the SS to 5.5 m with double-differenced pseudorange and Doppler processing, and to 4.8 m with DOWR. An uncorrected DOWR hardware bias significantly degraded positioning, whereas the proposed bias-estimation method achieved a final estimation error of 0.058 +/- 0.057 m. The benefit of DOWR also depended on observation geometry. These results demonstrate the potential of a lunar surface station to improve vertical relative positioning through direct radiometric ranging, complementing its communication and navigation support roles.

</details>


### [80] [SWAP: Test-Time Steerable Humanoid Whole-Body Collision Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25711)
*Zeji Yi,Guanqi He,Xiaofeng Guo,Guannan Qu,Pieter Abbeel,Yan Duan,Karen Liu,Guanya Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SWAP分层框架，将人形机器人全身避碰决策与动态平衡解耦：高层扩散策略生成多模态避碰运动提案，低层全身RL控制器跟踪并保持平衡，并支持测试时可控引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在狭窄杂乱的人类环境中需全身协调避碰并保持动态稳定；现有方法难以同时处理高维感知输入下的动态稳定性与碰撞避免，且单一确定性策略难以表达同一障碍下的多种安全通行方式。

Method: SWAP采用分层架构：高层为感知条件扩散策略，生成多模态、碰撞感知的运动提案；低层为全身强化学习控制器，跟踪提案并维持动态平衡。训练高层策略时使用 hindsight labeling 获取可扩展监督：在随机指令下滚动低层策略，将障碍放置于已执行运动未扫过的空间，从而构造无碰撞运动并重渲染机器人感知。部署时通过蒙特卡洛采样与简单代价信号引导扩散模型，实现安全且任务对齐的行为，无需大量奖励调参。

Result: 在仿真和真实Unitree G1人形机器人上验证，可对静态与动态障碍实现可靠避碰；测试时引导支持定制化，在保持高避碰率的同时，使速度、朝向或姿态等目标奖励平均提升34%。

Conclusion: SWAP为人形机器人全身避碰提供了一种实用方案，兼顾动态平衡、多模态安全通行与测试时可引导性，并展示了仿真到真实硬件的可行性。

Abstract: Humanoid robots operating in human-centric environments must coordinate the entire body to traverse tight, cluttered spaces while maintaining balance. Existing methods often struggle to jointly consider dynamic stability and collision avoidance from high-dimensional perceptual inputs. Moreover, for humanoids, safe behavior is inherently multimodal: the same obstacle may admit multiple valid traversals, which is difficult to represent with a single deterministic policy. To address these challenges, we propose SWAP (Steerable Whole-body Avoidance Policy), a hierarchical framework that separates collision-aware decision making from stabilization. A high-level, perception-conditioned diffusion policy generates multimodal, collision-aware motion proposals, while a low-level whole-body RL controller tracks these proposals and maintains dynamic balance. To efficiently train the high-level policy, we obtain scalable supervision via hindsight labeling: we roll out the low-level policy under randomized commands, place obstacles only in the space that each executed motion did not sweep, so the motion is collision-free by construction, and re-render the perception the robot would have seen. At deployment, Monte Carlo sampling with simple cost signals steers the diffusion model toward safe, task-aligned behaviors without heavy reward tuning. Experiments in simulation and on a real Unitree G1 humanoid show reliable avoidance with static and dynamic obstacles. Test-time steering further enables customization, improving targeted rewards (e.g., speed, orientation, or posture) by 34% on average while maintaining a high collision avoidance rate. Together, these results support a practical solution to humanoid whole-body collision avoidance with test-time steerability. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [81] [Validation of Human Motor Development Scales with Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25701)
*Fanxing Meng,Jing Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究将人类婴儿运动发育量表转化为发育图，并嵌入强化学习框架，使仿人机器人按发育阶段学习站立与行走等粗大运动技能，并评估量表作为自包含训练指南的完备性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索哪些婴儿运动发育量表足够全面，能够作为仿人机器人从基础动作发展到站立和行走的完整训练指南。

Method: 将每个运动发育量表表示为指导训练的发育图，并在强化学习框架中按量表描述的人类运动发育自然阶段训练仿人机器人。

Result: 四种常用儿科运动量表中有三种可成功指导机器人发展目标技能；第四种因缺少部分中间里程碑而导致失败，补入缺失里程碑后也能成功。

Conclusion: 基于发育量表的发育图可有效组织强化学习训练；量表的完备性关键取决于是否包含必要的中间里程碑，缺失项可通过补充得到修正。

Abstract: We investigate how humanoid robots can learn a series of motor skills defined by human infant motor development scales, and we evaluate which scales are comprehensive enough to serve as self-contained guides for developing gross motor skills up to standing and walking. We represent each motor development scale as a developmental graph that guides the training of a humanoid robot. We then apply this graph within a reinforcement learning framework so that the robot learns skills following the natural phases of human motor development described by the scale. Our work demonstrates that graphs derived from three out of four popular pediatric motor scales can successfully guide humanoid robots to develop these skills. The fourth scale misses some intermediate milestones that the other scales have, which causes some failures. After adding a missing milestone in its graph, it also enables the robot to develop those skills successfully.

</details>


### [82] [ReDOA: Real-World Distillation via Observation Alignment for Image-Based Manipulation Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25691)
*Kotaro Kajitsuka,Masaki Murooka,Fumio Kanehiro*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 ReDOA 框架，将控制学习与感知学习解耦：先在仿真中训练状态教师策略，再在真实机器人上执行并收集轨迹，通过观察对齐将控制知识蒸馏为基于图像的学生策略，从而无需人类演示即可学习真实世界图像策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人操作的数据驱动学习面临数据采集成本与真实环境适应性之间的权衡：模仿学习依赖昂贵的人类演示，而仿真学习存在动力学和传感器观测上的 sim-to-real 差距。

Method: ReDOA 将控制学习与感知学习分离。状态教师策略在仿真中训练后部署到真实机器人执行，生成成功且高效执行的轨迹及真实视觉观测；随后利用这些轨迹将教师控制知识蒸馏到图像学生策略中。观察对齐用于解决数据采集与部署阶段的观测不匹配问题。

Result: 在三个非抓取式操作任务（包括双臂操作）的真实机器人实验中，综合考虑任务成功率和执行效率，ReDOA 的整体性能优于基于人类演示的模仿学习和仿真中的策略蒸馏。

Conclusion: ReDOA 能够在不需要人类专家演示的情况下，以较低成本学习真实世界图像策略，并有效缓解仿真到真实的观测与动力学差距，在真实机器人操作任务中取得更好的综合表现。

Abstract: Data-driven learning for general-purpose robotic manipulation presents a fundamental trade-off between data-collection cost and adaptability to real-world environments. Imitation learning (IL) requires costly human demonstrations, whereas simulation-based learning is vulnerable to sim-to-real gaps in dynamics and sensor observations. We propose ReDOA (Real-World Distillation via Observation Alignment), a framework that decouples control learning from perception learning to learn image-based policies in the real world. A state-based teacher policy trained in simulation is executed on a real robot, and its control knowledge is distilled into an image-based student policy using the collected trajectories. Observation Alignment resolves observation mismatches between data collection and deployment. This framework collects successful, efficiently executed trajectories paired with real-world visual observations, without requiring demonstrations from human experts. In real-robot experiments on three non-prehensile manipulation tasks, including bimanual manipulation, our method achieves better overall performance when jointly considering task success and execution efficiency than both IL based on human demonstrations and policy distillation in simulation. this https URL

</details>


### [83] [ScaleNav: Scalable Internet Video Pretraining for Language-Conditioned Local Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25690)
*Yihui Yang,Yuanhong Yu,Jiawei Wang,Zhongrui Yu,Jia Shi,Kun Zhan,Ruizhen Hu,Xiaowei Zhou,Yu Liu,Sida Peng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ScaleNav，一个基于互联网自我中心视频学习目标导向视觉动态、并用于机器人语言条件局部导航的世界-动作统一框架，采用三阶段训练并在仿真与真实四足机器人上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言条件局部导航需要机器人到达初始观测中文本指定的可见目标并停在其描述位置；端到端策略依赖多样训练数据，但真实机器人数据采集成本高，仿真数据存在sim-to-real差距。互联网自我中心视频可提供可扩展的真实世界导航经验，关键洞见是从中学习目标导向视觉动态以支持机器人动作生成。

Method: ScaleNav采用三阶段训练：首先在互联网自我中心视频上进行指令条件视频预训练，学习相机接近指令目标时观测如何在多样真实场景中演化；随后利用带动作标签的仿真数据进行动作对齐，训练模型预测可执行动作块；最后进行联合微调，将所有可训练组件共同优化用于导航。

Result: ScaleNav在Short-Horizon OVON单视图适配版本上达到86.5%成功率，在HabitatGS-LN基准上达到94.9%，在两个基准上均优于所评估基线；并部署于真实四足机器人，结果支持其泛化到真实世界导航的能力。

Conclusion: 从互联网自我中心视频学习目标导向视觉动态，并结合仿真动作对齐与联合微调，可有效提升语言条件局部导航的性能与真实机器人泛化能力，为可扩展的真实世界导航经验利用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: We study language-conditioned local navigation, where a robot must navigate to a text-specified target visible in its initial observation and stop at the described location. End-to-end navigation policies require diverse training data to generalize across scenes, yet real-robot data is costly to collect and simulated data leaves a sim-to-real gap. Internet egocentric videos offer a scalable source of real-world navigation experience. Our key insight is to learn goal-directed visual dynamics from Internet egocentric videos, and use these dynamics to support robot action generation. To this end, we propose ScaleNav, a unified world-action framework with three-stage training. We first perform instruction-conditioned video pretraining on these videos to learn how observations evolve as the camera approaches instructed targets across diverse real-world scenes. Action alignment then uses action-labeled simulated data to train the model to predict executable action chunks. Joint fine-tuning subsequently optimizes all trainable components together for navigation. ScaleNav achieves success rates of 86.5% on a single-view adaptation of Short-Horizon OVON and 94.9% on our HabitatGS-LN benchmark, outperforming the evaluated baselines on both benchmarks. We further deploy and evaluate ScaleNav on a real quadruped robot, with results supporting the model's ability to generalize to real-world navigation. Code and videos are available at this https URL

</details>


### [84] [Vehicle-State-Aware Confidence Estimation for Direct Wind Sensing on a Multirotor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25688)
*Sri Arjun Senthil Kumar,April Zi Qian Chan,Chen-Hua Yeow*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于飞行器状态（水平与垂直速度）的置信度方法，用于量化多旋翼无人机直接测风观测的可靠性，并据此自适应调节二维风速矢量估计器中各观测的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多旋翼无人机直接测风可实现局部大气测量，但风速计可靠性受飞行器运动影响；需要区分并抑制运动过程中不可靠的风观测。

Method: 利用水平速度和垂直速度两个特征构建车辆状态感知的置信度分数，并将该分数用于调整二维矢量估计器中每次风观测的权重；在水平平移、垂直运动和留出自主航点飞行中验证。

Result: 两特征方法达到ROC-AUC 0.995，识别出98.84%的平移观测为不可靠；置信度自适应使平移期间估计器中位增益从0.207降至0.064，同时悬停期间中位增益保持0.151。

Conclusion: 飞行器状态提供了有用的本体感知上下文，可用于调节直接机载风观测的影响，从而提高多旋翼无人机测风的可靠性。

Abstract: Direct wind sensing from a multi-rotor UAV enables localized atmospheric measurements, but the reliability of the anemometer depends on vehicle motion. This work presents a vehicle-state-aware confidence method that uses horizontal and vertical vehicle speed to quantify the acquisition of each wind observation. The resulting confidence score is used to adapt measurement influence to a two-dimensional vector estimator. Experiments were conducted during horizontal translation, vertical motion, and held-out autonomous waypoint flight. The proposed two-feature formulation achieved an ROC-AUC of 0.995, with 98.84% of translation observations identified as unreliable. The confidence-aware adaptation reduced median estimator gain during translation from 0.207 to 0.064 while retaining median gain of 0.151 during hover. The results show that vehicle state provides useful proprioceptive context for regulating the influence of direct airborne wind observations.

</details>


### [85] [Revealing and Bridging the Physical Executability Gap in Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25687)
*Gang He,Zhile Cheng,Kepeng Xu,Zhenyang Liu,Yu-Wing Tai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文指出连续VLN中仅用到达目标评估会掩盖物理可执行性差距，提出VeSTA通过对齐视觉-语义可通行性，使策略倾向语义有效、几何可行且物理鲁棒的未来轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前仿真器的宽松接触模型可能掩盖物理不可行动作，导致语义能力与具身实现之间存在物理可执行性差距；仅以目标到达成功率评估无法反映该问题。

Method: VeSTA包含两个机制：Trajectory Relative Group Policy Optimization利用严格接触反馈将候选轨迹分布重塑到可执行区域；Traversability Risk Critic学习风险调整的标量效用，用于多模态未来候选的风险感知选择。

Result: 在R2R和RxR上，现有VLN智能体显著依赖仿真器接触语义；严格接触评估下，VeSTA大幅减少碰撞导致的失败，同时保持或提升目标到达性能，控制真实机器人实验表明学到的偏好可迁移到仿真之外。

Conclusion: VeSTA通过显式对齐可执行未来，弥合VLN中语义 competence 与物理具身实现之间的差距，并提升严格接触条件下的鲁棒性与真实迁移性。

Abstract: Continuous vision-and-language navigation (VLN) is typically evaluated by goal-reaching success, assuming it reflects an agent's ability to execute language-grounded behavior. We show that this assumption can fail: permissive contact models in current simulators may mask physically infeasible actions, creating a physical executability gap between semantic competence and embodied realization. To bridge this gap, we introduce Visual-Semantic Traversability Alignment (VeSTA), a framework that aligns VLN policies with physically executable futures. We define visual-semantic traversability as the ability to predict instruction-conditioned trajectories that are semantically valid, geometrically feasible, and physically robust. VeSTA addresses this misalignment through two complementary mechanisms: improving executable support in trajectory generation and risk-aware selection among multimodal futures. Specifically, Trajectory Relative Group Policy Optimization uses strict-contact feedback to reshape the candidate distribution toward executable regions, while the Traversability Risk Critic learns a risk-adjusted scalar utility for candidate-wise future selection. Experiments on R2R and RxR reveal substantial dependence of existing VLN agents on simulator contact semantics. Under strict-contact evaluation, VeSTA substantially reduces collision-induced failures while preserving or improving goal-reaching performance, and controlled real-world robot experiments provide evidence that the learned preference transfers beyond simulation.

</details>


### [86] [SpaRV: Sparse Radiance Voxels for Scalable Geometrically Consistent LiDAR-Visual Mapping and Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25665)
*Jianheng Liu,Yunfei Wan,Fangming Cheng,Xiangyu Hong,Zuhao Zou,Haotian Li,Longji Yin,Chunran Zheng,Jiarong Lin,Fu Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SpaRV 是一种稀疏辐射体素地图，通过可微光线步进渲染，并利用 LiDAR 扫描监督和相机参数优化，实现大规模照片级且几何一致的重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人仿真、导航和巡检需要照片级且几何准确的地图。机器人数据构建辐射场面临三大挑战：轨迹服务于任务导致视角少、几何欠约束；相机位姿和内参残差使地图模糊；GPU 内存随场景规模增长限制大场景细节。

Method: 提出 SpaRV：稀疏辐射体素地图，由可微光线步进渲染，使不规则 LiDAR 扫描与图像共同监督；用 LiDAR 深度和占据锚定欠约束几何，并以法线和平滑正则保持 LiDAR 点间平滑；交替进行地图训练与相机位姿、焦距的光度优化；训练和渲染时一次仅保留一个全分辨率子图，其余采用八叉树粗层级以限制 GPU 内存。

Result: 实验表明 SpaRV 在远离采集轨迹处保持几何一致，并通过优化相机参数锐化纹理；在单张 24 GB GPU 上重建由 63,582 张图像捕获的大学校园，并可模拟其他相机和 LiDAR。

Conclusion: SpaRV 能利用机器人数据构建大规模照片级、几何准确的地图，支持仿真、导航和巡检等应用，代码将开源。

Abstract: Robotic simulation, navigation, and inspection need photorealistic and geometrically accurate maps. Radiance fields achieve photorealism, but building them from robot data raises three challenges. A robot's trajectory serves its task, not reconstruction, so images from few viewpoints leave the geometry under-constrained. Residual errors in camera poses and intrinsics blur the map. The map's GPU memory grows with scene size, which limits its level of detail in large scenes. We present \textbf{SpaRV}, a \textbf{Spa}rse \textbf{R}adiance \textbf{V}oxel map rendered by differentiable ray marching, which lets irregular LiDAR scans supervise it alongside images. LiDAR depth and occupancy anchor the under-constrained geometry, and normal and smoothness regularizers keep it smooth between LiDAR points. To correct camera errors, SpaRV alternates map training with photometric optimization of the camera poses and focal lengths. To bound GPU memory in training and rendering, SpaRV holds one submap at a time at full resolution and the rest at coarser octree levels. Experiments show that SpaRV keeps the geometry consistent away from the captured trajectory and sharpens textures by optimizing the camera parameters. On one 24\,GB GPU, SpaRV reconstructs a university campus captured in 63{,}582 images and simulates other cameras and LiDARs. Our code will be released at \url{ this https URL }.

</details>


### [87] [InteractAgent: Agentic Human Motion Interaction with Memory-Augmented Reflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25649)
*Tao Zhang,Senhe Zhang,Zeyu Zhang,Yujia Zhang,Dong Gong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: InteractAgent 是一个面向长时程人-场景交互的智能体式多模态 LLM 系统，以单个 MLLM 作为规划器与评估器、冻结 MotionLLM 作为执行器，通过闭环“规划-执行-观察-反思”提升任务成功率并降低穿透。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本驱动运动模型大多与场景无关且缺乏闭环纠错；几何条件方法依赖配对场景-运动训练或显式 3D 预处理，难以泛化到新布局和开放词汇目标。长时程交互还需协同空间推理、物理可行性及复合执行误差恢复。

Method: 系统采用单一 MLLM 同时承担规划与评估，冻结 MotionLLM 作为执行器；集成 Online Observation 做逐步恢复决策（continue/redo/replan），并用 Memory-Augmented Reflection 进行多轮计划细化与自适应停止。

Result: 在 UniHSI 上，与无训练基线相比，InteractAgent 提高了任务成功率并减少了穿透。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，基于智能体式多模态 LLM 的闭环规划、观察与反思机制可有效支持长时程人-场景交互，并在无需配对场景-运动训练或显式 3D 预处理的条件下改善执行鲁棒性与物理可行性。

Abstract: Long-horizon human-scene interaction requires coordinated spatial reasoning, physical feasibility, and the ability to recover from compounding execution errors. Text-driven motion models are largely scene-agnostic and lack closed-loop correction, while geometry-conditioned approaches depend on paired scene-motion training or explicit 3D preprocessing that limits generalization to novel layouts and open-vocabulary goals. We present InteractAgent, an agentic multimodal LLM system for long-horizon human-scene interaction that plans, executes, observes, and reflects in a closed loop, using a single MLLM as both planner and evaluator and a frozen MotionLLM as the executor. InteractAgent integrates Online Observation for step-wise recovery decisions (continue/redo/replan) and Memory-Augmented Reflection for multi-round plan refinement with adaptive stopping. On UniHSI, InteractAgent improves task success and reduces penetration compared with training-free baselines. Code: this https URL .

</details>


### [88] [SteerAct: Unlocking Action Models through Few-Shot Temporal Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25632)
*Kaichen Zhou,Zhiyan Li,Chongkai Gao,Yuhan Wang,Wenlong Huang,Maggie Wang,Fangneng Zhan,Manling Li,Qianqian Wang,Mac Schwager,Zhuang Liu,Li Fei-Fei,Mengyu Wang,Paul Pu Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SteerAct 是一个少样本时序激活引导框架，仅用少量带结果标签的 rollout，在测试时通过稀疏自编码器特征选择与在线时序描述符控制干预，提升预训练机器人动作模型成功率且不更新模型权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时仅凭少量 rollout 改进预训练机器人动作模型仍然困难；激活引导虽轻量，但需确定修改哪些特征以及干预时机和强度。

Method: 使用预训练稀疏自编码器提供候选特征，并构建总结局部激活顺序模式分布的时间描述符，实现无需时间步对齐的 rollout 比较；离线阶段用成功与失败参考 rollout 选择最能区分结果的任务特定特征并建立参考库；在线执行时将特征演化描述符与参考比较，决定何时干预及通过引导系数增强或抑制其激活。

Result: 每个任务仅用 24 条带结果标签的 rollout，在 π0.5、Fast-WAM、RoboTwin 2.0、DexJoCo 和真实世界操作评估中，相比未引导模型任务成功率约提升 10%，且不更新动作模型权重。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于稀疏特征时序动态的少样本激活引导能够轻量有效地提升预训练机器人动作模型的测试时性能。

Abstract: Improving pretrained robot action models at test time from only a few rollouts remains challenging. Activation steering offers a lightweight approach by modifying feature activations while keeping model weights fixed. However, effective steering requires identifying which features to modify and determining the timing and strength of modification. We introduce SteerAct, a few-shot temporal steering framework that uses a shared temporal descriptor of sparse-feature dynamics for offline feature selection and online control. A pretrained sparse autoencoder provides candidate features, and our temporal descriptor summarizes the distribution of local activation-order patterns for each feature, enabling comparisons across rollouts without requiring timestep alignment. Before steering begins (offline), we use successful and failed reference rollouts to select a single task-specific feature whose temporal descriptors best distinguish the two outcomes and construct its reference banks. During each new execution (online), we compare the feature's evolving temporal descriptor with these references to determine when to intervene and how strongly to increase or decrease its activation via a steering coefficient. Using only 24 outcome-labeled rollouts per task, SteerAct improves task success by approximately 10% relative to unsteered models in evaluations spanning $\pi_{0.5}$, Fast-WAM, RoboTwin 2.0, DexJoCo, and real-world manipulation, without updating action-model weights. Code and demos are available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [89] [Humanoid SRL: Augmenting Humanoids with Supernumerary Limbs for Coordinated Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25628)
*Fukang Liu,Feiyang Wu,Paul Barsa,Jaehwi Jang,Akshat Jain,Zhigen Zhao,Yipu Chen,Zhaoyuan Gu,Nikhil Vemuri,Anshul Chandaliya,Kaijie Zhu,Sean Lublinsky,Matthew Zhu,Tony G. Chen,Ye Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究将超数机械臂集成到人形机器人上，探索形态增强如何扩展其可行动作空间，并实现接触丰富的三臂移动操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人的双臂形态在日常任务中具有优势，但当双臂被占用或需要超出原生肢体范围的额外接触时，会限制移动操作能力；超数机械臂有望突破这些形态约束，但需与全身运动和平衡协调。

Method: 研究在两种配置下进行定量分析和硬件实验，将超数手臂集成到人形平台；采用基于模型的轨迹优化生成动态可行运动，为目标条件强化学习控制器提供训练数据，控制器在仿真中训练并零样本迁移到硬件；同时利用硬件演示训练的扩散策略实现部分任务的视觉引导自主执行。

Result: 额外肢体扩展了全身交互能力，使操作与支撑能够协调进行，并超越原生双臂配置的限制；系统实现了接触丰富的三臂移动操作中的协调全身技能，并在选定任务上展示了视觉引导的自主执行。

Conclusion: 人形机器人形态不必局限于类人结构，而可被视为可扩展的设计空间，通过超数肢体实现机器人能力增强。

Abstract: Humanoid robots are envisioned to perform diverse human-level tasks through their anthropomorphic morphology. Their dual-arm configuration enables a broad range of everyday actions. Yet, as in humans, this morphology can constrain loco-manipulation when both arms are occupied or additional contacts are required beyond their reach. Supernumerary robotic limbs (SRLs) offer a way to extend these capabilities, but their integration requires the added limb to be coordinated with whole-body motion and balance. In this study, we develop and integrate a supernumerary arm into a humanoid platform to investigate how morphological augmentation reshapes its feasible action space. Through quantitative analysis and hardware experiments across two configurations, we show how the additional limb expands whole-body interaction capabilities by enabling manipulation and support to be coordinated beyond the constraints of the native dual-arm configuration. To control this augmented morphology, we leverage model-based trajectory optimization (TO) to generate dynamically feasible motions for goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (RL) controllers. The controllers are trained in simulation and transferred to hardware in a zero-shot manner, enabling coordinated whole-body skills for contact-rich tri-arm loco-manipulation. We further show vision-guided autonomous execution on selected tasks using diffusion policies trained from hardware demonstrations. Our findings suggest that humanoid morphology need not be restricted to human-like forms, but can be reimagined as an extensible design space for robotic augmentation.

</details>


### [90] [Vector-Quantized World Model: Latent Feedback Motion Planning for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25600)
*Xuezhou Xu,Chung-Pang Wang,Zhenzhen Li,Benjamin Riviere*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VQ-WM：用离散向量量化世界模型做长时程搜索，结合连续视觉反馈控制器执行规划状态，从而提升机器人长时程操作规划可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉世界模型用于机器人规划受限于预测误差累积、搜索复杂度过高，以及低层控制器难以可靠到达潜空间规划目标。

Method: 构建离散潜变量预测器建模向量量化潜状态间转移，使规划器通过离散搜索前瞻并寻找最短路径；连续学习控制器以视觉反馈依次到达规划状态并纠正偏差。采用新颖的首达时间估计作为准度量替代欧氏距离，使规划抽象与控制器能力对齐。

Result: 在物理机器人汉诺塔操作中，仅用短时示范数据达到100%成功率；现有VLAs、WAMs等基础模型仅在训练数据包含解轨迹时成功。在操作与运动仿真基准上，较最强世界模型提升13.3–85.3个百分点。

Conclusion: VQ-WM能够组合超出短示范轨迹的长时程解并可靠执行，在物理操作与仿真任务中展现一致进步和新能力。

Abstract: Visual world models have shown strong potential for robot planning, yet long-horizon manipulation remains challenging due to compounding prediction errors, large search complexity, and latent plans that the low-level controller cannot reliably reach. In this work, we introduce Vector-Quantized World Model (VQ-WM), a latent feedback motion planning framework that integrates discrete long-horizon search with continuous visual feedback control: a discrete latent predictor models transitions between vector-quantized latent states, allowing the planner to look far ahead and find a shortest path to the goal through discrete search and a continuous learned controller sequentially reaches the planned states through visual feedback, correcting deviations to mitigate compounding errors. Whereas most vector quantization uses Euclidean distance, we use a novel estimator of the first-passage-time as a quasi-metric, which aligns the planning abstraction with the controller's capabilities. We evaluate on the physical robot manipulation of the Tower of Hanoi puzzle: whereas existing state-of-the-art foundation models (VLAs, WAMs) only succeed when the solution trace in their training dataset, our method achieves 100\% success rate with only short-play data, demonstrate its ability to compose long-horizon solutions beyond the short demonstrated trajectories and execute them reliably. Our solution also outperforms strongest world-models by 13.3--85.3 percentage points on simulation benchmarks spanning manipulation and locomotion, demonstrating consistent progress and new capabilities.

</details>


### [91] [Adaptive Bendpoint Formation in Octopus-Inspired Soft Continuum Arms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25585)
*Jessica Gumowski,Julian Finn,Krishna Manaswi Digumarti,David Howard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 受章鱼臂启发，提出一种腱驱动软体臂：通过可充气外壳产生局部摩擦并“卡住”腱，形成可自适应改变的临时锚点，从而在不破坏臂连续性的情况下调节曲率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 章鱼可在臂上任意位置形成弯曲点，实现高灵巧性；传统单腱连续体 manipulator 工作空间和灵巧性有限，而多段多 actuator 系统机械复杂。

Method: 在软体臂中集成阵列式可充气外壳；充气使局部摩擦增大并夹紧腱，形成新的临时锚定端点，改变腱的锚定位置与驱动段曲率；驱动段与松弛段之间仍保持连续。

Result: 驱动段呈现非恒定钟形曲率，与真实章鱼臂伸够运动相似；相比传统单腱连续体 manipulator，可通过选择性曲率调节独立塑造不同区域，扩大工作空间并提升灵巧性，同时避免多段多 actuator 系统的机械复杂性。

Conclusion: 自适应腱锚定机制为软体臂提供了一种结构简单、连续性好且具备章鱼式灵巧性的曲率调控方案。

Abstract: Octopuses exhibit a powerful form of embodied intelligence from a highly adaptable soft body. One key mechanism is the ability to instantiate bending points at any location along the arm, which enables performant and dexterous behaviours. Taking inspiration from the octopus for its dexterity, this study focuses on changing the curvature of a tendon-actuated arm by adaptively changing the anchoring end-point along the arm. An array of inflatable shells is integrated into the arm, where inflating a shell causes an increase in friction at the location, jamming the tendon and creating a temporary new anchoring point. The novel soft actuator does not break the continuity of the arm between the actuated segment and the loose segment. Experiments show that the actuated segment has a non-constant bell-shaped curvature which is comparable to that of a real octopus arm during a reaching motion. Compared to conventional single-tendon continuum manipulators, the proposed design offers an expanded workspace and enhanced dexterity through the ability to independently shape different regions of the arm through selective curvature tuning. At the same time, it avoids the mechanical complexity typically associated with multi-segment, multi-actuator systems.

</details>


### [92] [AQUAACT: A Joystick-Level Underwater Teleoperation Dataset and Closed-Loop Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25534)
*Zhenqi Wu,Trung Tien Dong,Aditya Penumarti,Mohamed A. Fouad,Menna A. Fouad,Mingyang Mao,Jane Jaejeong Shin,Xiaomin Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出首个公开的、带语言标注的水下数据集AQUAACT，将相机图像与人类飞行员摇杆指令及惯性测量配对，并基于该数据集评估水下自主策略与VLA模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下机器人任务日益增多，但目前多由人类飞行员手动操控；陆地上的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型已能根据语言指令自主执行任务，有望替代飞行员，但水下领域缺乏训练此类模型的数据。

Method: 受现有飞行员“看图像、用摇杆操控、飞控将指令转为运动”的流程启发，作者记录人类飞行员操作，构建AQUAACT数据集：包含1.9小时真实世界和17.9小时仿真的人类演示，配对相机图像、摇杆指令和惯性测量；并在仿真中开展闭环基准，评测七种策略，包括模仿学习基线和按默认配方训练的VLA模型，同时考察仿真演示到真实数据的迁移及陆地数据预训练的作用。

Result: 实验表明，AQUAACT中的仿真演示可迁移到真实数据，并显著减少策略所需的真实演示数量；闭环基准显示这些任务远未解决，没有任何策略完成超过五分之一的情节；在陆地数据上预训练的大型VLA模型相对于从头训练的小型策略没有优势，陆地预训练模型尚未弥合水下差距。

Conclusion: AQUAACT为水下语言条件自主控制提供了公开数据与基准，但当前策略和VLA模型在水下任务中表现仍有限，陆地预训练向水下的迁移仍存在明显差距，水下自主任务仍是开放问题。

Abstract: Underwater robots support a growing range of work, from inspecting offshore structures and pipelines to surveying reefs and accompanying divers. Most of these tasks, however, are still flown by hand by a pilot, which raises the question of whether they can be carried out autonomously instead. On land, vision-language-action (VLA) models already let robots carry out tasks autonomously from language instructions, which makes them a natural candidate for taking the pilot's place. Underwater, however, there is little data to train such models. We take inspiration from how these tasks are flown today: the pilot watches the camera image and steers with a joystick, whose commands the flight controller turns into motion. We therefore record the pilots and introduce AQUAACT, to our knowledge the first publicly available language-annotated underwater dataset pairing camera images with human joystick commands. It comprises 1.9 hours of real-world and 17.9 hours of simulated demonstrations, both flown by human pilots, together with the vehicle's inertial measurements. Experiments with AQUAACT show that its simulated demonstrations transfer to real data and greatly reduce the real demonstrations a policy needs. A closed-loop benchmark of seven policies in simulation, from imitation-learning baselines to VLA models, each trained with its default recipe, shows that these tasks remain far from solved: no policy completes more than a fifth of the episodes. Pretraining on land data gives large VLA models no advantage over small policies trained from scratch, a gap that land-trained models have yet to close underwater.

</details>


### [93] [CusADi2: Latency-Optimized Batch Computation of Robot Kinematics, Jacobians, and Hessians on the GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25476)
*John Viljoen,Lasse Peters,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Negar Mehr*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CusADi2 是一个通过批内并行降低批量动力学函数评估延迟的软件库，在 MuJoCo Menagerie 的 67 个机器人上对前向运动学、雅可比和海森矩阵进行基准测试，并在 64 到 4096 的批大小下优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨迹优化方法需要大量评估机器人动力学模型导出的函数，如正向运动学及其雅可比和海森矩阵；现有 GPU 框架主要跨批实例并行以优化吞吐量，而非在线决策所需的低延迟。

Method: CusADi2 在跨批实例并行之外，额外并行化每个批实例内部的计算，以降低批量评估延迟；作者在 MuJoCo Menagerie 的全部 67 个机器人上基准测试前向运动学、雅可比和海森矩阵，并与 MuJoCo XLA、MuJoCo Warp、frax、CusADi 和 CasADi 对比，还展示了在基于采样的控制应用中的性能优势。

Result: CusADi2 在批大小 64 到 4096 时比所有基线延迟更低，在更大批大小时与基线相当；在基于采样的控制应用中展示了性能优势，代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过增加批内并行，CusADi2 能有效降低批量动力学函数评估的延迟，适合对低延迟有要求的在线轨迹优化与控制应用。

Abstract: Trajectory optimization methods, from sampling-based zero-order to gradient-based first- and second-order approaches, require many evaluations of dynamical system functions, i.e., functions derived from a robot's dynamical model, such as forward kinematics and their Jacobians and Hessians. These evaluations are largely independent, e.g., across sampled trajectories or time steps, and can thus be evaluated in parallel as a batch. When used to make decisions online, these methods require low latency, i.e., each batch must complete in as little wall-clock time as possible. Existing frameworks utilize modern GPU hardware primarily to parallelize across batch instances, thereby optimizing throughput but not latency. We present CusADi2, a software library that reduces the batch evaluation latency by additionally parallelizing computation within each batch instance. We benchmark forward kinematics, Jacobians, and Hessians for all 67~robots in the MuJoCo Menagerie, including arms, hands, quadrupeds, humanoids, and biomechanical models, against MuJoCo XLA, MuJoCo Warp, frax, CusADi, and CasADi. CusADi2 achieves lower latency than all baselines at batch sizes of 64 to 4096, while matching them at larger batch sizes. We also demonstrate the performance advantage of CusADi2 in a sampling-based control application. All code is open-source at: this https URL

</details>


### [94] [BiDex-100: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Bimanual Dexterous Manipulation Across 100+ Simulation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25459)
*Yong Wang,Kecheng Zheng,Xing Zhu,Chao Xu,Yujun Shen,Shengze Cai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 BiDex-100——一个面向双手灵巧操作的仿真基准，包含106个任务、五类能力维度、域外变体、两种手部具身与8,080条遥操作演示，并在统一协议下评测7个视觉-语言-动作模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手双手操作在机器人动作模型研究中日益受关注，但少有专门面向灵巧操作的基准，导致现有模型的灵巧操作能力缺乏充分刻画与系统比较。

Method: 构建仿真基准 BiDex-100：任务覆盖广泛，沿泛化、指令遵循、少演示、长时程、记忆五个能力轴组织106个任务；每个任务配有受控视觉与空间变化的域外变体以评估鲁棒性；支持两种具身，提供8,080条经验证成功的遥操作演示，共625万帧；在统一协议下评测7个近期视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型。

Result: 建立了按能力维度划分的性能基线，并给出各模型在鲁棒性与数据效率方面的系统评测结果；同时发布基准网站与相关资源。

Conclusion: BiDex-100 填补了灵巧双手操作基准的空白，为现有VLA模型的灵巧操作能力提供多维度、可复现的评测与比较基础，推动机器人动作模型研究。

Abstract: Bimanual manipulation with dexterous hands has attracted growing attention in research on robotic action models. However, few studies have developed benchmarks specifically tailored for dexterous manipulation, leaving the dexterous manipulation capabilities of existing models insufficiently characterized. In this paper, we present BiDex-100, a simulation benchmark for bimanual dexterous manipulation that combines broad task coverage, multi-dimensional evaluation, and high-DoF hand embodiments. Concretely, the benchmark comprises 106 tasks organized along five capability axes: generalization, instruction following, limited demonstrations, long horizon, and memory. Each task is paired with an out-of-domain variant featuring controlled visual and spatial changes, allowing systematic assessment of robustness. In addition, the benchmark supports two embodiments and provides 8,080 success-validated teleoperated demonstrations, totaling 6.25 million frames. We evaluate seven recent Vision-Language-Action models under a unified protocol, establishing baselines for capability-specific performance, robustness, and data efficiency. The website is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [95] [Self-Crystallizing Behavior Trees: Amortizing Foundation-Model Reasoning into Deterministic Control for Autonomous Drones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25455)
*Behnam Moradi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SCBT 将基础模型用作离线“编译器”而非运行时控制器：在 Behavior Tree 节点失败时生成并筛选最小子树补丁，最终结晶为确定性 BT，从而以低 LLM 调用成本获得接近专家树的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典机器人控制（FSM/BT）确定、可检查但设计时固定，难以应对未预见情形；基础模型可运行时合成控制逻辑，但延迟高、非确定性强，不适合安全关键航空系统；现有 LLM 驱动方法让模型长期在环且丢弃生成结果。

Method: 提出 Self-Crystallizing Behavior Trees（SCBT）。当 BT 节点失败时，LLM 提出最小、任务无关的子树补丁；CMA-ES 在仿真中调参；上下文 bandit 权衡存储补丁与新 LLM 调用成本；三阶段准入管线（静态检查、不变量监控回放、相对未打补丁树的经验裕度）仅接纳可证明恢复的补丁。准入补丁进入机队库，待 LLM 调用率衰减后结晶为无人手写的确定性 BT。在 Microsoft Project AirSim 上以模式约束 LLM 管线（Gemini 3.1 Pro）和锁步仿真器实现。

Result: 在含风阵和电池故障的 20 回合学习阶段、3 个随机种子下，SCBT 使用 11±1.4 次 LLM 调用，对比整树再生成 26 次、无库 22 次；后半程每回合 0.33 次调用。结晶树为 28 和 33 节点、各含两个补丁，在无学习运行时达到 97±2% 任务成功率，未打补丁树为 12%，手写专家树为 98%，离线重 tick 可精确复现决策轨迹。两个负结果：回放准入把风致地理围栏越界归因于补丁，错误拒绝有用风恢复；仅静态消融在留出故障上泛化更好。

Conclusion: SCBT 表明将基础模型作为补丁编译器，结合参数优化、成本感知选择、严格准入与结晶，可在低 LLM 调用下获得确定性、可检查且接近专家水平的 BT 恢复能力；但回放准入的因果归因和静态泛化仍需改进。

Abstract: Classical robot control, Finite State Machines and Behavior Trees (BTs), is deterministic and inspectable but fixed at design time: it fails on situations its designers did not anticipate. Foundation models can synthesize new control logic at runtime, at a cost in latency and non-determinism hard to accept in safety-critical aerial systems. Existing LLM-driven approaches keep the model in the loop permanently and discard what it produces. We propose Self-Crystallizing Behavior Trees (SCBT), in which the foundation model is a compiler rather than a runtime. When a BT node fails, an LLM proposes a minimal, mission-agnostic subtree patch; CMA-ES tunes its parameters in simulation; a contextual bandit decides whether a stored patch or a new LLM call is worth its cost; and a three-stage admission pipeline (static check, invariant-monitored replays, an empirical margin over the unpatched tree) admits only patches that demonstrably recover. Admitted patches are stored in a fleet library and, once the LLM call rate decays, crystallized into a deterministic BT that no human wrote. We implement SCBT on Microsoft Project AirSim with a schema-constrained LLM pipeline (Gemini 3.1 Pro) and a lockstep simulator. Over three seeds of a 20-episode learning phase with wind-gust and battery failures, SCBT used $11\pm1.4$ LLM calls against 26 for whole-tree regeneration and 22 without a library, with 0.33 calls per episode in the second half. The crystallized trees, 28 and 33 nodes with two patches each, reached $97\pm2\%$ mission success with no learning running, against 12\% for the unpatched tree and 98\% for a hand-coded expert tree, and reproduce decision traces exactly when re-ticked offline. Two negative results: replay admission rejected useful wind recoveries by attributing wind-induced geofence excursions to the patch, and a static-only ablation generalized better to held-out failures.

</details>


### [96] [Intent-Informed Model Predictive Control for Shared Autonomy in Remote Manipulation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25436)
*Lorena Maria Genua,Camilla Tabasso,Mya Schroder,Joshua Migdal,Tye Brady,Taskin Padir*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于多模态意图推断信念分布熵的动态共享自主仲裁策略，并用MPC融合人类输入与自主控制，实现从直接遥操作到全自主的连续过渡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享自主需根据人、任务和环境信息自适应调整自主等级；现有方法难以实现连续、渐进的自主性过渡。

Method: 用动态贝叶斯网络（DBN）结合操作者注视数据与机械臂运动实时更新目标信念；以信念分布熵驱动仲裁，并将人类输入与自主控制的融合建模为模型预测控制（MPC）问题。

Result: 与两种基于MPC的共享自主基线相比，物体取回时间至少降低61%，轨迹长度降低44%，人类控制输入降低60%；参与者报告较高的感知控制感、可预测性、意图识别和响应性，以及较低工作负荷。

Conclusion: 基于信念熵的MPC仲裁可实现连续自适应的共享自主，在效率、人机输入负担和主观体验上优于基线方法。

Abstract: Shared autonomy is a control paradigm that combines human cognitive abilities with robot precision to enable effective remote manipulation in complex environments. A key challenge is how to adapt the level of autonomy using information extracted from the human, task, and/or environment. In this paper, we propose a novel arbitration strategy that dynamically adjusts the level of autonomy in the system using the entropy of a belief distribution derived from a multimodal intent inference. Human intent is modeled with a Dynamic Bayesian Network (DBN), which updates beliefs over goals given real-time operator gaze data and robot arm motion during teleoperation. We then formulate the blending of human input and autonomous control as a Model Predictive Control (MPC) problem, where the system's autonomous behavior is guided by the belief distribution. This approach enables continuous, gradual transitions across the full spectrum of autonomy levels, from direct teleoperation to full autonomy. To validate our approach, we conducted experiments to compare the performance of our proposed algorithm against two MPC-based shared autonomy methods. Compared to the baselines, our approach achieved reductions of at least 61% in object retrieval time, 44% in trajectory length, and 60% in human control input. Participants reported high perceived control, predictability, intent recognition, and responsiveness, along with low workload.

</details>


### [97] [SurGPT: A Multi-Task Foundation Model for Robotic Surgical Skills Assessment and Gesture Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25426)
*Parham M. Kebria,Saeid Nahavandi,Mehrdad Saif,Fernando Bello*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于视觉编码器与Transformer的基础模型，可自动分析达芬奇机器人手术视频，同时完成手术任务分类、技能水平评估和手术手势自然语言描述生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人手术技能评估依赖人类专家主观评分，存在效率瓶颈，难以规模化并制约手术教育发展。

Method: 融合视觉编码器与Transformer架构，采用多任务学习框架和自适应不确定性加权，联合优化三项任务：手术任务分类（打结、穿针、缝合）、标准化技能水平评估、手术手势自然语言生成；通过位置编码建模时序信息，利用注意力机制整合时空特征。

Result: 在达芬奇技能模拟器数据集上，该方法在手术任务识别上达到最先进性能，技能评估具有可解释性且与专家评价相关，并能生成手术手势的自然语言描述。

Conclusion: 该框架为自动化手术教育、实时反馈系统以及手术培训与认证中的客观技能评估提供了新可能。

Abstract: The assessment of surgical skills in robotic surgery training has traditionally relied on subjective evaluations by human experts, creating bottlenecks in surgical education and limiting scalability. We present a novel foundation model that automatically analyzes robotic surgical videos to provide comprehensive skill assessment and gesture understanding. Combining a visual encoder with a transformer architecture, the model performs three key tasks simultaneously: 1) surgical task classification (knot tying, needle passing, suturing), 2) skill level assessment on a standardized scale, and 3) natural language generation of surgical gesture descriptions. The model processes video sequences from the da Vinci surgical robot and generates human-readable text descriptions of surgical actions, similar to how language models generate text from prompts, using a multi-task learning framework with adaptive uncertainty weighting to optimize performance across all objectives. Our transformer-based architecture incorporates positional encoding for temporal understanding and attention mechanisms for spatiotemporal feature integration. Evaluated on the da Vinci Skills Simulator dataset, the proposed framework demonstrates state-of-the-art performance in surgical task recognition and provides interpretable skill assessments that correlate with expert evaluations, with the model's ability to generate natural language descriptions of surgical gestures opening new possibilities for automated surgical education, real-time feedback systems, and objective skill assessment in surgical training and certification programs.

</details>


### [98] [Learning When to Adapt: Reactivity-Conditioned Residual Adaptation under Human-Robot Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25419)
*Immanuel R. Santjoko,Benedictus C. G. Cinun,Jing Chen,Bin Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RCRA在线自适应框架：在冻结的保守导航策略上，根据估计的行人反应性调节学习到的残差；用Transformer估计器从运动历史与机器人上下文推断潜在反应性，并将估计结果空间聚合为门控LoRA模块的自适应尺度，从而在反应性行人附近合作、在非反应性行人附近保守。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行人是否对机器人作出反应存在差异，但许多强化学习策略假设所有行人均非反应性以优先保证安全；未以局部行人反应性为条件的策略可能过度保守，或依赖实际不会发生的合作行为。

Method: RCRA以冻结的保守导航策略为骨干，学习残差进行在线自适应；Transformer估计器利用行人运动历史与机器人上下文推断潜在行人反应性；将反应性估计按空间聚合为自适应尺度，用于门控LoRA模块，实现按局部行人反应性切换合作与保守导航行为。

Result: 反应性估计器在场景平均仿真准确率上较假设已知行人目标的贝叶斯基线提升12.62个百分点；在所有场景成功率均高于90%的基线中，RCRA将场景平均路径长度较保守骨干缩短16.7%，较随机训练基线缩短7.3%；平均成功率较反应性训练策略提升14.88个百分点。

Conclusion: 显式估计并利用行人反应性可使导航策略在安全与效率间更精细权衡：对反应性行人采取合作、对非反应性行人保持保守，从而在多场景中提升成功率并缩短路径长度。

Abstract: Pedestrians differ in whether they respond to a robot, yet many RL policies assume all are non-reactive to prioritize safety. Policies trained without conditioning on local pedestrians' reactivity may therefore be unnecessarily conservative or rely on cooperation that does not occur. We propose RCRA, an online adaptation framework that modulates a learned residual around a frozen conservative navigation policy according to estimated pedestrian reactivity. A Transformer-based estimator infers latent pedestrian reactivity from motion histories and robot context, providing a more accurate signal for navigation adaptation and improving scenario-averaged simulation accuracy by $12.62$ percentage points over the Bayesian baseline, which assumes known pedestrian goals. These estimates are spatially aggregated into an adaptation scale that gates LoRA modules, enabling cooperative behavior near reactive pedestrians and conservative behavior near non-reactive pedestrians. Among baselines achieving SR above $90\%$ in every scenario, RCRA reduces scenario-averaged path length by $16.7\%$ relative to the conservative backbone and by $7.3\%$ relative to randomized-training baselines. It improves mean SR by $14.88$ percentage points over the reactive-trained policy.

</details>


### [99] [T(R,O) Grasp++: Cross-Embodiment Dexterous Grasping in Cluttered Environments at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25410)
*Xin Fei,Zhixuan Xu,Huaicong Fang,Fangjun Chen,Zhaoyu Mu,Rui Yang,Zhexi Luo,Lin Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文构建了约5亿抓取标注、覆盖8种灵巧手和约5万物体的仿真基准，并提出基于扩散的跨本体灵巧抓取合成框架T(R,O) Grasp++，用于杂乱环境中的灵巧抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 杂乱场景中的灵巧抓取需要在高维手部协调与复杂物体-环境约束下完成，仍是机器人操作的核心难题。

Method: 作者构建大规模仿真抓取基准，包含约0.5B抓取标注、8种灵巧手和约50K物体；并提出T(R,O) Grasp++，一个可扩展的、基于扩散的跨本体灵巧抓取合成框架，面向杂乱环境。

Result: 实验表明该模型显著优于现有基线，在8种灵巧本体上平均成功率达88%；同时推理延迟仅0.3s，并将推理样本效率提升10倍以上。

Conclusion: 该工作通过大规模基准与可扩展扩散框架，推动了杂乱环境下跨本体灵巧抓取合成，并在成功率与推理效率上取得显著优势。

Abstract: Dexterous grasping in cluttered scenes remains a central challenge in robotic manipulation, as it requires high-dimensional hand coordination under complex object-environment constraints. To address this problem, we introduce one of the largest simulation benchmarks to date, comprising around 0.5B grasp annotations across eight dexterous hands and approximately 50K objects. Furthermore, we propose T(R,O) Grasp++, a scalable diffusion-based framework for cross-embodiment dexterous grasp synthesis in cluttered environments. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our model significantly outperforms existing baselines, achieving an average success rate of 88% across eight dexterous embodiments. Meanwhile, our pipeline achieves only 0.3s inference latency while improving inference sample efficiency by over 10 times.

</details>


### [100] [AeroDASH: Asynchronous Vision-Language-Action Control for Continuous UAV Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25407)
*Zhe Liu,Yuxuan Zheng,Jinzhe Qin,Ziqi Gao,Boyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 AeroDASH，一种面向无人机连续语言条件飞行的异步 VLA 框架，通过 B 样条轨迹表示、轨迹融合和执行感知 GRPO 缓解机载推理延迟导致的预测轨迹与真实运动不匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 策略在无人机机载推理时存在延迟，使预测轨迹与无人机当前运动不同步，导致跟踪误差和飞行动作不连续。

Method: 先用专家演示对 B 样条策略进行监督微调，输出位置和偏航的连续轨迹参考；执行时采用轨迹融合删除过期预测前缀，并以导数连续方式连接剩余轨迹与当前参考，同时限制轨迹形变；再针对异步执行偏差，使用轨迹级奖励的执行感知 GRPO 自适应优化策略。

Result: 在 UAV-Flow 测试集上成功率达 76.56%，分别比 OpenVLA-UAV 和去除 GRPO 的 AeroDASH 高 38.1 和 20.1 个百分点；执行实验表明推理期间仍能维持运动，并相比异步航点序列跟踪降低加速度、加加速度和速度波动。

Conclusion: AeroDASH 能有效支持机载推理与异步轨迹执行的无人机语言条件飞行，在成功率和运动平滑性上优于相关基线，代码将在接收后开源。

Abstract: Onboard inference latency creates a mismatch between the trajectories predicted by vision-language-action (VLA) policies and the ongoing motion of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs). We present AeroDASH, an asynchronous VLA framework for continuous language-conditioned flight. A B-spline policy is initialized through supervised fine-tuning on expert demonstrations, providing continuous trajectory references in position and yaw. During execution, trajectory fusion removes the expired prediction prefix and connects the remaining segment to the active reference with derivative continuity while limiting trajectory deformation. To account for deviations induced by asynchronous execution, execution-aware group relative policy optimization (GRPO) further adapts the policy using trajectory-level rewards. On the UAV-Flow test set, AeroDASH achieves a success rate of 76.56%, exceeding OpenVLA-UAV and AeroDASH w/o GRPO by 38.1 and 20.1 percentage points, respectively. Execution experiments show that AeroDASH sustains motion during inference and reduces acceleration, jerk, and speed fluctuations relative to asynchronous tracking of waypoint sequences. We further demonstrate language-conditioned flight with onboard inference and asynchronous trajectory execution. The code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [101] [Energy-Score Stabilized Attentive Mixture-Density Policy: A Lightweight Approach to Multimodal Robot Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25401)
*Sachiya Fujita,Hideyuki Ichiwara,Shigeki Sugano,Tetsuya Ogata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于注意力混合密度策略与Energy Score训练的运动生成方法，在单次前向传播中输出显式概率分布，兼顾多模态、低推理成本与有限示范下的泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际运动生成需处理同一观测对应多种正确动作的多模态性，同时保持低推理成本，并能从有限示范中强泛化。

Method: 构建注意力混合密度策略，将未来视觉运动轨迹表示为显式概率分布；单次前向输出关节命令（及可选注意力点）的高斯混合参数；采用Energy Score（对角协方差假设下的一维CRPS之和）替代负对数似然进行优化，以避免MDN奇异性和梯度尺度不稳定；并将显式空间注意力与混合密度输出耦合。

Result: 在8自由度机械臂上生成不同的避障轨迹并稳定避障学习；在随机移动物体抓取任务中，面对多种未见条件，性能较Diffusion Policy提升15-25%，展现更强泛化性与实时可行性。

Conclusion: 该方法通过注意力混合密度输出与Energy Score训练，有效实现多模态运动生成，在视觉噪声和小数据条件下稳健选择多模态行为，并优于扩散策略。

Abstract: For practical motion generation, it is essential to handle multimodality (multiple actions can be correct for the same observation) while maintaining low inference cost and strong generalization from limited demonstrations. We introduce an attentive mixture-density policy trained with the Energy Score that represents future visuomotor trajectories as explicit probability distributions with a single forward pass. Concretely, we output Gaussian mixture parameters for joint commands (and optionally attention points) and optimize them with the Energy Score (computed as a sum of 1D CRPS under a diagonal covariance assumption) instead of the negative log-likelihood (NLL) to avoid MDN singularities and gradient-scale instabilities. Our architecture couples explicit spatial attention with mixture-density outputs, enabling robust multimodal behavior selection under visual noise and small-data regimes. On an 8-DOF manipulator, the method generates distinct avoidance trajectories and stabilizes learning in obstacle avoidance. Moreover, in a randomized moving-object grasping task, where spatiotemporal multimodality and fast timing decisions are required, the proposed model outperforms Diffusion Policy by 15-25% under several unseen conditions, demonstrating superior generalization and real-time feasibility.

</details>


### [102] [Visual-Inertial Odometry for Ground Robots with Dynamic Camera-IMU Extrinsic Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25397)
*Bo Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对视觉惯性里程计中相机-IMU刚性安装假设的局限，提出基于Denavit-Hartenberg约定与机器人运动学模型的时间变相机-IMU外参在线标定框架，并采用两步初始化策略估计初始关节角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉惯性里程计通常假设传感器刚性安装，难以适应存在结构运动、相机与IMU相对位姿随时间变化的实际场景，因此需要研究时间变相机-IMU外参标定。

Method: 将机器人运动学模型引入动态相机-IMU外参标定，基于Denavit-Hartenberg约定建立标定方法；设计两步初始化策略高效估计初始关节角，为后续外参优化提供可靠初值，并实现在线估计时间变相机-IMU外参。

Result: 仿真实验表明，该框架能够有效适应相机-IMU外参变化，同时保持具有竞争力的定位精度。

Conclusion: 所提框架可处理时间变相机-IMU外参标定问题，提升视觉惯性系统对结构运动的适应性；当前结果基于仿真验证。

Abstract: Visual-inertial odometry systems are often constrained by the assumption that sensors are rigidly mounted, which limits their adaptability in practical scenarios involving structural motion. To address this limitation, this paper investigates time-varying camera-IMU extrinsic calibration. First, a dynamic camera-IMU extrinsic calibration method based on the Denavit-Hartenberg convention is proposed by incorporating the robot kinematic model. Second, a two-step initialization strategy is developed to efficiently estimate the initial joint angles, providing a reliable starting point for subsequent extrinsic parameter optimization and enabling online estimation of timevarying camera-IMU extrinsic parameters. Simulation experiments demonstrate that the proposed framework can effectively accommodate variations in camera-IMU extrinsic parameters while maintaining competitive localization accuracy.

</details>


### [103] [TILDE: Teacher-in-the-Loop Distillation from Embodied Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25376)
*Yuxiang Lu,Yantong Liu,Jinghua Hou,Xueli Sun,Ruihua Han,Zhe Liu,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TILDE框架，将大规模动作预训练具身基础模型（EFM）的能力蒸馏到未做动作预训练的策略中，并结合AutoDAgger自动采集教师恢复轨迹，在真实任务中显著优于行为克隆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模动作预训练能赋予EFM强操作能力、泛化性和闭环鲁棒性，但复现此类预训练需要大量机器人数据和计算资源；因此需要将预训练EFM能力迁移到无动作预训练策略。

Method: TILDE采用教师参与的在环蒸馏：在学生对弈诱导状态上进行on-policy蒸馏，同时学习教师执行的恢复轨迹；并提出AutoDAgger自主数据聚合流程，利用机器人奖励模型监测任务进度并触发教师干预，从而无需人工监控或手动控制即可采集恢复经验。

Result: AutoDAgger相比人类在环方法将恢复轨迹采集速度提升超过2倍；TILDE统一利用示范学习、on-policy教师监督和教师纠正行为，在三个真实世界任务上相较行为克隆最高提升4.1倍任务性能，并支持有效的跨架构蒸馏。

Conclusion: TILDE与AutoDAgger能够在无需动作预训练和大量人工干预的情况下，将预训练EFM的操作与恢复能力有效迁移至学生策略，提升真实任务性能并支持跨架构蒸馏。

Abstract: Large-scale action pretraining equips Embodied Foundation Models (EFMs) with strong manipulation capabilities, with particular strengths in generalization and closed-loop robustness, but reproducing such pretraining requires substantial robot data and computation. To transfer these capabilities from pretrained EFMs to policies without action pretraining, we introduce TILDE (Teacher-In-the-Loop Distillation from EFMs), which synergizes on-policy distillation at student-induced states with learning from teacher-executed recovery trajectories. We further propose AutoDAgger, an autonomous data aggregation pipeline in which a robotic reward model is utilized to monitor task progress and trigger teacher intervention, thereby collecting recovery experience without human monitoring or manual control. AutoDAgger accelerates recovery-trajectory collection by more than $2\times$ compared with the human-in-the-loop method. By unifying learning from demonstrations, on-policy teacher supervision, and teacher's corrective behavior, TILDE improves task performance by up to $4.1\times$ over behavior cloning across three real-world tasks and supports effective cross-architecture distillation.

</details>


### [104] [Learning Structured Locomotion References for Optimization-Based Whole-Body Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24779)
*Ömer Tarık Banuş,Uluç Saranlı,M. Mert Ankaralı*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种分层四足运动控制架构，将强化学习用于自适应调整结构化运动参考，而由步态调度器、反应式落脚点规划器和全身脉冲控制器负责高频执行，并在六方向扰动课程下训练与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足运动需要协调身体运动、接触力、落脚点与步态时序；作者希望让学习策略专注于“何时、如何”修改一个本身已可工作的基于模型的运动控制器，而非从零学习全部控制。

Method: 构建分层控制器：基于模型的骨架可独立维持名义trot；强化学习策略自适应调整结构化运动参考；步态调度器、反应式落脚点规划和全身脉冲控制器保留高频执行职责。完整层级在向量化controller-in-the-loop环境中训练，并采用六方向扰动课程。

Result: 在统一仿真器与评估协议下，所提控制器在测试扰动网格上的平均生存率为77.29%，基于模型的参考控制器为50.35%。训练时通道消融表明，自适应落脚点规划对扰动网格生存率的边际贡献最大，并揭示步态频率自适应具有方向依赖效应。

Conclusion: 结果证明该分层控制器架构可行，能够通过结构化运动参考将学习到的自适应能力与基于优化的全身控制相结合，提升四足机器人在扰动条件下的运动鲁棒性。

Abstract: Quadruped locomotion requires coordinating body motion,contact forces, foothold placement, and gait timing. We present a hierarchical controller in which reinforcement learning adapts these structured locomotion references while a gait scheduler, reactive footstep planner, and whole-body impulse controller retain responsibility for high-frequency execution. The model-based backbone can sustain a nominal trot independently, allowing the policy to focus on when and how to modify an already functional locomotion controller. We train the complete hierarchy in a vectorized controller-in-the-loop environment using a six-direction disturbance curriculum. Under a common simulator and evaluation protocol, the proposed controller achieves 77.29% mean survival across the tested disturbance grid, compared with 50.35% for the model-based reference controller. Training-time channel ablations identify adaptive foothold placement as the largest marginal contributor to grid survival and reveal direction-dependent effects of gait-rate adaptation. Together, these results demonstrate the viability of the proposed hierarchical controller architecture for integrating learned adaptation with optimization-based whole-body control through structured locomotion references.

</details>


### [105] [EndoVLA-OOV: Language-Conditioned Out-of-View Target Acquisition in Robotic Endoscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24747)
*Chi Kit Ng,Yidong Zhang,Tianchun Wu,Zhiqing Tang,Hanlin Zhao,Siu Hing Lui,Tao Yang,Jingsong Lin,Huxin Gao,Yeung Trevor,Raymond Tang,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出语言条件视觉-语言-动作策略 EndoVLA-OOV，用于机器人内镜中根据描述将视野外可疑目标重新纳入视野，并发布大规模VLA数据集与跨环境/跨 embodiment 验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上消化道内镜的“扫描后回访”流程需要把先前观察到的可疑区域重新带回视野；这要求机器人从受限、可变形环境的部分观测中，将解剖与外观描述转化为相机运动，实现视野外目标获取。

Method: EndoVLA-OOV 将预训练骨干网络适配为连续二自由度远端弯曲控制，并融合初始、中间与当前图像指导搜索；同时构建开源VLA数据集，含6类任务、1050个episode、约26.2万观测-动作对。

Result: 物理评估覆盖湿镜头、低光、两个未见胃体模及跨embodiment迁移至未见离体环境；跨embodiment全量策略目标获取成功率为93.3%（28/30）。在相同模型、训练与测试配置下，训练数据从312增至1050 episodes使成功率从40.0%升至90.0%；指令与观测干预进一步支持语言和时间上下文的作用。

Conclusion: 结果支持语言引导的内镜回访在评估环境和机器人embodiment上的有效性，并表明扩展任务特定示范数据能显著提升视野外目标获取性能。

Abstract: The scan-then-revisit workflow of upper gastrointestinal endoscopy requires bringing a previously observed suspicious region back into view for closer inspection. Acquiring such targets requires translating anatomical and appearance descriptions into camera motions from partial observations of a confined, deformable environment. We present EndoVLA-OOV, a language-conditioned vision-language-action policy for out-of-view target acquisition in robotic endoscopy. The policy adapts a pretrained backbone to continuous two-degree-of-freedom distal bending commands and combines initial, intermediate, and current images to guide search. To support policy learning, we provide an open-source VLA dataset containing 1,050 episodes across six tasks and approximately 262k observation-action pairs. Physical evaluation spans wet-lens and low-light conditions, two unseen stomach phantoms, and cross-embodiment transfer to an unseen ex-vivo environment. In the cross-embodiment evaluation, the full-data policy achieves 93.3% target acquisition success (28/30). In a controlled training-data comparison, expanding the corpus from 312 to 1,050 episodes increases acquisition success from 40.0% to 90.0% under the same model, training, and test configuration. Instruction and observation interventions further support the use of language and temporal context in the evaluated acquisition tasks. These results support language-guided endoscopic revisitation across the evaluated environments and robot embodiments and demonstrate the benefit of expanded task-specific demonstrations.

</details>


### [106] [Relational Spatial Alignment for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24705)
*Chaoran Zhu,Hengyi Wang,Chenglin Cui,Yazhe Wan,Taein Kwon,Changjae Oh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文指出将VLA表征直接与预训练3D基础模型对齐会导致秩坍缩，并提出基于成对关系对齐的3D空间感知注入方法，在保持原模型表达能力的同时提升机器人空间理解与操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人需要空间理解来感知环境并执行动作，但现有方法将3D感知直接编码进视觉-语言-动作模型时，通常使VLA表征与预训练3D基础模型逐点对齐；这种直接几何特征对齐本质上是利用3D基础先验作为特征空间中的低秩正则，造成预训练VLA表征严重秩坍缩。

Method: 作者将对齐目标从逐点相等转向成对关系一致性，提出一种表征对齐方法，将3D空间感知注入VLA模型，同时不牺牲模型原有表达能力。

Result: 在公开基准、作者新提出的空间推理基准以及真实世界操作场景上的大量实验表明，该方法能有效将3D空间表征融入VLA推理，并在多样操作任务中取得稳健性能与增强的空间感知。

Conclusion: 研究表明，避免直接逐点几何对齐、改用成对关系对齐，是向VLA模型注入3D空间先验并保持表征表达能力的有效途径。

Abstract: Spatial understanding is essential for robots to perceive their surrounding environments and execute actions. Existing approaches typically encode 3D awareness into vision-language-action (VLA) models by directly aligning their representations with pre-trained 3D foundation models. However, we observe that such direct geometric feature alignment essentially exploits 3D foundation priors as a form of low-rank regularization in the feature space, resulting in severe rank collapse of the pre-trained VLA representations. In contrast, we shift the focus from enforcing point-wise equality to aligning pair-wise relationships. We propose a representation-alignment method that enables the injection of 3D spatial awareness without compromising the model's original expressivity. Extensive experiments on public benchmarks, our new spatial reasoning benchmark, and real-world manipulation scenarios demonstrate that our approach effectively incorporates 3D spatial representations into VLA reasoning, achieving robust performance and enhanced spatial awareness across diverse manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [107] [Action-Aligned Latent Planning for Versatile Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24675)
*Yutang Lin,Guanya Shi,C. Karen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出FIBER，利用冻结行为基础模型（BFM）的可执行潜空间作为异构人形全身移动操作技能的共享规划表示，并通过离线训练单一感知流规划器实现多任务统一控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为多样化人形移动操作学习单一感知策略时，需统一高层任务决策且保留各交互成功所需的状态相关物理响应；运动学/接触目标抽象紧凑但不编码交互特定控制响应，直接预测低层动作又要求规划器建模高频闭环反馈。

Method: FIBER将独立训练的任务专用策略通过潜策略投影（LPP）映射到冻结BFM的可执行潜空间：寻找诱导动作匹配其局部控制响应的潜命令，从而以较低频率规划同时保留BFM高频状态反馈。聚合各技能闭环潜 rollout 后，完全离线训练单一感知流规划器。另提出AH-BFM，用多gamma前向-后向（FB）学习训练自适应时域BFM，支持精确控制与长时程全身行为。
