Result: 在仿真中的箱子拾取、开门和物体抓取任务上，每任务128种变化下FIBER平均成功率为42.0%，超过投影潜空间回放（11.6%）三倍以上，并显著优于运动学规划（3.5%）；在真实Unitree G1人形上展示了sim-to-real迁移、技能链式和条件引导。

Conclusion: FIBER表明，以BFM可执行潜空间为共享表示可有效统一异构人形移动操作技能的高层规划与全身控制，并支持离线训练、真实迁移和组合泛化。

Abstract: Learning a single perceptive policy for diverse humanoid loco-manipulation requires unifying high-level task decisions without losing the state-dependent physical responses that make each interaction succeed. This makes the representation between high-level planning and whole-body control critical: kinematic or contact targets provide compact abstractions but do not directly encode interaction-specific control responses, while directly predicting low-level actions requires the planner to model high-frequency closed-loop feedback. We propose FIBER, a framework that uses the executable latent space of a frozen behavioral foundation model (BFM) as a shared planning representation for heterogeneous interaction skills. Our latent policy projection (LPP) maps independently acquired task-specific policies into this representation by identifying latent commands whose induced actions match their local control responses, enabling lower-rate planning while retaining the BFM's high-frequency state feedback. After projection, we aggregate closed-loop latent rollouts from all skills and train a single perceptive flow planner entirely offline. We further introduce AH-BFM, an adaptive-horizon BFM trained with multi-gamma forward-backward (FB) learning to support both precise control and temporally extended whole-body behavior. We evaluate FIBER on box pickup, door opening, and object grasping in simulation and on a real Unitree G1 humanoid. Across 128 diverse variations per task in simulation, FIBER achieves 42.0% mean success, more than tripling projected latent replay (11.6%) and substantially outperforming kinematic planning (3.5%). Real-world experiments further demonstrate sim-to-real transfer, skill chaining, and conditional steering through the same unified planner.

</details>


### [108] [Noise-Invariant Spatial Encoding for 3D Manipulation Policies under Depth Corruption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24674)
*Zhiyuan Yang,Jiaju He,Ruiteng Zhao,Anqi Cheng,Haiyue Zhu,Kezhi Mao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出Noise-Invariant Spatial Encoding（NISE），一种面向含噪声深度输入的3D操作策略架构。该架构通过几何跨视图自监督与高斯边缘化3D注意力学习可信的几何特征，从而在保留干净深度性能的同时提升对深度噪声的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以显式场景几何为输入的3D操作策略能够推理度量距离、物体关系与机器人-场景可达性，优于仅依赖彩色图像的方法；但这一几何接口同时也是一种脆弱性来源：商品级RGB-D传感器在透明、反光、薄壁或无纹理物体周围会产生缺失测量、边界伪影以及“看似合理却错误”的表面。作者在真实感深度损坏下发现，现代3D策略会出现急剧的“深度噪声悬崖”，即使训练时使用匹配的噪声深度也无法消除这一残余差距。该差距的根源在于：损坏的深度会与动作专家所消费的token坐标及几何特征相互纠缠。

Method: 提出NISE架构，核心包含两部分：(1) Geometric Cross-View Self-Supervision（几何跨视图自监督）：利用经过校准的多视图深度来塑造策略的共享特征，使几何表示更可信；(2) Gaussian Marginalized 3D Attention（高斯边缘化3D注意力）：将位置不确定性显式引入部署时的注意力计算，而非把每个lifted token当作精确点处理，从而把深度噪声建模为不确定性，而不是把它当成精确的表面。

Result: 在带有传感器级真实损坏的仿真基准以及涉及透明与反光材料的真实机器人任务中，NISE显著提升了深度噪声鲁棒性，同时保持了干净深度输入下的原有性能。

Conclusion: 结果表明，应面向商品级RGB-D的实际部署条件来设计3D操作策略，而不是只在干净深度基准上评估；将几何自监督与不确定性感知注意力结合，是缓解深度损坏导致性能陡降的有效途径。

Abstract: 3D manipulation policies derive actions from explicit scene geometry rather than color images alone, enabling them to reason over metric distances, object relations, and robot-scene reachability. This geometric interface is also a vulnerability: commodity RGB-D sensors return missing measurements, boundary artifacts, and plausible but wrong surfaces around transparent, reflective, thin, or textureless objects. We study this failure under realistic depth corruption and observe a sharp depth-noise cliff across modern 3D policies, even when they are trained with matched noisy depth. This residual gap motivates a spatial-encoding approach: corrupted depth becomes entangled with the token coordinates and geometry features consumed by the action expert. We propose Noise-Invariant Spatial Encoding (NISE), a 3D policy architecture that learns trustworthy geometry features even facing imperfect depth. NISE uses Geometric Cross-View Self-Supervision to shape shared policy features with calibrated multi-view depth, and Gaussian Marginalized 3D Attention to carry position uncertainty into deployed attention rather than treating every lifted token as exact. Across simulated benchmarks with sensor-realistic corruption and real-robot tasks involving transparent and reflective materials, NISE improves depth-noise robustness while preserving clean-depth performance, supporting 3D policies designed for commodity RGB-D deployment rather than clean-depth benchmarks alone.

</details>


### [109] [Solving a Giant Rubik's Cube with a Quadruped Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24610)
*Lukas Amiet,Gabriel Sánchez-Blanco Repgen,Mayank Mittal,Victor Klemm,Andrei Cramariuc,Marco Hutter,Cesar Cadena*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 四足机器人仅用足部自主完成巨型魔方的多步操作求解，融合机载RGB-D感知、任务规划与强化学习全身技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现无手持的自由关节物体操作需同时控制物体位姿、内部构型并保持机器人平衡；魔方求解要求重复精确交互，层误动会使后续步骤失效。

Method: 构建机载RGB-D感知流程，通过移动与翻转从部分视角估计魔方度量位姿和完整颜色状态；规划器将求解方案转换为机器人可执行技能序列；利用特权观测强化学习训练全身技能并蒸馏为基于深度与本体感受输出关节目标的策略，实现重定向魔方且保持层构型、单足固定另一足推转层。

Result: 策略可容忍机器人-魔方相对位姿变化并恢复外部扰动；系统在硬件上完成3次自主求解，每次约30步、耗时8-9分钟。

Conclusion: 结果表明，结合机载感知与协调足式操作可使四足机器人完成长时间多步骤物理任务。

Abstract: Pedipulating a freestanding articulated object requires controlling its pose and internal configuration while maintaining balance. We demonstrate this capability by autonomously solving a giant Rubik's Cube with a quadruped using only its feet. The task demands repeated precise interactions, since unintended layer motion can invalidate subsequent operations. We develop an onboard RGB-D perception pipeline that estimates the cube's metric pose and its full color state from partial views acquired through locomotion and flipping. A planner converts the computed solve into robot-executable skill sequences. Learned whole-body skills reorient the cube while preserving its layer configuration and turn its layers with one foot pinning the cube as the other pushes. We train these skills using reinforcement learning with privileged observations and distill them into policies that map onboard depth and proprioception to joint targets. The deployed policies tolerate variations in relative robot-cube placement and recover from external disturbances. The system completes three autonomous hardware solves, each about 30 steps over 8-9 minutes. These results show that combining onboard perception with coordinated pedipulation enables a quadruped to complete extended multi-step physical tasks. Additional material can be found at: this https URL

</details>


### [110] [RoMo-GS: Efficient Online 3D Gaussian Splatting for Robot Mapping via Exposure-Integrated Motion Blur](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24605)
*Sangwoo Jung,Di Deng,Jiahui Fu,Laura Herlant,Ayoung Kim,Yafei Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoMo-GS 是一个面向机器人应用的在线 3D Gaussian Splatting 系统，通过解析式运动模糊建模、Hessian 剪枝和联合位姿/速度/高斯优化，解决在线建图中的模糊、位姿漂移与高斯数量无界增长问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线 3DGS 在真实机器人平台部署时面临快速运动导致的图像运动模糊、重建几何受损与相机位姿漂移，以及连续在线建图中高斯数量无限增长等问题。

Method: 提出 RoMo-GS：对每个投影高斯在曝光区间内进行解析积分，而非对 N 个子帧取平均，使每个高斯计算复杂度为 O(1)；使用基于 Hessian 的剪枝分数限制高斯增长；并用 Adam 及针对高斯的在线 LM 步骤联合优化位姿、速度和高斯。

Result: 在多个公开数据集和自采集数据集上，RoMo-GS 的渲染质量匹配或超过离线去模糊 3DGS 方法，同时运行速度快 15--150 倍；在线运行时质量具竞争力，且比在线 3DGS 基线少约 20% 的高斯。

Conclusion: RoMo-GS 证明了通过解析模糊建模、受控高斯增长和联合在线优化，可在机器人平台上实现高质量、高效的在线 3DGS 建图。

Abstract: Online 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) is emerging as a practical way to build a representation for robotics applications. However, deploying it on real-world platforms exposes multiple challenges. Rapid maneuvers of a robot induce motion-blurred images, which corrupt the reconstructed geometry and cause camera pose drift. Furthermore, the number of Gaussians grows without bound during continuous online mapping. In this study, we present RoMo-GS, an online 3DGS system designed to address these challenges. Instead of averaging N discrete sub-frames as in prior motion blur-aware 3DGS, we analytically integrate each projected Gaussian over the exposure interval, yielding an O(1) complexity per Gaussian. Building on this, we bound the Gaussians' growth with a Hessian-based pruning score, while jointly refining pose, velocity, and Gaussians with Adam and an online LM step over the Gaussians. On multiple public datasets and a self-collected dataset, RoMo-GS matches or exceeds offline deblurring 3DGS methods in rendering quality while running 15--150x faster, and operates online at competitive quality with about 20% fewer Gaussians than the online 3DGS baseline.

</details>


### [111] [Refuse the Harm, Complete the Task via Graph-of-Rules Planning for LLM-Enabled Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24603)
*Ali Taheri,Alireza Taban,Qizhou Wang,Wanhua Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出“安全任务完成”问题：LLM机器人需拒绝复合指令中的有害部分并完成合法部分；为此提出多智能体规则图规划框架RuleGraph，在多个推理与验证阶段分布式执行安全约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在具身AI中的部署面临越狱攻击等安全风险，现有推理时防御多针对含单一危险因素的短指令，难以处理安全与不安全子任务交织、危险步骤被隐藏或将多个安全动作组合成不安全结果的复合指令，单一集中式安全决策易漏检或误拒整个指令。

Method: RuleGraph采用五个协作智能体：将通用安全原则落地为具体环境规则、结合上下文评估指令子任务、从合法子任务推导安全目标；随后将规划组织为规则特定节点图，高层节点继承父节点规则的并集，候选计划逐步验证与聚合，直到单一计划满足所有相关规则，从而降低对单一集中式安全判断的依赖。

Result: 在两个环境的两个基准上，以攻击成功率ASR和任务成功率TSR评估纯攻击与复合指令；RuleGraph在两个基准上均将ASR降至0%，并在所有方法中取得最高TSR，RoboGuard上达98.57%，长时程复合指令上达84.54%。

Conclusion: RuleGraph通过多智能体与规则图规划将安全执行分散到多个阶段，可在拒绝有害子任务的同时完成合法任务，在复合指令场景下同时实现更低攻击成功率和更高任务成功率。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used in embodied AI for robotic manipulation and planning, but their deployment in physical systems exposes vulnerabilities such as jailbreak attacks that induce unsafe behavior. Existing inference-time defenses are mainly evaluated on short instructions containing a single hazard, whereas realistic tasks may interleave safe and unsafe sub-tasks, conceal hazardous steps, or combine individually safe actions into unsafe outcomes. Such compound instructions make it harder for a single safety decision to distinguish hazardous sub-tasks from legitimate ones, risking either overlooking the hazard or rejecting the whole instruction. We introduce safe task completion, a task that requires an LLM-enabled robot to refuse the harmful part of an instruction while completing its legitimate part. To address this problem, we propose RuleGraph, a multi-agent Graph-of-Rules planning framework that distributes safety enforcement across multiple reasoning and verification stages. Five cooperating agents are used to ground general safety principles into world-specific rules, assess the instruction's sub-tasks in context, and derive a safe goal from the legitimate ones. Planning toward this goal is then organized as a graph of rule-specific nodes, where higher-level nodes inherit the union of their parents' rules, and candidate plans are progressively verified and aggregated until a single plan satisfies every relevant rule. In this way, RuleGraph reduces reliance on a single centralized safety decision. We evaluate RuleGraph on pure-attack and compound instructions across two benchmarks in two environments using Attack Success Rate (ASR) and Task Success Rate (TSR). RuleGraph reduces ASR to 0% on both benchmarks while achieving the highest TSR among all methods, reaching 98.57% on the RoboGuard benchmark and 84.54% on our long-horizon compound instructions.

</details>


### [112] [LIBERO-Pro2.0: A Benchmark for Evaluating the Robustness of Embodied Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24587)
*Xueyang Zhou,Yangming Xu,Qianjiang Li,Guiyao Tie,Junhao Wu,Ziheng Huang,Guowen Zhang,Zhijie Yuan,Di Wu,Duanfeng Chu,Pan Zhou,Lichao Sun,Yongchao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 LIBERO-Pro2.0 鲁棒性基准，系统评估具身基础模型在分布偏移下的表现，发现现有策略在运行时扰动下平均性能下降 26.5 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LIBERO 上的强性能可能源于利用基准特定规律，而非真正的具身泛化能力，因此需要系统评估模型在多样运行条件下的鲁棒性。

Method: 构建包含 22 种静态扰动和 20 种运行时干预的基准，覆盖 Condition、Environment、Observation、Execution、Robot State Configuration 和 Language 六个维度，形成 1,648 个经校验的偏移任务实例；收集 39,390 条模型生成与人类演示轨迹，并进行六轮人工审查与修正；评估 17 种代表性机器人策略，包括视觉-语言-动作模型、世界-动作模型和面向鲁棒性的策略。

Result: 所有评估范式均存在显著鲁棒性差距；在运行时分布偏移下，模型平均性能下降 26.5 个百分点，个别下降达 32.5 个百分点。

Conclusion: 名义基准性能与分布偏移下的鲁棒性之间存在明显差距，凸显了对具身基础模型进行系统鲁棒性评估的必要性。

Abstract: LIBERO has emerged as a widely adopted benchmark for evaluating robotic manipulation policies. However, strong performance on LIBERO does not necessarily indicate robust embodied capabilities, as models may exploit benchmark-specific regularities rather than generalize to diverse operating conditions. In this work, we introduce LIBERO-Pro2.0, a comprehensive benchmark for systematically evaluating the robustness of embodied foundation models under distribution shifts. LIBERO-Pro2.0 encompasses 22 static perturbations and 20 runtime interventions across six dimensions: Condition, Environment, Observation, Execution, Robot State Configuration, and Language, yielding 1,648 validated shifted task instances. To support robustness-oriented learning, we further collect 39,390 training trajectories through model-generated and human demonstrations. All shifted task instances undergo six rounds of iterative human review and correction to ensure physical plausibility and task validity. We evaluate 17 representative robot policies spanning vision-language-action models, world-action models, and robustness-oriented policies. Our results reveal substantial robustness gaps across all evaluated paradigms: under runtime distribution shifts, the evaluated models exhibit an average performance drop of 26.5 percentage points, with individual drops reaching 32.5 points. These findings highlight the gap between nominal benchmark performance and robustness under distribution shifts, underscoring the need for systematic robustness evaluation of embodied foundation models.

</details>


### [113] [LIBERO-Dynamic: Benchmarking Robotic Manipulation in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24579)
*Shaojie Ren,Jingwen Sun,Zezhi Liu,Hongjin Chen,Xin Jin,Wei Zou,Zhibo Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LIBERO-Dynamic 将 40 个 LIBERO 任务扩展为动态目标操作基准，显式建模目标运动、推理延迟与动作执行时序，用于评估和改进移动目标下的机器人策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作仿真基准通常保持目标物体静态，并将策略推理视为瞬时完成，掩盖了目标在计算期间如何演化以及执行动作时观测已过时的问题。

Method: 通过接触摩擦驱动目标沿三类轨迹族和不同速度层级运动，并按校准的模型特定推理延迟推进物理世界；同一机制支持回放已验证静态示范进行训练，并在线补偿目标位移，生成 9000 条质量过滤的动态轨迹。评估 6 个预训练策略，每配置 1800 个动态条件，比较执行策略与动作时域，并用动态/混合数据微调 3 种架构。

Result: 更快运动普遍降低所有策略性能但程度不一；执行时序和时域会改变同一策略行为；异步重规划仅在动作队列避免下溢时受益于频繁反馈；动态示范在至少一种配方下提升每种架构，增益随时间条件、数据组成和训练预算变化；小规模物理实验显示与仿真一致的任务相关执行策略差异。

Conclusion: LIBERO-Dynamic 使目标运动、推理延迟和动作执行成为显式且可控的变量，为移动目标操作中的策略评估与改进提供基准。

Abstract: Most simulation benchmarks for robotic manipulation keep target objects static and treat policy inference as instantaneous. These assumptions simplify evaluation but hide how a moving target evolves during computation and how stale an observation is when an action is executed. We introduce LIBERO-Dynamic, a dynamic extension of all 40 LIBERO tasks. It drives objects through contact friction along three trajectory families and speed tiers, while advancing the physical world according to calibrated, model-specific inference latency. The same mechanism supports training by replaying verified static demonstrations and compensating robot actions online for target displacement, producing 9,000 quality-filtered dynamic trajectories. We evaluate six pretrained policies on 1,800 dynamic conditions per configuration, compare execution strategies and action horizons with fixed checkpoints, and fine-tune three architectures using dynamic and mixed data. Faster motion degrades every policy but not uniformly; execution timing and horizon can change the behavior of the same policy; and asynchronous replanning benefits from frequent feedback only when the action queue avoids underflow. Dynamic demonstrations improve each architecture under at least one recipe, with gains varying by temporal conditioning, data composition, and training budget. A small-scale physical study shows task-dependent execution-strategy differences consistent with simulation. LIBERO-Dynamic makes object motion, inference latency, and action execution explicit and controllable for evaluating and improving robot policies in moving-target manipulation.

</details>


### [114] [Retrieval-triggered Action Projection Adjustment for Frozen VLA Policies: An Empirical Study and Failure Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24573)
*Jinming Wang,Ruiheng Zhang,Yong Wang,Zipan Xiong,Keyuan Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出检索触发投影校正（RTPC），一种用于冻结OpenVLA策略的可审计外部记忆包装器，并在CALVIN单任务操作中评估其效果与失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略无法在不更新模型的情况下利用此前遇到的失败经验，因此需要一种外部记忆机制来干预和纠正策略行为。

Method: RTPC检索压力采集的局部深度记录，仅对投影施加平移校正，同时保持旋转和夹爪命令不变；在CALVIN单任务上使用主评估队列和独立复现队列进行评估。

Result: 主评估中RTPC降低了基于深度的近碰撞或停滞代理事件率，但未改变任务成功数；独立复现未重现该降低，且检索到的校正方向与随机同族方向不可区分。分析指出端点耦合、批次敏感性和缺乏触发匹配的无记忆基线限制了将效应归因于检索式校正。

Conclusion: 这些发现未证明物理安全性提升或记忆特异性收益，但提供了针对冻结VLA策略的检索条件干预的实证评估与失败分析。

Abstract: Frozen Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies cannot incorporate previously encountered failures without updating the underlying model. We present retrieval-triggered projection correction (RTPC), an auditable external-memory wrapper for frozen OpenVLA policies that retrieves stress-collected local-depth records and applies translation-only projection correction while keeping rotation and gripper commands unchanged. RTPC is evaluated on CALVIN single-task manipulation using primary and independent replication cohorts. In the primary evaluation, RTPC reduced a depth-based near-collision or stall proxy event rate without changing task-success count. However, this reduction was not reproduced in the independent replication, and retrieved correction directions were not distinguishable from random same-family directions. Analysis identifies endpoint coupling, batch sensitivity, and the lack of a trigger-matched memory-free baseline as limitations for attributing effects to retrieval-based correction. These findings do not establish physical safety improvement or memory-specific benefits, but provide an empirical evaluation and failure analysis of retrieval-conditioned intervention for frozen VLA policies.

</details>


### [115] [SocialCoAct: Enabling and Benchmarking Egocentric Social Co-Action for VLM-Based Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24559)
*Jiancong Ma,Kun Li,Jin Jiang,Shengcai Liao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 SocialCoAct，一个以智能体为中心的 VLM 自我中心社交协同动作框架，并构建 SocialCoAct-Bench 用于评估人形机器人在多人社交场景中协调语音与动作的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常将预生成语音直接映射为连续动作轨迹，对复杂社交环境感知不足，且缺乏显式的高层语义动作规划，难以支持有效社交互动。

Method: 将自我中心社交协同动作建模为端到端智能体任务，提出 SocialCoAct-Agent，把自我中心观测与对话历史转化为语言回复和原子动作序列，并将每个动作与对应话语片段对齐，在 MuJoCo 和实体人形机器人上执行；同时基于自然多人交互的连续自我中心记录构建包含 423 道人工校验多选题的 SocialCoAct-Bench，覆盖长片段理解、说话者意图理解和协同动作规划三个子任务。

Result: 在多种闭源与开放权重 VLM（含通用和自我中心模型）上，最高总体准确率为 77.54%，困难子集仅 46.51%；扩展推理使 Qwen3-VL-2B 和 Qwen3-VL-4B 分别提升 19.62 和 17.02 个百分点，但推理吞吐分别下降 53.6 倍和 20.2 倍；视觉输入消融显示视觉上下文收益因模型而异。

Conclusion: SocialCoAct 为开发和评估能够在复杂社交互动中协调上下文感知语音与动作的 VLM 人形机器人智能体提供了基础。

Abstract: Enabling humanoid robots to participate effectively in social interactions is important for their integration into human-centered environments. Existing methods, however, typically map pre-generated speech directly to continuous motion trajectories but have limited awareness of complex social environments and lack explicit high-level semantic action planning. We introduce SocialCoAct, a unified agent-centered framework for VLM-based egocentric social co-action. We formulate egocentric social co-action as an end-to-end agent task and develop SocialCoAct-Agent, which transforms egocentric observations and dialogue history into verbal responses and atomic action sequences, aligns each action with its corresponding utterance span, and executes the resulting behaviors in MuJoCo and on a physical humanoid robot. To evaluate the VLM capabilities required by this task, we construct SocialCoAct-Bench from continuous egocentric recordings of natural multi-party interactions. The benchmark comprises 423 human-verified multiple-choice questions across three subtasks: Long Episode Understanding (LEU), Speaker Intention Understanding (SIU), and Coordinated Action Planning (CAP). Across a diverse set of closed-source and open-weight VLMs, including general and egocentric models, the highest overall accuracy is 77.54%, while accuracy on the hard subset reaches only 46.51%. Extended reasoning improves Qwen3-VL-2B and Qwen3-VL-4B by 19.62 and 17.02 percentage points, respectively, but reduces inference throughput by 53.6$\times$ and 20.2$\times$. Visual-input ablations further show that the benefits of visual context vary across models. SocialCoAct provides a foundation for developing and evaluating VLM-based humanoid agents that coordinate context-aware speech and action in complex social interactions. The codebase and benchmark will be made publicly available upon publication.

</details>


### [116] [ContactBench 1.0: A Benchmark for Everyday Contact-Rich Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24512)
*Jingyi Xiang,Yixuan Wang,Jeannette Bohg,Wenzhen Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ContactBench 1.0 是一个面向接触丰富操作的可扩展基准，基于八类日常操作技能构建36个参考任务和40余个可3D打印复现、并配有可调阻力仿真模型的物体资产，用于可复现评估策略泛化与接触感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富操作对日常活动至关重要，但其接触行为多样且跨实验室任务难以复现，导致机器人相关能力评估困难。

Method: 作者从日常人类操作中归纳八类重复出现的技能类别，构建ContactBench 1.0；该基准包含36个参考任务和40余个物体资产，资产捕捉任务相关几何与接触行为，支持消费级3D打印复现，并配有带可调阻力负载的仿真模型；通过受控任务变化评估策略泛化，并在六个基准任务上评测近期基于视觉和视觉-触觉的策略学习方法。

Result: 评测结果显示，所评估的纯视觉策略难以感知或响应接触事件，而所评估的视觉-触觉策略并未显著提升性能。

Conclusion: ContactBench 1.0 通过共享任务及物理与仿真资产，为接触丰富操作研究建立可复现评估基础，并已开源。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation is essential to everyday activities, yet evaluating robots' ability to perform these activities remains challenging because of their diverse contact behaviors and the difficulty of reproducing tasks across laboratories. Drawing on observations of everyday human manipulation, we identify eight recurring skill categories that provide a practical framework for studying these capabilities. We introduce ContactBench 1.0, an extensible benchmark that instantiates these skills through 36 reference tasks and more than 40 object assets. The assets capture task-relevant geometry and contact behavior, support reproducible fabrication using consumer-grade 3D printers, and are paired with simulation models with tunable resistance loads. Controlled task variations enable evaluation of policy generalization. We evaluate recent vision-based and vision-tactile policy learning methods on six benchmark tasks. The results reveal that the evaluated vision-only policies struggle to perceive or react to contact events, while the evaluated visual-tactile policies do not meaningfully improve performance. By providing shared tasks and physical and simulated assets, ContactBench 1.0 establishes a foundation for reproducible evaluation of contact-rich manipulation research. The benchmark is open-sourced at this https URL

</details>


### [117] [GazeAct: A Human-Gaze-Annotated Manipulation Dataset for Attention-Regularized Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24507)
*Anupam Pani,Wang Yuan,Qifan Wang,Yanchao Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GazeAct 提出一个将真实人类注视与多相机机器人观测同步的机器人操作数据集和训练框架，通过在训练时用注视导出的目标正则化语言条件视觉注意力，在不增加推理时注视输入的情况下提升多视图 VLA 策略的视觉 grounding 和操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 策略需要将语言指令正确 grounding 到对象、接触点和目标位置，而人类注视是自然的 grounding 信号；现有机器人数据集很少将真实人类注视与现代策略使用的多相机观测配对。

Method: 采用屏幕式遥操作，在操作员看到的机器人相机画面上直接记录注视，构建 GazeAct 数据集：40 个任务、5 个领域、12000 条演示，300 Hz 注视与图像、本体感知和动作同步。将注视从操作员选定视角迁移到其余同步相机，生成跨策略视觉输入空间一致的目标；训练时用这些注视导出的目标正则化语言条件视觉注意力，推理时无需注视。

Result: 在 18 个真实任务上，平均成功率从 48.1% 提升到 58.6%，最大提升 25 个百分点；消融表明注视作为软监督最有效，且真实人类注视比通用注意力塑形先验提供更强注意力目标。增益可迁移到 LIBERO-Spatial（57.9% 到 69.6%）和不同 VLA 模型。

Conclusion: 在机器人观测空间中采集的注视可作为有效的训练时目标，用于改善 VLA 操作策略的视觉 grounding，并具有跨任务和跨模型的迁移效果。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies must ground language instructions in the right objects, contact points, and target locations for reliable manipulation. Human gaze provides a natural signal for this grounding, but existing robot datasets rarely pair real human gaze with the multi-camera observations used by modern policies. We introduce GazeAct, a gaze-equipped real-robot manipulation dataset and training framework for multi-view VLA policies. Using screen-based teleoperation, we record gaze directly on the robot camera feed viewed by the operator, yielding 12000 demonstrations across 40 tasks and five domains with 300 Hz gaze synchronized with images, proprioception, and actions. We transfer gaze from the operator-selected view to the remaining synchronized cameras, producing spatially consistent targets across the policy's visual inputs. We then use these gaze-derived targets to regularize language-conditioned visual attention during training while keeping inference gaze-free. Across 18 real-world tasks, our method improves average success from 48.1% to 58.6%, with gains up to 25 percentage points. Ablations show that gaze works best as soft supervision and that real human gaze provides stronger attention targets than generic attention-shaping priors. The gains transfer to LIBERO-Spatial (57.9% to 69.6%) and different VLA this http URL results show that gaze collected in the robot observation space can provide effective training-time targets for improving visual grounding in VLA manipulation.

</details>


### [118] [Touch Where It Matters: Learning Local Tactile Policies for Fine-Grained Gripper Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24485)
*Mingyuan Ba,Yanan Zhou,James Zhao,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出ContactPolicy：在视觉基础策略控制机械臂时，用学习型局部触觉控制器接管夹爪，实现接触丰富任务中的细粒度夹爪调节，并通过Intent策略管理控制权交接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉模仿策略能组织任务运动，但接触丰富物体操作需要超越粗略开合的夹爪调整；因此将细粒度夹爪控制建模为持续任务执行中的局部反馈问题，并为触觉反馈分配专用控制角色。

Method: ContactPolicy融合触觉与力历史及当前机器人状态，联合预测夹爪增量及其调制，并根据每个新观测更新夹爪目标；视觉基础策略继续控制机械臂，Intent策略负责视觉基础策略与ContactPolicy之间的控制权交接。

Result: 在开容器、物体处理和工具操作的真实世界实验中，方法相比Visuomotor Policy和Tactile-Force Policy基线提高任务成功率；执行分析显示夹爪可在任务中调整接触，包括目标保持期和交互条件变化时的细粒度变化；臂状态消融与失败分析进一步考察执行上下文对局部调节的支持。

Conclusion: 结果支持在视觉操作中使用任务局部触觉策略进行细粒度夹爪控制，表明触觉局部反馈可有效补充视觉模仿策略以应对接触丰富操作。

Abstract: Visual imitation policies can organise task motion, yet contact-rich object use demands gripper adjustments beyond coarse opening and closing. We study fine-grained gripper control as a local feedback problem within ongoing task execution. Our approach assigns tactile feedback a dedicated control role during contact-rich task stages. We introduce ContactPolicy, a learned local controller that takes over the gripper while a visual base policy continues to control the arm. ContactPolicy combines tactile and force histories with current robot state to jointly predict gripper increments and their modulation, updating the gripper target from each new observation. An Intent policy manages control handover between the visual base policy and ContactPolicy. Real-world experiments on container opening, object handling, and tool operation show improved task success over both the Visuomotor Policy and Tactile-Force Policy baselines. Execution analysis illustrates how the gripper adjusts contact within an ongoing task, including periods of target retention and fine-grained changes as interaction conditions evolve. Arm-state ablations and failure analysis further examine how execution context supports local regulation. These results support task-local tactile policies for fine-grained gripper control within visual manipulation.

</details>


### [119] [Tension Tells the Tail: Cable-Obstacle Interaction Sensing for Tethered Mobile Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24449)
*Ryuki Shimada,Koki Suzuki,Genya Ishigami*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究利用缆绳张力作为传感模态，判断系绳移动机器人接触到的障碍物是否可移动，以评估缆绳-障碍物交互风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缆绳与环境交互会造成障碍物意外位移、缆绳卡挂或缠绕甚至损伤，导致任务失败，因此需要在线评估单个缆绳-障碍物接触的风险，为缆绳感知自主导航提供基础。

Method: 开展系绳移动机器人实验，改变机器人速度、障碍物与缆绳接触角、障碍物质量和障碍物侧面材料并采集张力数据；从张力曲线中选择6个特征，基于117次带标签试验训练逻辑回归分类器。

Result: 障碍物可移动性可由初始张力上升以及峰值后张力是否松弛或维持高位来表征；所提方法在接触后行驶30 cm时区分可移动与不可移动障碍物的平衡准确率达到95.5%。

Conclusion: 结果表明缆绳张力可用于缆绳-障碍物交互风险评估，并朝着在线、缆绳感知的自主导航迈进一步。

Abstract: Tethered mobile robots can sustain long-duration operation through continuous power supply and stable communication via their cables. However, cable-environment interaction causes unintended obstacle displacement, cable snagging or entanglement, and even cable damage, leading to mission failure. To assess the risk of individual cable-obstacle contacts, this study investigates cable tension as a sensing modality for estimating whether a contacted obstacle is movable or non-movable. We conducted experiments with a tethered mobile robot and collected tension data while varying robot velocity, contact angle between the obstacle and cable, obstacle mass, and obstacle side-surface material. The results show that obstacle movability is characterized by the initial tension rise and, more importantly, by whether the tension relaxes or remains high after its peak. Based on these observations, we select six features from the tension profiles and train a logistic-regression classifier using 117 labeled trials. The proposed method distinguishes movable and non-movable cases with a balanced accuracy of 95.5% after 30 cm of post-contact travel. These results demonstrate the potential of cable tension for risk assessment of cable-obstacle interactions and provide a step toward online, cable-aware autonomous navigation.

</details>


### [120] [Gen2Dex: Learning Dexterous Manipulation from Simulation-Initialized Generated Video Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24392)
*Xiaowen Qiu,Yian Wang,Haoxiang You,Chuang Gan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出端到端框架，将基于仿真生成的人类操作视频转换为可物理执行的灵巧机器人演示，并在仿真与真实机器人上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视频演示富含灵巧操作策略，但仿真生成视频存在几何畸变、接触不准等物理不一致，导致恢复的运动难以直接用于机器人执行。

Method: 利用已知仿真场景信息，通过基于模型的目标跟踪、稠密基础模型特征和基于深度的手-物对齐恢复人手运动；随后重定向，并用残差强化学习进行物理接地，结合反向课程和物理有效重置状态缓存，在纠正不完美参考的同时保留演示策略。

Result: 在干净的ARCTIC演示上达到与现有灵巧重定向方法有竞争力的性能，对生成视频提取的参考更鲁棒；在仿真中完成多种刚性与铰接物体操作任务，并将优化轨迹开环迁移到真实灵巧机器人。

Conclusion: 该框架能够将存在物理不一致的仿真视频参考转化为可执行的灵巧机器人演示，兼顾演示策略保持与物理可行性，并具备仿真到真实的迁移潜力。

Abstract: Human video demonstrations provide rich manipulation strategies for dexterous manipulation learning. We study a setting in which a fully specified simulated scene is used to generate videos that specify the demonstrated manipulation behavior. This setup provides access to scene information such as object geometry, articulation, and camera parameters, while allowing direct control over task and scene configuration. However, generated videos may contain geometric distortions, inaccurate contacts, and other physical inconsistencies, making the recovered motion difficult to execute directly on a robot. We present an end-to-end framework for converting such simulation-grounded videos into physically executable dexterous robot demonstrations. Our perception pipeline leverages the known scene through model-based object tracking with dense foundation-model features and depth-based hand-object alignment. The recovered human motion is then retargeted and physically grounded using residual reinforcement learning, where a backward curriculum and a cache of physically valid reset states enable the policy to correct imperfect video-derived references while preserving the demonstrated manipulation strategy. On clean ARCTIC demonstrations, our method achieves competitive performance with prior dexterous retargeting approaches and shows greater robustness when applied to references extracted from generated videos. We further demonstrate diverse rigid and articulated manipulation tasks in simulation and successful open-loop transfer of the optimized trajectories to a real dexterous robot.

</details>


### [121] [Beyond Human-Derived Motion: Exploring Robot-Specific Execution for Construction Humanoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24312)
*MinJun Chang,Taehyung Kim,Yong K. Cho,Francis Baek*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究比较了人形机器人执行建筑搬运任务时，“人类衍生动量”与“机器人特定运动”在运动执行效率上的差异，发现后者显著更高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑生产率长期受限，人形机器人有望在为人设计的工作空间和工具环境中作业；但将人类示范动作迁移到机器人后，是否应完全复现人类运动，还是允许机器人利用自身形态生成更高效运动，尚需验证。

Method: 在成年尺寸Unitree H1-2上，人类衍生动量通过视觉捕捉与重定向生成；机器人特定运动则采用逆运动学与最小加加速度（minimum-jerk）转移，在到达相同目标的同时偏离示范姿态，并用RULA进行姿态风险评估。实验为抓取-堆叠序列，代表建筑材料搬运，包含四个匹配运动目标和每种条件三次试验。

Result: 机器人特定运动相比人类衍生动量：执行时间12.87 s对25.03 s，轨迹长度0.92 m对4.33 m，双臂机械功58.7 J对247.3 J。

Conclusion: 允许人形机器人偏离人类示范运动，可能为更高效的运动执行提供额外机会；但是否能转化为建筑生产率提升，仍需在更广泛建筑任务中验证。

Abstract: Construction productivity has remained a per- sistent challenge, motivating growing interest in humanoid robots that can operate in workspaces and with tools designed for human workers. Realizing this potential requires effective motion control, for which human-derived motion transfer has emerged as a promising approach by capturing and retargeting human demonstrations to humanoid robots. While promising, however, human-derived motion transfer may not fully define how a humanoid should ultimately execute the transferred motion. Once task-relevant motion information has been con- veyed, the humanoid may also exploit its own embodiment through motions that depart from the human demonstration. This study examines whether such Robot-Specific Motion offers an advantage in motion execution efficiency over Human- Derived Motion. On an adult-sized Unitree H1-2, Human- Derived Motion was generated through vision-based capture and retargeting, whereas Robot-Specific Motion used inverse kinematics and minimum-jerk transfers to reach the same targets while departing from the demonstrated posture, as verified using Rapid Upper Limb Assessment (RULA). Across a pick-and-stack sequence representing construction material handling, with four matched motion targets and three trials per condition, Robot-Specific Motion achieved 12.87 s versus 25.03 s in execution time, 0.92 m versus 4.33 m in trajectory length, and 58.7 J versus 247.3 J in two-arm mechanical work. These findings suggest that allowing humanoids to depart from demonstrated human motion may offer additional opportunities for more efficient motion execution, while broader construction tasks are needed to determine whether such differences trans- late into productivity gains.

</details>


### [122] [ACTS: Learning Visuo-Tactile World Models from Human Physical Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24285)
*Yuxiang Ma,Weihan Xu,Pengfei Ye,Anna Mei,Yuxin Ray Song,Tingle Li,Haonan Chen,Edward H. Adelson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ACTS：仅用人类交互数据训练的视触觉世界模型，可将接触动力学迁移到机器人；触觉动力学迁移较可靠，视觉外观迁移较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富操作的世界模型需要捕捉接触形成、滑动和释放的数据，但机器人遥操作采集慢、常缺力反馈且受夹爪几何限制。作者探究能否从人类物理交互中学习这些动力学并迁移到机器人。

Method: 构建React数据集：操作者手持GelSight传感器，记录第三人称与腕部视频、触觉图像、6-DoF运动及触觉法向力，共4.9小时、双臂、三物体。将触觉传感器处动作表示为运动加力变化，形成人类采集与机器人执行共享的动作空间。ACTS联合预测未来场景、腕部和触觉观测。

Result: 在留出人类数据上，ACTS的场景视图预测比在React上从零训练的两种视触觉世界模型中更强者的PSNR高0.7-2.0 dB。机器人遥操作rollout中不经微调，预测触觉印迹仍随记录接触出现和消失。视觉迁移较不稳健：PushT上相机预测优于帧复制，Rope上混合，Motherboard上低于帧复制；腕部视图可能模糊或幻觉结构。

Conclusion: 从人类到机器人，接触动力学比视觉外观迁移更可靠；ACTS表明仅用人类交互数据学习触觉接触世界模型具有可行性。

Abstract: Abstract Action-conditioned world models for contact-rich manipulation require data that captures how contact forms, slides, and releases under varying force. Such data are slow to collect through robot teleoperation, which often lacks force feedback and is constrained by gripper geometry. We ask whether these dynamics can instead be learned from human physical interaction and transferred to a robot. We present ACTS, a visuo-tactile world model trained only on human interaction data. Operators manipulate handheld GelSight sensors while we record third-person and wrist video, tactile images, 6-DoF sensor motion, and tactile-derived normal force, yielding React, a 4.9-hour bimanual dataset across three objects. We represent actions at the tactile sensor as motion augmented by force change, providing a shared action space for human collection and robot execution. ACTS jointly predicts future scene, wrist, and tactile observations. On held-out human data, ACTS improves scene-view prediction by 0.7-2.0 dB PSNR over the stronger of two visuo-tactile world models trained from scratch on React. Without fine-tuning, in the robot teleoperation rollouts we inspected, predicted tactile imprints still appear and disappear with recorded contact. Visual transfer is less robust: camera predictions exceed frame copying on PushT, are mixed on Rope, and fall below it on Motherboard, while wrist views can blur or hallucinate structure. These results suggest that contact dynamics transfer more reliably than visual appearance from human to robot.

</details>


### [123] [How exposed is work to robotics? Occupational, industry, and geographic measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24267)
*Sebastian Sartor,Robert Seamans,Neil Thompson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文构建了首个基于能力映射、覆盖职业—行业—地理尺度的机器人及物理AI暴露指标，发现机器人暴露集中于体力密集职业和行业，且与生成式AI暴露模式不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI职业暴露指标多聚焦软件AI，机器人暴露常通过专利或安装数据代理，反映的是过去几十年的工业机器人；缺乏基于能力、覆盖职业/行业/地理的机器人与物理AI暴露测度。

Method: 沿用Felten等框架，从78个学术、产业和标准来源构建含8个领域16项能力的机器人能力分类体系；用大语言模型评估每项能力与52项O*NET工作能力的相关性；聚合得到774个职业的ROE、250个NAICS行业的RIE及县級RGE。

Result: 机器人暴露集中于建筑、农业、运输、生产等体力密集职业，以及伐木、采矿、卡车运输、仓储等行业。与生成式AI暴露不同，ROE在低工资、低教育要求、创造性能力内容较少的职业中更高。物理协作与辅助、地形移动、抓取与操作、任务与功能推理对暴露排名影响最大，但无单一能力主导指数。

Conclusion: 这些指标衡量技术相关性而非准备度、成本或采用情况，应视为物理自动化可能影响工作的上限估计。

Abstract: Exposure indices that map technical capabilities onto occupational abilities have become a standard tool for anticipating how AI may reshape work, but existing measures either cover software AI or proxy robotics through patents and installation data that reflect the industrial robots of past decades. We construct the first abilities-based exposure measures for robotics and physical AI, and the first to span occupations, industries, and geographies. Following the framework of Felten et al. (2018, 2021), we build a robotics capability taxonomy of 16 capabilities in eight domains from 78 academic, industry, and standards sources, use a large language model to score the relatedness of each capability to the 52 ONET work abilities, and aggregate to Robotics Occupational Exposure (ROE) for 774 occupations, Robotics Industry Exposure (RIE) for 250 NAICS industries, and county-level Robotics Geographic Exposure (RGE). We find that robotics exposure concentrates in physically intensive work such as construction, farming, transportation, and production, and in industries such as logging, mining, trucking, and warehousing. Unlike generative-AI exposure, ROE is higher in occupations with lower wages, lower education requirements, and less creative-ability content. Physical collaboration and assistance, terrain-based locomotion, grasping and handling, and task and functional reasoning move the exposure ranking most, yet no single capability carries the index. Because the measures capture technical relatedness rather than readiness, cost, or adoption, they should be read as an upper bound on where physical automation could affect work.

</details>


### [124] [Evaluating Lightweight Contact Feedback in Dual-Operator Collaborative Humanoid Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24257)
*Austin Graves,Chuizheng Kong,Yunho Cho,Yuanshao Yang,Advaith Nidumukkala,Shreyas Kousik,Mohsen Moghaddam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出XRT扩展现实双人形机器人遥操作框架，通过指尖振动触觉和/或听觉反馈传递机器人-物体接触信息，并在16组双人被试的协作抬箱翻转任务中比较仅视觉与三种反馈条件。结果显示指尖振动触觉主观评价优于开放式声化，但对任务绩效的影响未检测到显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双操作者人形机器人遥操作支持协作操作，但操作者缺乏物理交互中通常可得的机器人-物体接触直接感知。研究旨在通过轻量触觉/听觉反馈补足这一感知缺口，并构建同步双人形机器人测试平台。

Method: 提出XRT框架，将机器人指尖接触通过振动触觉和/或听觉反馈返回给操作者；构建面向两名操作者的同步双人形机器人测试床。16组双人被试在平衡化被试内设计中完成协作木箱抬起与翻转任务，条件包括仅视觉及三种反馈条件，并验证反馈通道送达。

Result: 第一，所有32名操作者均将指尖定位振动触觉显示排在开放式声化之上，18人认为其最有效，且触觉线索更易感知和解释。第二，完成时间、失败、工作负荷、不适和伙伴意识未检测到条件差异，但完成时间区间较宽且较小效应未解析。第三，练习效应显著，尤其较难的翻转任务：完成时间随区块改善，双人组内尝试间变异（SD×1.51）大于最大条件对比（×1.16）。探索性分析未发现反馈条件间即时抓握或运动响应差异。

Conclusion: 对新手协作遥操作而言，指尖振动触觉线索在主观有效性和可解释性上优于听觉线索，但其对任务绩效的影响仍未确定。

Abstract: Dual-operator humanoid teleoperation enables collaborative manipulation through two separate robots, but operators lack direct awareness of robot-object contact that is normally available during physical interaction. To address this, we present eXtended Reality Teleoperation (XRT), an extended reality teleoperation framework that returns a robot's fingertip contact through lightweight vibrotactile and/or auditory feedback, and a synchronized dual-humanoid testbed built on it for two operators. Sixteen dyads performed a collaborative crate lift-and-flip under vision only and the three feedback conditions in a counterbalanced within-subject design with verified feedback-channel delivery. Three results follow. First, operators rated our finger-localized vibrotactile display as more effective than our open-ear sonification: all 32 ranked it above the sonification, 18 ranked it most effective, and its cues were rated far easier to perceive and interpret than the auditory cues. Second, completion time, failures, workload, discomfort, and partner awareness showed no detected condition differences, with wide completion-time intervals and smaller meaningful effects unresolved. Third, performance changed substantially with practice, especially on the harder flip-over task: completion time improved across blocks, and attempt-to-attempt variation within a dyad (SD x1.51) was larger than the largest condition contrast (x1.16). Exploratory analyses detected no difference in immediate grip or motion responses between feedback conditions. For novice collaborative teleoperation, our fingertip vibrotactile cues were rated more effective and easier to interpret than our auditory cues, while their effect on task performance remains unresolved.

</details>


### [125] [TrajEdit: Real-Time Flow-Matching Trajectory Adaptation via FlowEdit and CBF Safety Filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24256)
*Wondmgezahu Teshome,Arya Honarpisheh,Octavia Camps,Mario Sznaier*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 TrajEdit：一种结合流匹配、轨迹编辑与安全过滤的分层轨迹规划器，用于动态追捕者场景下的实时避障与逃逸。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当动态障碍物主动追捕自主体时，环境快速变化，安全且实时的运动规划十分困难，传统每步从头重规划计算开销大。

Method: TrajEdit 在名义情况下沿当前计划执行；当追捕者接近时，仅通过 FlowEdit 对剩余轨迹进行自适应编辑，而非完全重规划；若对抗智能体进入安全阈值，则回退到控制屏障函数（CBF）安全滤波器。

Result: 在带静态障碍物和对抗追捕者的二维追捕-逃逸任务中，TrajEdit 取得高成功率、低捕获率和低碰撞率，并相较完整重规划基线显著节省计算时间；使用 FlowEdit 进行轨迹编辑可在保持相近成功率的同时降低计算时间。

Conclusion: TrajEdit 通过分层轨迹编辑与安全过滤，在追捕-逃逸场景中实现了高效、安全且实时的运动规划。

Abstract: Safe, real-time motion planning is especially difficult when a dynamic obstacle actively pursues the ego agent, requiring rapid adaptation as the environment changes. We present TrajEdit, a hierarchical trajectory planner for pursuit-evasion that combines flow matching, trajectory editing, and safety filtering. Rather than regenerating trajectories from scratch at every step, TrajEdit follows the current plan under nominal conditions and adapts its remaining portion through FlowEdit as the pursuer approaches, and falls back to a control barrier function safety filter if the adversarial agent is within a safety threshold. Our use of FlowEdit for trajectory editing reduces computation time while maintaining a comparable success rate. We evaluate TrajEdit in 2D pursuit-evasion with static obstacles and an adversarial pursuer, demonstrating high success rates with low capture and collision rates, while achieving computational time savings over full replanning baselines.

</details>


### [126] [Towards Reinforcement Learning for Space Robotics: Fast Training and Reliable Real-World Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24241)
*Abhishek Naik,Michael Wu,Michael O'Sullivan,Colin Bellinger,Yunli Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作利用快速仿真训练框架和强化学习，在单块消费级GPU上不到20分钟训练出四足机器人控制策略，并可可靠迁移到真实世界的多种地形，包括月面模拟设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向更具能力和鲁棒性的太空机器人系统开发需求，降低强化学习训练的硬件门槛，并解决仿真策略向真实机器人可靠迁移的问题。

Method: 构建基于仿真的快速训练框架，使用强化学习学习四足机器人控制策略；硬件需求极低，并将仿真中学习到的策略部署到真实四足机器人，在多种真实地形及月面模拟设施中测试。

Result: 四足机器人能够可靠穿越多种真实世界地形，并在月面模拟设施中完成测试；训练仅需单块消费级GPU上少于20分钟，且仿真学习策略可可靠迁移到真实世界。

Conclusion: 结果表明，快速仿真训练与强化学习能够高效开发可迁移的机器人控制策略，展示了强化学习在开发更强大、更鲁棒太空机器人系统中的重要潜力。

Abstract: This work demonstrates the use of a fast simulation-based training framework to learn control policies using reinforcement learning. The hardware requirements are minimal and the simulation-learned policies transfer reliably to the real world. With this setup, we show that a quadruped robot can learn to traverse diverse real-world terrains reliably---including in a lunar-analog facility---after less than 20 minutes of training on a single consumer-grade GPU. These results provide a glimpse of a future where reinforcement learning is a crucial tool in developing more capable and robust space robotic systems. Video demonstrations are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [127] [Multimodal Interaction Estimation for Contact-Aware Adaptive Impedance Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24235)
*Mateusz Jaszczuk,Paris Perdikaris,Nadia Figueroa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种任务无关的交互估计与自适应控制框架，融合力历史与3D点云区分未知负载和人类推力，并实现安全柔顺的机器人操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人类中心环境中，机器人需同时补偿未知负载并在人机交互时保持柔顺；单一模态难以可靠区分负载与人类推挤：力信号混淆二者，视觉易受遮挡。

Method: 用基于Transformer的分类器融合力历史与3D点云，采用模态丢弃训练提升遮挡鲁棒性，并从仿真微调到硬件；分类结果驱动系统辨识以选择性补偿未知负载，驱动可变阻抗控制器在自适应与反应模式间切换，同时估计交互力用于人类意图估计等下游任务。

Result: 分类器达到91.89%准确率，在单一模态完全遮挡时仍保持90%以上；在外部扰动和未知负载下验证了单臂操作、模仿学习策略部署、意图驱动目标切换，以及两台Franka机械臂零样本双臂抬升未知质量箱体，展现对闭运动链和未见设置的泛化。

Conclusion: 该框架通过多模态交互估计与自适应控制实现了未知负载补偿、人机交互柔顺性和跨设置泛化，支持安全精确部署。

Abstract: With advancements in learning-based policies, robots are increasingly being deployed in human-centric environments, such as manufacturing floors and homes, where safe and precise operation requires compensating for unknown loads while remaining compliant during human interactions. Reliably distinguishing between these interaction types is challenging with a single sensing modality, as force signals cannot separate payloads from human pushes, and vision is susceptible to occlusions. We propose a task-agnostic interaction estimation and adaptive control framework that addresses this challenge by fusing force history and 3D point cloud through a transformer-based classifier. Trained with modality dropout for occlusion resilience and fine-tuned from simulation to hardware, the classifier achieves 91.89% accuracy and maintains above 90% under complete occlusion of one modality. The classifier drives a system identification framework that selectively compensates for unknown payloads and a variable-impedance controller that switches between adaptive and reactive modes, while simultaneously estimating interaction forces for downstream tasks, such as human intent estimation. We validate the framework under external perturbations and unknown loads across single-arm manipulation, deployment with an imitation-learning-based policy, intent-driven target switching, and zero-shot bimanual deployment on two Franka arms lifting a box of unknown mass, demonstrating generalization to closed kinematic chains and unseen setups, enabling safe and precise deployment.

</details>


### [128] [FLASH-CBF-RRT: Fast Lipschitz-Adaptive Safe Horizon CBF Steering for Certified Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24149)
*Mani Amani,Hesam Mojtahedi,Reza Akhavian,Nikolay Atanasov,Ki Myung Brian Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FLASH-CBF-RRT 通过闭式运动学碰撞间隙界计算 CBF 滤波速度可恒定施加的时间范围，实现自适应步长的局部转向，从而加速 RRT-Connect 运动规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速运动规划需要样本高效的探索和计算高效的局部转向；直线转向便宜但易因碰撞拒绝浪费样本，而基于 CBF 约束的 QP 控制器局部转向有效但顺序评估开销大。

Method: 提出 FLASH-CBF-RRT：利用碰撞间隙的闭式运动学界，计算 CBF 滤波速度可恒定应用的时间视界，减少 CBF 顺序评估；规划器采用近障碍短、空旷区长自适应转向步长，并在 OMPL 中作为 RRT-Connect 的扩展实现。

Result: 在 689 个 MotionBenchMaker 问题上，FLASH-CBF-RRT 达到与 RRT-Connect 相同的 100% 成功率，中位规划时间缩短 3.82 倍，比固定步长 CBF-RRT 快 3.88 倍；在 17 自由度移动机械臂硬件实验中规划快 2.2 倍、评估次数少 4.2 倍。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持高成功率的同时显著降低 CBF 局部转向的评估次数和规划时间，适用于需要高效局部转向的快速运动规划。

Abstract: Fast motion planning requires both sample-efficient exploration and computationally efficient local steering. Conventional straight-line steering is inexpensive but wastes samples by rejecting colliding extensions. Quadratic program controllers with control barrier function (CBF) constraints can be used to achieve effective local steering but can increase the planning time due to the expensive sequential evaluations. We develop a method to accelerate CBF steering by computing a time horizon within which CBF-filtered velocity may be applied constantly using closed-form kinematic bounds on collision-clearance. Our method, FLASH-CBF-RRT, significantly reduces the number of evaluations compared to prior CBF-RRT approaches because the planner can take adaptive steering step sizes that are shorter near obstacles and longer in open space. We implement this steering procedure as an extension of RRT-Connect in OMPL. Across 689 MotionBenchMaker problems, FLASH-CBF-RRT matches RRT-Connect's 100% success rate while reducing median planning time by 3.82x, and is 3.88x faster than fixed-step CBF-RRT. Hardware experiments on a 17-DoF mobile manipulator demonstrate 2.2x faster planning and 4.2x fewer evaluations. Videos and results are available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [129] [Rollbot 2: Agile Locomotion by a Single-Actuator Spherical Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24129)
*Jingxian Wang*,Chen Yu*,Sam Kriegman,Michael Rubenstein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Rollbot 2 在保持单执行器球形机器人设计的同时，通过重新设计质量分布、增加板载惯性/UWB 传感并采用学习控制，实现了接近直线滚动、自主寻标与路径跟随，显著提升速度与自主性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减少执行器数量可简化机器人硬件，但会失去独立操控输入；原版 Rollbot 依赖准稳定螺旋轨道转向，速度慢且需要外部动捕系统。

Method: Rollbot 2 保留单执行器架构，重新设计质量分布以实现近直线滚动和更直接的运动；利用板载惯性与超宽带传感支持学习转向、信标寻的与航点跟随，并通过单一电机速度指令控制。

Result: 机器人无需动捕即可自主接近 UWB 信标，以 0.87 m/s 完成 468 m 室内手动指令路线；在共享 4.8 m “N” 航点基准中 16 次试验全部完成，平均用时 10.8 s、平均时间偏差 7 cm，最快 8.0 s，较原版 Rollbot 的 564 s 快 70 倍以上（协议略有差异）。

Conclusion: 结果表明，机械重构、板载传感与基于学习的控制能够提升单执行器球形机器人的敏捷性和自主性。

Abstract: Reducing actuator count simplifies robot hardware but also removes independent inputs for maneuvering. The original Rollbot steered by modulating a quasi-stable helical orbit, but moved slowly and relied on external motion capture. Rollbot 2 preserves the single-actuator design while its redesigned mass distribution enables near-straight rolling and more direct travel toward commanded paths and beacon targets. Onboard inertial and ultra-wideband sensing support learned steering, beacon seeking, and waypoint following through one motor-velocity command. Rollbot 2 autonomously approaches UWB beacons without motion capture and completes a 468~m manually commanded indoor route at 0.87~m/s. On the shared 4.8~m ``N'' waypoint benchmark, all 16 trials complete the path with a mean traversal time of 10.8~s and mean time-averaged deviation of 7~cm. Its fastest traversal takes 8.0~s, over 70 times faster than the original Rollbot's 564~s run despite minor protocol differences. These results show how mechanical redesign, onboard sensing, and learning-based control improve the agility and autonomy of a single-actuator spherical robot.

</details>


### [130] [Switch-Time-Aware Hybrid Model Predictive Control Using the Hybrid Minimum Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24125)
*Valentin Gaucher,Wenlong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种时间感知混合模型预测控制（MPC）框架，将事件切换时刻作为显式决策变量，与连续控制输入联合优化，并在每个MPC步求解单个稀疏二次规划（QP）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统固定时间网格混合MPC难以精确优化事件切换时刻，依赖saltation矩阵计算混合灵敏度，且互补约束方法求解慢。

Method: 将切换时刻提升到决策向量中，形成光滑非线性规划；其KKT条件与混合最小原理（HMP）的事件时间平稳性条件一致，从而通过KKT系统隐式恢复一阶混合灵敏度，而非显式计算saltation矩阵；每步实时联合优化控制输入与碰撞/冲击时刻。

Result: 在弹跳质量块落到振荡平台算例中，附着时间仅为固定网格saltation MPC的一半，累计控制 effort 降低30%，消除碰撞后反弹，单周期求解时间增加15%；累计 effort 与离线时间感知求解器误差在4%以内，每周期2.4 ms，而同一问题的互补约束形式为281 ms；固定求解器下仅转写方式即带来10.6倍加速。

Conclusion: 该框架能实时联合优化控制与事件时序，兼顾精度与效率，显著优于固定网格saltation MPC和互补约束形式，并接近离线时间感知求解器性能。

Abstract: This article presents a time-aware hybrid model predictive control (MPC) framework that simultaneously optimizes the objective with respect to both continuous control inputs and event switching times. Rather than relying on a fixed time grid, the switching instant is lifted into the decision vector and treated as an explicit optimization variable. The resulting transcription is a smooth nonlinear program whose Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) conditions are consistent with the Hybrid Minimum Principle (HMP) event-time stationarity condition, so first-order hybrid sensitivities are recovered implicitly through the KKT system rather than by evaluating saltation matrices. Each MPC step solves a single sparse Quadratic Program (QP), jointly optimizing control inputs and impact timing in real time. On a bouncing mass landing on an oscillating platform, the method attaches in half the time of fixed-grid saltation MPC, reduces cumulative control effort by 30%, and removes the post-impact rebounds, at a 15% increase in per-cycle solve time. It matches the cumulative effort of offline time-aware solvers to within 4% while running at 2.4 ms per cycle against 281 ms for a complementarity-based formulation of the same problem, of which a 10.6x reduction is attributable to the transcription alone at a fixed solver.

</details>


### [131] [CLARO: Clutter Aware Robot Orchestrator for Open-Vocabulary Grasping using VLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24016)
*Sungsu Kim,Vineet Bhat,Prashanth Krishnamurthy,Ramesh Karri,Farshad Khorrami*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CLARO 是一个面向杂乱环境的 VLM 机器人智能体，通过编排开放词汇检测、分割、6-DoF 抓取和非抓取推挤等技能，完成自然语言指定的目标定位与取回任务，并在仿真与真实机械臂上取得高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实家庭、仓库和实验室环境多样且常高度杂乱，机器人需多步规划、执行并重规划；现有方法难以在杂乱场景中结合感知与操作技能完成长时程语言指令任务。

Method: 提出 CLARO（CLutter-Aware Robot Orchestrator），一个 VLM 智能体，编排开放词汇目标检测、分割、6-DoF 抓取和非抓取推挤技能以定位并取回语言指定目标；维护历史动作记忆支持长时程执行；输出抓取位姿/3D 路点作为操作目标，从而无需任务特定训练即可迁移到不同机械臂。

Result: 在动态仿真环境和标准图像基准上评估，CLARO 在物体类别、光照和杂乱程度上表现强泛化；平均成功率达 89.1%，在高杂乱场景中相对最强基线提升超过 16%；并从 grounding 与 grasping 维度分析各方法成败；真实机械臂部署成功率达 88.8%，展示强 sim2real 泛化能力。

Conclusion: CLARO 表明，基于 VLM 的技能编排、动作记忆和可迁移操作目标输出，可有效提升杂乱环境下语言指令机器人的长时程操作性能与仿真到现实迁移能力。

Abstract: Robots that follow natural-language instructions unlock a wide range of autonomous applications in households, warehouses, and labs. Real-world environments are diverse and often heavily cluttered, requiring robots to plan over multiple steps, act, and replan as they work toward a goal. We present CLARO (CLutter-Aware Robot Orchestrator), a VLM agent that orchestrates a set of perception and manipulation skills: open-vocabulary object detection, segmentation, 6-DoF grasping, and non-prehensile pushing to locate and retrieve a language-specified target in clutter. CLARO maintains a memory of its past actions to support long-horizon execution, and emits manipulation targets (grasp poses/3D waypoints) allowing transfer across robot arms without task-specific training. We evaluate CLARO against a range of methods on a dynamic simulation environment and standard image-based benchmarks, demonstrating strong generalization across object classes, lighting, and clutter levels. CLARO attains an average success rate of 89.1%, a relative improvement of over 16% on the strongest competing method in high cluttered scenes. We further analyze performance along grounding and grasping axes to characterize where each method succeeds and fails. Finally, we deploy CLARO on a robot arm across diverse cluttered scenes, achieving a success rate of 88.8%, and demonstrating strong sim2real generalizability.

</details>


### [132] [ChairNav: Cross-Embodiment Pre-training and Personalization for Long-Horizon Wheelchair Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24012)
*Junhua Huang,Zhizheng Liu,Honglin He,Xiang Zhang,Bolei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ChairNav框架，将通用视觉导航基础模型适配为个性化电动轮椅户外人行道导航系统，并在商用电动轮椅上实现轻量硬件部署与长期自主导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电动轮椅导航多局限于室内地图导航，或依赖昂贵复杂传感器实现完全自主；为实现可及的个人出行，需要利用轻量硬件和具备强泛化能力的视觉导航方案完成长时程户外人行道导航。

Method: ChairNav采用三阶段数据金字塔：先在大规模跨具身数据集上预训练并转换到轮椅域，再用少量轮椅示范进行中训练以消除域差距，最后通过用户干预进行后训练以个性化行为；系统部署于配备RGB-D相机和NVIDIA Jetson计算模块的商用电动轮椅。

Result: 预训练使横向ADE降低18%；后训练将用户干预间的平均距离从29.2 m提升至57.4 m；在公里级城市人行道路线评估中，系统在总驾驶时间中93.4%可自主运行。

Conclusion: 实验验证了数据金字塔各阶段的有效性，表明ChairNav在轻量硬件上具备日常可及出行所需的户外人行道自主导航潜力。

Abstract: Developing autonomous electric wheelchairs capable of long-horizon sidewalk navigation is critical for accessible personal mobility, yet existing approaches either focus on indoor map-based navigation or rely on expensive, complex sensor stacks to achieve full autonomy. In this work, we propose to tackle outdoor sidewalk navigation by leveraging visual navigation foundation models and large-scale navigation datasets, which offer strong generalization using lightweight hardware. To this end, we present ChairNav, a novel framework that adapts a general visual navigation model into a personalized electric wheelchair navigation system using a three-stage data pyramid. To address the scarcity of in-domain data, ChairNav first pre-trains on large-scale cross-embodiment datasets transformed into the wheelchair domain, followed by mid-training on a small number of wheelchair demonstrations to eliminate remaining domain gaps. Finally, a post-training stage refines the deployed model from user interventions to personalize its behavior. We deploy ChairNav on a commercial electric wheelchair equipped with an RGB-D camera and an onboard NVIDIA Jetson compute module. Experimental results validate the effectiveness of each stage in our data pyramid: pre-training reduces lateral ADE by 18%, and post-training increases the mean distance between user interventions from 29.2 m to 57.4 m. Further evaluation on kilometer-scale urban sidewalk routes shows that our system operates autonomously for 93.4% of total driving time, demonstrating its potential for everyday accessible mobility. Demo video and more details are at this https URL .

</details>


### [133] [Weakly Supervised Discovery of Motion Styles for Compositional Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24008)
*Heedo Woo,Antonio Marino,Eduardo Sebastián,Amanda Prorok*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CODES 提出一种从多样录制中无监督发现风格并组合独立任务命令的机器人控制框架，在 Unitree Go2 上验证了风格一致性与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当机器人自由度多于任务需求时，同一目标可由多种运动风格实现；组合式控制需在保留指定风格的同时执行独立任务命令，并平衡潜在冲突目标。

Method: CODES 首先通过运动重定向将录制映射为目标机器人可执行运动；随后用弱监督共识编码和密度聚类，在无需预设数量下识别单段及跨段录制中的风格；最后用强化学习训练策略，将发现的风格与独立命令组合。

Result: 在 Unitree Go2 四足机器人上，于两个仿真器和真实硬件评估；相比基线显著提升运动学一致性，并能跟踪超出录制数据支撑范围的速度命令，在跨仿真器和真实部署中表现出稳健且可区分的风格特征。

Conclusion: CODES 能有效从多样录制中学习可组合风格，实现风格保持与独立任务执行的结合，并具备跨仿真器与真实硬件的泛化部署能力。

Abstract: When a robot possesses more degrees of freedom than a task requires, multiple trajectories can achieve the same objective using distinct motion styles. Compositional robot control aims to preserve a selected style while simultaneously executing an independent task command, effectively balancing these potentially competing objectives. In this paper, we present CODES, a framework for compositional robot control that discovers a coherent style repertoire from diverse recordings and composes it with independent commands. First, motion retargeting maps the recordings to motions executable by the target robot; next, weakly supervised consensus encoding and density-based clustering identify styles within and across recordings without a prescribed count; finally, reinforcement learning trains a policy to compose the discovered styles with independent commands. We evaluate CODES on a Unitree Go2 quadruped across two simulators and physical hardware. Compared to baselines, CODES significantly improves kinematic coherence. Furthermore, our controller successfully tracks velocity commands that generalize beyond the support of the recorded data, exhibiting robust and distinguishable style characteristics during both cross-simulator and real-world hardware deployments. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [134] [REAP: Learning General Rearrangement Heuristics from Motion-Planner Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23973)
*Daniel Swoboda,Hector Geffner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: REAP 通过在简单符号抽象上学习关系图神经网络启发式，并利用运动规划器在训练中监督 eliciting 隐藏几何前置条件，从而提升机器人重排任务的可解性、几何可行性与规划效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人重排任务既需要满足高层规格的动作序列规划，又必须保证几何可行。符号抽象能处理组合结构，但通常无法充分刻画决定动作能否实际执行的几何约束，且这些约束难以直接写入符号模型。

Method: 提出 REAP 框架：在简单符号抽象上训练关系图神经网络启发式；训练时以运动规划器作为监督者，eliciting 问题中隐藏的几何前置条件；部署时结合贪婪最佳优先搜索进行规划。

Result: 学习到的启发式在对象数量、规划长度和对象布局上具备分布外泛化能力，并可跨多个域联合训练。与经典启发式搜索和基于学习的基线相比，REAP 解决更多问题，运动规划器查询次数约少一个数量级；在采样状态中，其排名第一的动作有 94% 几何可行，而随机选择为 22%。

Conclusion: REAP 表明将运动规划器作为训练监督来学习符号启发式，可有效弥补符号抽象与几何可行性之间的差距，并在重排任务中实现更高成功率、更低规划查询成本和更强泛化能力。

Abstract: Robotic rearrangement tasks require planning sequences of actions that fulfill high-level specifications while also being geometrically feasible. Symbolic abstractions make the combinatorial structure of rearrangement tasks tractable, but they usually do not fully capture the geometric constraints that determine which actions a robot can actually realize. These constraints are hard to express in the symbolic model. We propose REAP (Rearrangement with Elicited Action Preconditions), a framework in which a relational graph neural network heuristic is learned over a simple symbolic abstraction. A motion planner serves as a supervisor that elicits the hidden geometric preconditions of the problem during training. Deployed with greedy best-first search, the learned heuristic generalizes out-of-distribution along object count, plan length, and object arrangement, and can be trained jointly on several domains. Compared to classical heuristic search and learning-based baselines, REAP solves more problems, requires roughly an order of magnitude fewer motion-planner queries than comparable methods, and the action it ranks first is geometrically feasible in 94% of sampled states, against 22% for a random choice.

</details>


### [135] [ESCAPE: Egocentric Sensing for Collision Avoidance and Policy Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23943)
*Weiqing Shen,Wenhao Huang,Baokang Zhang,Shuo Shang,Yanmiao Hu,Haojun Li,Yifan Han,Wenzhao Lian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ESCAPE：一种仅依赖分布式深度传感器自中心感知的在线无碰撞轨迹生成框架，通过障碍感知点云表示与流匹配生成策略，实现仿真训练到真实硬件的零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非结构化环境中的运动规划是机器人部署瓶颈；现有视觉运动策略依赖外部相机，存在感知盲区，并易受遮挡、杂乱和标定约束影响而脆弱。

Method: 聚合分布式深度传感器的稀疏测量，转换为结构化障碍感知点云；采用基于流匹配的生成式策略，在超过10万条安全关键轨迹上训练，并联合优化到达目标与避碰目标，从自中心输入直接生成安全轨迹；全程仿真训练，零样本迁移到真实硬件。

Result: 仿真与真实环境评估表明，ESCAPE仅用自中心感知即可实现稳定运动规划，并在仿真和真实场景中优于外部视角与体素化规划基线。

Conclusion: ESCAPE证明了基于分布式深度传感器的自中心感知、障碍感知点云表示与流匹配生成策略可在无真实微调下实现稳健无碰撞轨迹生成与零样本真机部署。

Abstract: Motion planning in unstructured environments is a bottleneck for robot deployment. Current visuomotor policies predominantly rely on external cameras, introducing perceptual blind spots and fragility due to occlusions, clutter, and calibration constraints. To overcome these limitations, we propose ESCAPE, an online egocentric sensing framework for collision-free trajectory generation. ESCAPE aggregates sparse measurements from distributed depth sensors, converting them into a structured, obstacle-aware point cloud representation. Our policy leverages a flow matching-based generative framework, and trains on a high-quality dataset comprising over 100K safety-critical trajectories. With joint optimization of goal-reaching and collision objectives, it produces safe trajectories directly from these egocentric inputs. Crucially, ESCAPE is trained entirely in simulation and transfers zero-shot to physical hardware without any real-world fine-tuning. Extensive simulation and real-world evaluations demonstrate that ESCAPE achieves stable motion planning using only egocentric perception, outperforming external-view and voxelized-planning baselines across both simulation and real-world evaluations.

</details>


### [136] [UMI-WM: Interactive World Modeling for Bimanual Manipulation from Wrist Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23913)
*Jingxiang Guo,Haoyu Zhao,Xidong Zhang,Yuhang Zheng,Hao Wu,Chen Gao,Jianshu Zhou,Shuicheng Yan,Weihao Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UMI-WM 是一个动作条件化的双臂世界模型，可从历史观测、每臂相对相机位姿和夹爪宽度联合生成双腕视角视频，并兼顾跨视角一致性、动作跟随与视觉保真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UMI 数据将腕部相机视频与相机轨迹、夹爪开合配对，为学习双臂动作如何改变共享场景提供可扩展的机器人无关数据。但现有交互式世界模型多依赖文本或单相机控制，难以分别跟随两臂动作并保持两个腕部视角间的一致性。

Method: UMI-WM 将每臂动作注入各自的腕部视频流，并用稀疏 hub tokens 在两流间交换场景信息；引入相机感知几何条件与相机引导噪声，以支持逐视角运动控制和跨视角几何交换。模型适配 block-causal 生成，并将 30 步 Teacher 蒸馏为 4 步 Student。训练数据包括自采开放世界与棚内 UMI 记录及公开操作数据。评估时用逆动力学模型从生成视频恢复相对相机位姿和夹爪宽度，并提出 Bimanual Action Fidelity 衡量恢复动作与条件动作的匹配程度。

Result: UMI-WM 在五项参考视频保真度指标上均取得最佳结果，在 VBench 和 WorldArena 上取得最佳或竞争性表现；Teacher 与 Student 在动作跟随上优于所有对比基线，且四步 Student 生成成对腕部视频的速度比 Teacher 快 6.9 倍。

Conclusion: UMI-WM 表明，通过双臂分流动作注入、跨视角 token 交互和相机几何条件，可构建高保真、动作可跟随且跨视角一致的双臂腕部视频世界模型，并经蒸馏实现高效生成。

Abstract: Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI) pairs wrist-camera videos with camera trajectories and gripper openings, providing scalable, robot-free data for learning how bimanual actions change a shared scene. Existing interactive world models, however, typically rely on text or single-camera control and struggle to follow each arm's actions while maintaining consistency between the two wrist views. We introduce UMI-WM, an action-conditioned bimanual world model that jointly generates both wrist views from observation history, per-arm relative camera poses, and gripper widths, with cross-view consistency, action following, and visual fidelity. UMI-WM injects each arm's actions into its own wrist stream, while sparse hub tokens exchange scene information between the two streams. Camera-aware geometry conditioning and camera-guided noise further support per-view motion control and cross-view geometric exchange. We adapt the model for block-causal generation and distill the 30-step Teacher into a Student with four denoising steps. We train UMI-WM on self-collected open-world and in-studio UMI recordings combined with public manipulation data. To evaluate action following, we use an inverse dynamics model (IDM) to recover relative camera poses and gripper widths from generated videos, and introduce Bimanual Action Fidelity (BAF) to measure how closely these recovered actions match the conditioning actions. UMI-WM achieves the best results on all five reference-video fidelity metrics and the best or competitive performance on VBench and WorldArena. The Teacher and Student outperform all compared baselines in action following, while the four-step Student generates paired wrist videos 6.9 times faster than the Teacher.

</details>


### [137] [MicroSplat: Towards Automating 3D Small-Scale Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23899)
*Roman Mineyev,Robert Azarcon,Suvrat Jain,Chuizheng Kong,Shreyas Kousik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MicroSplat 提出一种面向宏观小物体亚毫米细节重建的自动化 3D Gaussian Splatting 流程：使用低成本消费级显微镜与机械臂，在一次性物体定位后无人值守完成标定、无碰撞视角规划、逐视角焦距堆栈采集与融合、跨视角物体分割，并以机械臂位姿热启动 3DGS 优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小物体亚毫米细节重建对电路板缺陷检测、标本数字化和归档等任务很重要。3DGS 可从已定位图像实现逼真重建，但宏观光学使其依赖的两个条件失效：视场和景深缩小到毫米级，视角误差可能使样本出画，且单帧仅薄焦平面清晰。现有自动宏观采集装置虽用逐视角焦点堆栈处理浅景深，但多依赖传统摄影测量和离线人工介入。

Method: 系统在单次前置物体定位后自动运行：执行标定，规划无碰撞视角，在每个视角采集并融合焦距堆栈，对所有视角进行物体分割，并以机械臂自身位姿热启动优化 3DGS 模型。作者还与模糊感知 3DGS 方法比较，后者从散焦图像推断清晰场景。

Result: 研究表明，通过受控焦距扫描直接测量清晰度优于从散焦图像推断清晰度。作者提供包含机器人及相机参数的真实与合成数据集，以及拟合得到的 3DGS 重建结果。

Conclusion: MicroSplat 将低成本消费级显微镜、机械臂自动采集与 3DGS 结合，实现无人值守的宏观小物体高细节重建；受控焦距扫描策略在浅景深条件下优于模糊感知推断方法，并公开数据集与重建结果以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Reconstructing sub-millimeter detail of small objects underpins tasks such as circuit-board defect detection, specimen digitization, and archiving. 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) makes photorealistic reconstruction possible from posed images alone, but macro optics violate two conditions it depends on. The field of view (FOV) and the depth of field (DoF) shrink to millimeters, so viewpoint error can take a sample out of frame, and only a thin focal plane is sharp in frame. Existing automated macro-capture rigs handle the shallow DoF with per-view focus stacking, but typically rely on classical photogrammetry and offline, human-in-the-loop processing. We present MicroSplat, which generates a 3DGS using a low-cost consumer microscope on a manipulator arm. After a single up-front object-localization step, the system runs unattended. It performs calibration, plans collision-free viewpoints, captures and fuses a focal stack at each viewpoint, segments the object across all views, and optimizes a 3DGS model warm-started with the arm's own poses. We further compare against blur-aware 3DGS methods, which infer sharp scenes from defocused images, and find that measuring sharpness with a controlled focal sweep outperforms inferring it. We provide real and synthetic datasets (including robot and camera parameters) and the fitted 3DGS reconstructions.

</details>


### [138] [InJePA: Intention-First Latent Planning with Coupled JEPA World Models for Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23896)
*Siming Zhou,Bowen Ma,Ruiran Du,Pengcheng Zheng,Sheng Zheng,Dongshen Han,Zhijie Li,Wei Dong,Fan Mo,Chaoning Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: InJepa 提出一种以潜在意图为中心的“意图优先”视觉导航规划范式，通过目标条件 JEPA 生成潜在意图、动作条件 JEPA 预测未来潜在状态，并在共享潜在空间中形成意图—动作—后果闭环，使世界模型直接参与候选方案构建而非仅用于评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航世界模型方法通常先提出完整动作候选，再预测后果用于选择或搜索更新；预测主要评估动作，而非逐步指导候选动作的构造。作者希望让世界模型通过潜在意图直接引导规划构建。

Method: 提出 InJepa：目标条件 JEPA 提议潜在意图，动作条件 JEPA 持续预测未来潜在状态，二者在共享潜在空间中对齐预测表示；每个候选内部，预测后果反馈到下一步意图提议与动作推断，形成意图、动作、后果的闭环。

Result: 在 Habitat MP3D 的闭环图像目标导航任务中，InJepa 通过意图引导的动作生成与后果反馈实现有效的多步导航；跨编码器实验进一步支持其适用于不同视觉表示。

Conclusion: 潜在意图可作为核心规划对象；世界模型能够通过对意图与后果的协同推理直接指导规划构建，而不仅限于候选评估。

Abstract: Visual navigation requires goal directed planning informed by predictions of environment dynamics. Representative navigation world model methods propose complete action candidates before predicting consequences for selection or search updates. For each candidate, predictions primarily evaluate proposed actions rather than guide its stepwise construction. To this end, we propose InJepa, an intention first planning paradigm centered on latent states. A goal conditioned JEPA proposes latent intentions, and an action conditioned JEPA continually predicts future latent states. Both operate in a shared latent space to align their predictive representations. Within each candidate, predicted consequences feed into the next intention proposal and action inference, forming a closed loop of intention, action, and consequence. This extends the world model's role beyond evaluation to leading candidate construction through latent intentions. We evaluate InJepa through closed loop image goal navigation in Habitat MP3D. The results show effective multistep navigation through intention guided action generation and consequence feedback. Experiments across encoders further support applicability across visual representations. These findings support latent intentions as the central planning object and show that world models can directly guide plan construction through coordinated reasoning about intentions and consequences. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [139] [Language-Forcing: Advancing Humanoid Loco-Manipulation via Semantic Alignment of Whole-Body World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23894)
*Yanjun Li,Jiarui Ye,Weiyu Guo,Zixiao He,Kangjie Ye,Dihan Wang,Yishuo Cheng,Zishang Xiang,Mingju Gao,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 LANGUAGE-FORCING 训练策略，用语言监督对齐全身世界动作模型中动作条件化的未来视觉特征，以突出任务相关变化，在不增加推理开销下提升全身策略成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型虽联合预测动作与未来观测，但视觉预测目标未显式区分任务相关状态转移与偶然场景变化；全身策略的任务进展依赖身体姿态、接触和物体状态的协同变化，因此需要强化未来表征中的任务相关性。

Method: 给定当前帧与真实短时未来帧，使用冻结视觉语言模型评估任务进展并预测合理后续状态变化；用冻结文本编码器编码描述，将其嵌入与中间层、动作条件化的未来视觉特征对齐，作为原始视觉与动作预测目标的补充。所有辅助组件仅训练时使用，不增加推理开销。

Result: 在 HumanoidArena 上平均成功率达 79.17%，比最强对比策略高 9.46 个百分点，比未语言对齐的同一 WAM 高 10.77 个百分点；在五个真实世界任务上，同一 WAM 基线平均成功率由 53.33% 提升至 71.33%。

Conclusion: 语言引导的未来视觉表征对齐能有效突出任务相关变化，提升全身世界动作模型的策略性能，且不引入额外推理成本。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) jointly predict robot actions and future observations. However, their visual prediction objectives do not explicitly distinguish task-relevant transitions from incidental scene changes. For whole-body policies, this distinction matters because task progress depends on coordinated changes in body pose, contact, and object state. We introduce LANGUAGE-FORCING, a training strategy that uses language to emphasize task-relevant changes in the future visual representations of a whole-body WAM. Given paired current and ground- truth short-horizon future frames, a frozen vision-language model assesses task progress and predicts plausible subsequent state changes. We encode these descriptions with a frozen text encoder and align their embeddings with intermediate, action-conditioned future-visual features, complementing the original visual and action prediction objectives. All auxiliary components are used only during training, introducing no additional inference overhead. On HumanoidArena, LANGUAGE-FORCING achieves an average success rate of 79.17%, exceeding the strongest competing policy by 9.46 percentage points and the same WAM without language alignment by 10.77 percentage points. Across five real-world tasks, language alignment raises average success from 53.33% to 71.33% over the same WAM baseline.

</details>


### [140] [When to Make a Decision: Task-Sufficient Active Perception for Mobile Manipulator Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23891)
*Mariana da Silva Barros,Matthias Kerzel,Cornelius Weber,Stefan Wermter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向未知环境的任务充分主动感知混合神经符号框架，使机器人能够判断何时已收集到足够任务相关信息并停止感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在未知环境中执行任务前需获取足够信息，但何时信息已充分仍是挑战；现有系统多关注如何感知，较少解决何时停止感知。

Method: 框架先解析任务并生成初始计划，随新信息迭代重评估；每轮可选择获取更有信息量的视角、检查或操作候选物体、向用户请求澄清或执行任务。神经感知模块维护以物体为中心的信念状态，符号决策层根据剩余不确定性判断是否继续收集信息。系统拟部署于由人形躯干和移动底座组成的移动操作机器人，并在仿真中补充评估。

Result: 摘要给出概念性框架与实现计划，未报告定量实验结果或性能指标。

Conclusion: 目标是使机器人不仅知道如何收集更多信息，还能判断进一步感知何时不再必要，从而实现任务充分的主动感知。

Abstract: A robotic agent operating in an unknown environment must gather sufficient task-relevant information before acting. However, determining when the available information is sufficient remains a challenge. We propose a conceptual hybrid neuro-symbolic framework for task-sufficient active perception in unknown environments. After interpreting a requested task, the agent generates an initial plan and iteratively reevaluates it as new information becomes available. At each iteration, it can acquire a more informative viewpoint, inspect or manipulate candidate objects, ask the user for clarification, or perform the task. Neural perception modules update an object-centric belief state, while a symbolic decision layer determines whether the remaining uncertainty justifies further information gathering. The framework will be implemented on a mobile manipulator comprising a humanoid torso and a mobile base, with complementary evaluation in simulation. The proposed approach aims to enable robots to determine not only how to gather additional information, but also when further perception is no longer necessary.

</details>


### [141] [MoDex: Towards Universal Multi-Object Dexterous Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23888)
*Jing Xiong,Jiale Xie,Haoqi Yuan,Chaoyi Xu,Zongqing Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MoDex 是一个面向多物体灵巧抓取的数据高效统一框架，通过参考轨迹残差修正与空间自适应双策略，在不同空间布局下实现单次或顺序抓取，并展现出跨本体、可扩展及零样本仿真到现实迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多物体抓取是基础但困难的问题，受数据采集成本高、空间布局多变以及需要广泛泛化能力等因素制约，现有方法难以统一处理不同布局下的多物体灵巧抓取。

Method: MoDex 将任务建模为单步决策问题，学习对参考轨迹的残差修正；提出空间自适应双策略：对邻近物体执行单次抓取，对分离物体执行顺序交互抓取，从而统一应对不同空间布局。

Result: 实验表明，MoDex 在邻近布局下显著优于基线，对未见物体达到 61.24% 成功率；在分离布局中也取得高成功率。进一步实验验证了其跨机器人本体泛化能力、可扩展性，以及仅用仿真数据训练视觉策略即可实现零样本仿真到现实部署。

Conclusion: MoDex 为多样空间布局下的通用多物体灵巧抓取提供了首个数据高效统一解决方案，兼具抓取性能、泛化能力和实际部署潜力。

Abstract: Multi-object grasping is a fundamental yet challenging problem, constrained by data collection costs, varying spatial layouts, and the need for broad generalization. To address these challenges, we propose MoDex, a data-efficient framework that provides the first unified solution for universal multi-object dexterous grasping across diverse spatial layouts. MoDex accomplishes this by formulating the task as a single-step decision problem of learning residual corrections to a reference trajectory, and introducing a spatially adaptive dual-strategy that performs single-shot grasping for proximal objects and sequential interaction for separated objects. Extensive evaluations show that MoDex significantly outperforms baselines in proximal configurations, achieving 61.24% success on unseen objects, and achieves high success rates in separated layouts. Further experiments demonstrate its generalization across robot embodiments, scalability and zero-shot sim-to-real deployment through a vision-based policy trained solely on simulation data. Code and pretrained models are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [142] [ContactKernels: Adaptive Proposals using Geometry-COnditioned Wrenches for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23887)
*Aditya Kamireddypalli,Matías Mattamala,João Moura,Sethu Vijayakumar,Steve Tonneau,Subramanian Ramamoorthy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ContactKernels：一种接触自适应提议核，利用几何条件化的力/力矩测量估计接触位置与局部接触几何，并据此调整采样分布，以提升采样型 MPC 在接触丰富插入任务中的搜索效率与成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富插入需在感知、几何与控制不确定性下实现精确对齐；意外接触可提供局部信息指导修正运动。但标准采样型 MPC 通常使用通用提议分布，未利用观测到的接触信息来决定下一步搜索方向。

Method: ContactKernels 从几何条件化的 wrench 测量中估计接触位置与局部接触几何；接触位置使搜索偏向修正运动，接触法向塑造采样协方差。将该提议核与 Predictive Sampling（PS）和 Model Predictive Path Integral（MPPI）控制结合，在多种插入几何的仿真与真实机器人上评估。

Result: 仿真中，ContactKernels 在两种控制器下均将平均插入成功率提升超过 50 个百分点；真实机器人上，插入成功率从 53.3% 提升至 90.0%，平均插入时间减少 33%。

Conclusion: 接触信息不仅可作为反馈信号，还可直接决定采样型控制器在何处搜索，从而显著改善接触丰富插入任务中的采样型控制性能。

Abstract: Contact-rich insertion requires precise geometric alignment despite uncertainty in perception, geometry, and con- trol. When this uncertainty causes unintended contact, the re- sulting contact provides local information that can guide correc- tive motion. Sampling-based Model Predictive Control (SMPC) is well suited to this setting because it optimizes through forward simulation without requiring gradients through contact dynamics. However, standard SMPC methods typically use generic proposal distributions that do not exploit observed contact to determine where to search next. We introduce ContactKernels, a contact-adaptive proposal kernel that uses geometry-conditioned wrench measurements to estimate contact location and local contact geometry. The estimated contact state directly adapts the proposal distribution: contact location biases the search toward corrective motions, while the contact normal shapes the sampling covariance. We evaluate ContactKernels with Predictive Sampling (PS) and Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control across multiple insertion geometries in simulation and on a real robot. In simulation, ContactKernels improves mean insertion success by over 50 % points across both controllers. On the real robot, it increases insertion success from 53.3 % to 90.0 % and reduces mean time to insertion by 33 %. These results show that contact information can improve sampling-based control not only as feedback, but also by directly determining where the controller searches

</details>


### [143] [EvoAD: Self-Evolving Autonomous Driving via Latent World-Model Guided Post-hoc Reflection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23886)
