*Junjie Yang,Qiming Zhang,Yuyan Li,Shuqi Shen,Yusong Huang,Yanggang Sheng,Wenpeng Xu,Ziyi Shi,Tianshun Li,Yijie Chen,Yujia Wang,Jun Ma,Xinhu Zheng,Hongliang Lu,Hai Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 EvoAD——一种基于潜在世界模型引导的事后反思（post-hoc reflection）的自演化自动驾驶框架，使端到端驾驶系统在部署后无需重训练基座策略即可持续自我改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶系统把传感器观测直接映射为轨迹，部署后策略冻结：相似场景重复相似决策，失败只能依靠昂贵的数据采集与重训练来修复；这与人类驾驶员通过经验、记忆与反思实现终身学习形成鲜明对比。本文追问自动驾驶系统能否获得类似的部署后自演化能力。

Method: 构建 BEV 特征记忆池存储驾驶经验，按几何与语义相似度检索场景相关先例；用潜在世界模型在紧凑的、面向规划的隐空间中评估候选轨迹的未来后果；将已执行的驾驶结果转化为可复用的改进信号，据此诊断既往轨迹应如何改进，并在相似未来场景中修正行为。与将记忆作为外部文本上下文、或仅用世界模型做事前仿真不同，该方法把世界模型用作事后反思机制，且不重训练基座策略。

Result: 在 NAVSIM v1 与 v2 上，性能随反思轮次增加而稳定提升，并取得当前最优（state-of-the-art）表现。

Conclusion: 世界模型可作为反思机制支撑自动驾驶的部署后自演化，为免重训练的持续学习提供了可行路径。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving systems have achieved rapid progress by learning unified policies that map sensor observations directly to driving trajectories. However, once deployed, these systems typically remain frozen: they may repeat similar decisions in similar scenes, and failures can only be addressed through costly data collection and retraining. This sharply contrasts with human drivers, who improve lifelong through experience, memory, and reflection. In this work, we ask whether autonomous driving systems can achieve similar post-deployment self-evolution to human drivers. We propose EvoAD, a self-evolving autonomous driving framework via latent world-model guided post-hoc reflection. EvoAD stores driving experiences in a bird's-eye-view feature memory pool, retrieves scene-relevant precedents according to geometric and semantic similarity, and uses a latent world model to evaluate the future consequences of candidate trajectories in a compact planning-oriented space. Instead of treating memory as external textual contexts or using world models only for pre-action simulation, EvoAD transforms executed driving outcomes into reusable improvement signals for future decisions. This enables the system to diagnose how prior trajectories could have been improved and to refine behavior in similar future scenarios without retraining the base driving policy. Experiments on NAVSIM v1 and v2 show consistent improvements across reflection rounds and state-of-the-art performance, demonstrating the potential of world models as reflective mechanisms for post-deployment self-evolution in autonomous driving.

</details>


### [144] [From Likelihood to Utility: Quality-Axis Diffusion for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23885)
*Junjie Yang,Ziyi Shi,Shuqi Shen,Xinhu Zheng,Hongliang Lu,Hai Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: QualiDrive 将扩散生成轴从高斯噪声改为驾驶质量轴，通过场景特定的质量阶梯和由低到高的有序细化生成自动驾驶轨迹，在 NAVSIM 上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的端到端自动驾驶规划器主要沿高斯噪声轴组织轨迹生成，驾驶效用仅作为辅助引导或候选选择信号，未直接作为生成过程的核心组织维度。

Method: 构建场景特定的质量阶梯：按测得的驾驶效用对观测轨迹排序，学习逆转有序退化过程，实现从低质量到高质量的有序细化；每个反向步骤以场景、自车状态、当前轨迹和目标质量等级为条件，逐步将轨迹细化到更高质量。

Result: 在 NAVSIM 上，QualiDrive 在 v1 达到 94.5 PDMS，在 v2 达到当前最优的 92.74 EPDMS；相比仅端点质量监督，Oracle EPDMS 仅从 98.52 小幅提升至 98.57，但 EPDMS 高于 0.95 和 0.98 的候选比例分别从 12.94%/9.71% 提升至 21.53%/15.93%，选中 EPDMS 从 92.63 提升至 92.74。

Conclusion: 结果表明扩散过程不仅是去噪，还可以是沿质量轴的有序渐进细化；将驾驶质量作为扩散组织轴能有效提升候选轨迹质量和最终选择性能。

Abstract: Diffusion models have become a dominant paradigm for end-to-end autonomous driving, yet existing planners organize trajectory generation along the Gaussian-noise axis, treating driving utility only as an auxiliary guidance or selection signal. We instead formulate diffusion along a driving-quality axis. We propose QualiDrive, which constructs scene-specific quality ladders by ranking observed trajectories according to measured driving utility and learns to reverse this ordered degradation through ordered low-to-high-quality refinement. Each reverse step is conditioned on the scene, ego state, current trajectory, and target quality level, progressively refining trajectories toward higher driving quality. On NAVSIM, QualiDrive achieves 94.5 PDMS on v1 and a state-of-the-art 92.74 EPDMS on v2. QualiDrive improves Oracle EPDMS only modestly over endpoint-only quality supervision (98.52 to 98.57), but substantially increases the fractions of candidates above 0.95 and 0.98 EPDMS from 12.94%/9.71% to 21.53%/15.93%, raising selected EPDMS from 92.63 to 92.74. These results suggest that diffusion is not merely a process of denoising, but one of progressive quality-ordered refinement.

</details>


### [145] [PrismPlan: A Shared GPU Substrate for Motion Planning Across Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23884)
*Hong Wu,ChaoXuan Liu,ShuJie Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PrismPlan 通过共享 GPU 碰撞检测与前向运动学底层，将采样、搜索和优化三类运动规划器置于同一后端下进行公平对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU 加速虽显著提升运动规划速度，但现有加速规划器各自绑定单一范式，跨库比较会混入碰撞检测器差异；三类范式又共同受限于批量碰撞检测及其批量前向运动学这一 GPU 原语。

Method: 构建统一的 per-sphere 碰撞-运动学设备代码作为共享底座，供所有规划器调用，从而在构造上固定后端；在 1800 个 MotionBenchMaker 问题、三种机器人（7--14 DoF，含双臂全身复合机器人）和统一 2s 预算下比较六种规划器。

Result: 7-DoF Panda 上采样法 pRRTC 最可靠，成功率达 98%；并行搜索 wPA*SE 低 4 个百分点内，且中位延迟低 3--4 倍；局部优化器从零求解明显落后。14-DoF 双臂复合机器人上采样仍解决约 90% 可行问题，而搜索与优化均不足三分之一。

Conclusion: 共享 GPU 碰撞-运动学底座可实现三类规划范式的头对头比较；采样法可靠性最高，尤其在高自由度场景，搜索法在低自由度下具低延迟竞争力，优化法从零求解较弱。

Abstract: GPU acceleration has made motion planning one to two orders of magnitude faster, but it has not made the three paradigms---sampling, search, and optimization---comparable to one another: every GPU-accelerated planner is built around a single paradigm, so comparing across libraries also compares their collision checkers. All three, however, bottleneck on the same GPU primitive: batched collision detection and the batched forward kinematics beneath it. PrismPlan builds that primitive once, as a shared substrate of per-sphere collision--kinematics device code that every planner consumes. Because they all execute the same device code, the backend is held fixed by construction and the paradigms can be compared head-to-head. We run six planners over 1800 MotionBenchMaker problems on three robots (7--14 DoF, one a dual-arm whole-body composite) at an equal 2s budget. On the 7-DoF Panda, sampling (pRRTC) is the most reliable paradigm at 98%, while parallel search (wPA*SE) follows within four points at a 3-4 times$ lower median latency, and the local optimizers trail well behind when made to solve from scratch. On the 14-DoF dual-arm composite, sampling still solves about 90% of feasible problems, while no search or optimization planner clears a third of them. Our open-source code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [146] [Learning Whole-Body Kinetic Coordination for Wheeled-Quadruped Long-Range Throwing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23883)
*Qinglian Wu,Yidian Hu,Weiqi Fan,Yutian Zhang,Siyuan Ma,Jiaqi Zhai,Wei Dong,Li Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出轮式四足机械臂全身动力学协调框架，通过动力学链建模、SQP轨迹优化与参考引导强化学习实现非结构化地形上的长距离抛掷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人在配送与取回任务中需要具备长距离抛掷能力，但这要求高释放速度、全身协调运动以及对地形扰动具有鲁棒性。

Method: 将机械臂简化为动力学链模型，在运动学与动力学约束下用SQP优化生成高速抛掷参考轨迹；再采用参考引导的强化学习策略协调基座运动，并结合轮式四足平台 mobility 进行全身协调。

Result: 固定基座下机械臂释放速度达12.75 m/s、抛掷距离16.68 m；启用全身协调后释放速度提升至16.64 m/s、距离达28.25 m。硬件实验在平地、草地、斜坡和粗糙路面验证完整策略。

Conclusion: 动力学链优化与全身协调相结合，使轮式四足机械臂能够在多种非结构化地形上实现鲁棒的长距离抛掷。

Abstract: Long-range throwing on unstructured terrain is a key capability for mobile robots in delivery and retrieval tasks, but it requires high release velocity, coordinated whole-body motion, and robustness to terrain-induced disturbances. We present a whole-body kinetic-coordination framework for a wheeled-quadruped manipulator that combines kinetic-chain arm modeling with SQP-based trajectory optimization and a reference-guided reinforcement learning policy for coordinated base motion. The manipulator is first reduced to a kinetic-chain model and optimized under kinematic and dynamic constraints to produce a high-speed throwing reference. On a stationary base, the optimized arm trajectory reaches a release speed of 12.75 m/s and a throwing distance of 16.68 m. When whole-body coordination is enabled, the system further increases the release speed to 16.64 m/s and the throwing distance to 28.25 m. We then integrate the mobility of the wheeled-quadruped platform and validate the complete policy through hardware experiments on flat ground, grass, slopes, and rough roads. The results show that kinetic-chain optimization and whole-body coordination together enable robust long-range throwing across diverse unstructured terrains.

</details>


### [147] [AutoPruner: Agentic Depth Pruning for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23880)
*Tao Lin,Yikeng Chen,Qiwei Liang,Zinan Xie,Zhengtu Liang,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AutoPruner 是一个由 LLM 智能体驱动的闭环实验框架，联合搜索 VLA 模型的层剪枝结构与性能恢复方案，在保持任务成功率的同时显著减少骨干层并提升推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层剪枝可降低视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的规模和推理成本，但实际部署需同时决定删除哪些层以及如何恢复任务性能；这些决策依赖模型和任务，且单次失败难以区分是删除集合不当还是恢复不足。

Method: 该框架通过离线层干预测量动作输出变化，提供功能性证据；LLM 智能体结合该证据、评估历史和保留的诊断信息，在搜索预算内提出删除集合与恢复配方；每个方案在目标任务上执行评估，反馈用于指导下一轮实验。

Result: 在 OpenVLA-OFT、StarVLA 和 FastWAM 上，于四个 LIBERO 套件中删除 16.7–87.5% 的骨干层，任务成功率仍高于 90%，实测推理加速最高达 2.50 倍；功能探针可在表示相似度高的情况下识别动作敏感层，恢复方案可使原先失败的剪枝结构变得可行。

Conclusion: AutoPruner 通过闭环、证据驱动的联合搜索，有效解决了 VLA 层剪枝中删除结构与性能恢复相互耦合的问题，为部署可用的 VLA 剪枝配置提供了可行方法。

Abstract: Layer pruning reduces the size and inference cost of vision-language-action (VLA) models, but selecting a deployable configuration requires deciding both which layers to remove and how to recover task performance. These decisions depend on the model and task, and a failed trial can reflect either the deletion set or insufficient recovery. We present AutoPruner, an agent-driven framework that jointly searches pruning structures and recovery recipes through closed-loop experimentation. Offline layer interventions measure changes in action output to provide functional evidence. An LLM agent combines this evidence with evaluation history and retained diagnoses to propose deletion sets and recovery recipes within a search budget. Each proposal is executed and evaluated on the target tasks, and the resulting feedback guides the next experiment. Across OpenVLA-OFT, StarVLA, and FastWAM on four LIBERO suites, AutoPruner removes 16.7--87.5% of backbone layers while maintaining task success above 90%, with measured inference speedups of up to 2.50$\times$. Further analysis shows that functional probes identify action-sensitive layers despite high representation similarity, and that recovery can make previously failing structures viable.

</details>


### [148] [Adaptive Rejection of Unknown Hybrid Sinusoidal Disturbances with Application to Humanoid Walking under Unknown Ground Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23879)
*I-Chia Chang,Jacob Stewart,Petros A. Ioannou,Yan Gu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文将非惯性环境下的腿式机器人步行控制建模为混合线性系统的未知混合正弦扰动抑制问题，提出基于Youla-Kucera参数化和自适应参数估计的输出反馈控制方法，并在双足机器人地面运动实验中显著提升躯干位置跟踪精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非惯性环境中地面和周围环境在惯性系下加速，产生未知、时变且持续的扰动，并与腿式机器人的混合动力学耦合，导致现有步行控制难以实用，因此需要系统性地解决未知扰动抑制问题。

Method: 将步行控制表述为受混合正弦扰动（离散事件处发生瞬时相位跳变的多个正弦分量叠加）的混合线性系统未知扰动抑制问题；首先推导问题可解的充要条件并刻画可抑制扰动的控制输入；其次利用Youla-Kucera参数化设计输出反馈控制器；最后在扰动及其参数未知的实际情形下提出自适应律估计控制器参数。

Result: 双足步行期间地面运动的仿真和实物实验表明，所提方法显著改善身体位置跟踪精度：仿真中平均提升超过60%，实物实验中最高提升47%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将非惯性环境扰动建模为混合正弦扰动并采用Y-K参数化与自适应输出反馈控制，可有效抑制未知持续扰动，提高腿式机器人非惯性环境步行控制的跟踪性能和实用性。

Abstract: Legged robot control is not yet practical in non-inertial environments, where the ground and surroundings accelerate in the inertial Earth frame, mainly because of the unknown, time-varying, persistent perturbations caused by environmental motion, compounded by the associated hybrid robotic dynamics. We formulate the problem of robot walking control as unknown-disturbance rejection for hybrid linear systems subject to hybrid sinusoidal disturbances (i.e., a linear combination of sinusoidal components with instantaneous phase jumps at discrete events). First, necessary and sufficient conditions for solvability of the disturbance rejection problem are derived and used to characterize the control input that rejects the disturbances. Second, with the Youla-Kucera parametrization, an output feedback controller capable of rejecting the disturbance is introduced. Third, an adaptive law is proposed to estimate the controller parameters that reject the disturbance in the practical case where the disturbance and its parameters are unknown. Experiments on bipedal walking during ground motion demonstrate substantially improved body-position tracking accuracy, with an average improvement of over 60% in simulations and up to 47% in real-world experiments.

</details>


### [149] [A Heterogeneous UAV UGV Methodology for Aerial Tracking of Mini Autonomous Cars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23878)
*Santiago Fernandez,Angel da Silva,Hiago Sodre,Pablo Moraes,Bruna Gutterres,Ricardo B. Grando*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出一种在仿真中融合无人机视觉检测与地面车辆状态的异构UAV–UGV空中跟踪方法，以应对视场受限、视角变化、遮挡和目标短时不可见等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯视觉无人机跟踪受相机视场、视角变化、遮挡及目标暂时丢失限制，固定外部感知基础设施在部分环境中不可用，因此需要提升空中跟踪连续性与鲁棒性。

Method: 在ROS 2与Gazebo Sim中搭建Tello类无人机（前视RGB相机）和多台LIMO类UGV的仿真系统；对比无训练颜色标记基线、YOLO视觉基线和提出的UAV–UGV融合方法。融合方法沿用YOLO检测器，并融合UGV状态信息，在视觉间隙维持目标估计。

Result: 在单車跟踪、交叉轨迹、遮挡和UAV辅助感知四类场景、共600次仿真运行中，融合方法实现近乎连续的跟踪覆盖，并较YOLO基线降低定位误差；四场景RMSE分别降低37.3%、64.2%、62.6%和61.8%；交叉场景和UAV辅助感知场景的冲突预警提前量分别为10.20 s和9.07 s。

Conclusion: 异构UAV–UGV融合可在视觉观测间歇时改善空中跟踪的连续性与鲁棒性，表明融合视觉检测与UGV状态信息是有效的跟踪增强途径。

Abstract: Aerial tracking of autonomous ground vehicles can provide useful situational awareness in environments where fixed external perception infrastructure is unavailable. However, visual-only tracking from an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) is limited by camera field of view, viewpoint changes, occlusions, and temporary loss of target visibility. This paper presents a heterogeneous UAV--UGV methodology for aerial tracking of small-scale autonomous ground vehicles in simulation. The system is implemented in ROS~2 and Gazebo Sim using a Tello-like UAV equipped with a front-facing RGB camera and multiple LIMO-like unmanned ground vehicles (UGVs). Three approaches are evaluated: a training-free color-marker baseline, a YOLO-based visual baseline, and a proposed UAV--UGV fusion method. The proposed method uses the same visual detector as the YOLO baseline, but combines visual detections with UGV state information to maintain the target estimate during visual gaps. The methods are evaluated across four scenarios: single-car tracking, crossing trajectories, occlusion, and UAV-assisted awareness. Results over 600 simulation runs show that the proposed fusion method achieves nearly continuous track coverage across all scenarios while reducing localization error relative to the YOLO baseline. Compared with the visual-only YOLO approach, the fusion method reduces RMSE by 37.3\%, 64.2\%, 62.6\%, and 61.8\% in the four evaluated scenarios, respectively. The method also enables conflict-awareness lead times of 10.20~s in the crossing scenario and 9.07~s in the UAV-assisted awareness scenario. These results indicate that heterogeneous UAV--UGV fusion can improve aerial tracking continuity and robustness when visual observations are intermittent.

</details>


### [150] [Stress-Query and Temporal Reliability of Vision-Language Models as copilots in Autonomous UAV Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23877)
*Hiago Sodre,Sebastian Barcelona,Vincent Sandin,Pablo Moraes,Andre S. Kelbouscas,Ricardo B. Grando*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文系统评测了将视觉语言模型（VLM）用于无人机自主导航时，模型规模、硬件约束与飞行可靠性之间的权衡，发现中等规模模型能在推理延迟与语义准确性之间取得最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM可提升无人机导航的语义推理能力，但其推理延迟等时间瓶颈会破坏飞行控制稳定性，因此需要厘清模型规模、硬件条件与飞行可靠性之间的操作权衡。

Method: 构建系统性基准，在高性能工作站与受限集成显卡笔记本上开展压力测试，隔离推理延迟对飞行动态的冲击，并对比不同规模VLM的推理准确性与导航稳定性。

Result: 呈现性能二分现象：轻量模型易产生结构性幻觉并破坏确定性状态机；重量级模型因推理滞后导致轨迹过度修正；中等规模架构实现最优平衡，在退化环境中恢复导航且在不同硬件配置下保持实时稳定性。

Conclusion: 面向无人机自主导航的VLM部署不应单纯追求更大模型，中等规模模型在语义推理、推理延迟与飞行可靠性之间更具综合优势，可作为跨硬件平台稳定运行的最优折中。

Abstract: Integrating Vision-Language Models (VLMs) into autonomous UAV navigation enables high-level semantic reasoning but introduces critical temporal bottlenecks that can destabilize flight control. This paper presents a systematic benchmark that evaluates the operational trade-offs among model scale, hardware constraints, and flight reliability. By subjecting the system to extensive stress tests on both a high-performance workstation and a constrained integrated-graphics laptop, we isolate the kinetic impact of inference latency against reasoning accuracy. Our findings reveal a performance dichotomy: lightweight models struggle with structural hallucinations that break deterministic state machines, while heavyweight models induce trajectory overcorrections due to inference lag. We demonstrate that a mid-weight architecture strikes the optimal balance, successfully rehabilitating navigation in degraded environments without compromising real-time stability across different hardware profiles.

</details>


### [151] [Adaptive Question Generation for Ambiguous Robot Instructions Based on Information Gain and Human Cognitive Characteristics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23874)
*Ryo Terashima,Yuga Yano,Hakaru Tamukoh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向服务机器人歧义指令的自适应提问生成机制，将信息增益与人类认知特征结合，以提升目标识别效率和用户回答便利性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言服务指令常存在歧义，多相似物体场景中机器人需通过提问确定目标；现有方法偏重概率判别效率，忽视用户回答难易，可能导致错误/不确定回答并增加澄清轮次。

Method: 利用视觉语言模型提取候选物体视觉属性，计算各属性的信息增益并选择最能降低候选集不确定性的属性；当信息增益相同时，基于认知特征进行视觉属性过滤，选择用户最直观可感知的属性；再将所选属性及各候选属性值输入VLM生成简洁自然语言问题，循环直至唯一识别目标。

Result: 在120个场景数据集评估中，目标识别成功率达到88.5%，并在回答便利性的主观评价中获得高分。

Conclusion: 该机制通过兼顾判别效率与用户认知易答性，可生成更直观的问题，提高歧义指令下服务机器人目标识别的成功率和对话效率。

Abstract: In this study, we propose an adaptive question generation mechanism that addresses uncertainty arising from ambiguous instructions. Natural-language instructions for service robots often contain ambiguities. In scenes with multiple similar objects, the robot must identify the target object by asking questions. However, existing question generation methods focus on probabilistic discrimination efficiency and do not sufficiently account for user ease of response. Asking questions without considering the user's ease of response may cause incorrect or uncertain answers. Such incorrect or uncertain answers lead to additional clarification turns and degraded dialogue efficiency. The proposed mechanism integrates probabilistic discrimination efficiency and human cognitive characteristics to generate questions that users can intuitively and easily answer. First, the proposed mechanism extracts visual attributes of candidate objects using a vision-language model (VLM). Next, the proposed mechanism computes the information gain (IG) of each attribute and selects the visual attribute (e.g., color, shape) that most reduces uncertainty in the candidate set. When multiple attributes have the same IG, the proposed mechanism applies a visual attribute filter based on cognitive characteristics to select the visual attribute that users can most intuitively perceive. The proposed mechanism then prompts the VLM with the selected attribute and the corresponding attribute values of each candidate object to generate a concise natural-language question. This process is repeated until the target object is uniquely identified. The proposed mechanism achieved a target identification success rate of 88.5% in the evaluation using a dataset of 120 scenes. Furthermore, the proposed mechanism received high ratings in subjective evaluations of response ease.

</details>


### [152] [Context-Aware RGB-Thermal-Radar Obstacle Perception for Resource-Constrained ASVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23873)
*Pranav Kedia,Edwin Redhead,Heiko Hamann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出一种面向小型自主水面艇的上下文感知多模态障碍感知架构，融合RGB、长波红外、FMCW毫米波雷达与惯性信息，并通过方位扇区接口向导航控制器输出校准后的障碍概率、雷达距离和传感器状态；在湖泊闭环试验与低光数据上验证了融合在RGB失效时仍可维持导航输入，但RGB可用时单模态排序更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型ASV的可靠障碍感知受反射眩光、船体运动导致水线变化和黑暗环境限制；现有研究常将多模态融合视为静态精度增益，而未充分处理传感器失效、低光可用性与可预测退化问题。

Method: 提出上下文感知融合架构，结合RGB与LWIR图像、FMCW毫米波雷达和惯性上下文；设计方位扇区接口，向导航控制器提供校准障碍概率、雷达距离和传感器状态，并区分传感器掉线与“有效观测但无障碍”。使用新采集的多模态湖泊数据，在非受控湖泊条件下开展24航段闭环试点（16段白天、4段黄昏、4段夜间，351 m），其中夜间航段RGB不可用，仅依赖LWIR与雷达证据航行。

Result: 在8段留出低光记录上，校准融合模型取得平均精度AP 0.869和期望校准误差ECE 0.108；规则融合AP 0.673，门控融合AP 0.620，RGB单模态AP 0.903。结果表明核心权衡：RGB可用时排序性能最佳，而融合在RGB失效时仍能保留导航输入。

Conclusion: 该工作面向多日ASV任务，推进了在多模态条件下保持可用且可预测退化的感知系统；融合的价值不仅是静态精度提升，更在于低光/RGB失效场景下维持导航感知链路。

Abstract: Reliable obstacle perception is challenging for small autonomous surface vessels (ASVs): reflections and glare confound visible-light cameras, vessel motion shifts the apparent waterline, and darkness removes the dominant sensing modality altogether. Rather than treating multimodal fusion as a static accuracy gain, we propose a context-aware architecture that combines RGB and long-wave infrared (LWIR) imagery, frequency-modulated continuous-wave (FMCW) mmWave radar, and inertial context. A bearing-sector interface provides the navigation controller with calibrated obstacle probabilities, radar ranges, and sensor status. It also distinguishes sensor dropout from a valid observation with no detected obstacle. Using new multimodal lake recordings, we deployed the pipeline in a 24-leg closed-loop pilot under uncontrolled lake conditions (16 day, 4 dusk, and 4 night; 351 m), including night legs in which RGB was unusable and the vessel drove on LWIR and radar evidence alone. On eight held-out low-light recordings, the calibrated fusion model achieved average precision (AP) 0.869 and expected calibration error 0.108, compared with AP 0.673 for rule-based fusion, 0.620 for gated fusion, and 0.903 for RGB alone. Our results expose a central tradeoff: RGB ranks best when usable, whereas fusion preserves a navigation input when RGB fails. This work is a step toward perception that remains usable and degrades predictably across multi-day ASV missions.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [153] [CARVS: A Congestion-Aware Algorithm for Online Multi-Agent Pickup-and-Delivery in Human-Collaborative Warehouses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24549)
*Chaoran Li,Zeyuan Zhao,Shao Zhang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该论文提出多智能体取送货与人类协作（MAPDwHC）问题，并设计CARVS算法以应对人类不确定性带来的动态障碍与实时性挑战，提升智能仓库中人机协作的任务分配与路径规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能仓库中机器人虽提升物流效率，但人机协作引入人类行为不确定性，导致动态交互和不可预测行为；传统MAPD问题未充分建模人类造成的动态障碍及实时响应需求。

Method: 将MAPD扩展为MAPDwHC，形式化新增约束与性质，包括机器人与人交互时临时阻挡路径形成的动态障碍和实时响应要求；提出CARVS算法，通过任务交换、验证检查和随机初始化改进任务分配与路径规划，以降低拥堵并增强对人机交互的适应性。

Result: 仿真结果表明，在大规模场景中，CARVS显著减少路径规划超时，而传统MAPD算法在这些场景中失败，同时提升了效率与可扩展性。

Conclusion: CARVS能有效处理人机协作下的人类不确定性和动态障碍，在实时性与大规模可扩展性方面优于传统MAPD方法，适用于智能仓库人机协同取送货场景。

Abstract: Intelligent warehouse systems have transformed logistics by integrating robots with minimal infrastructure changes, boosting efficiency and optimizing workflows compared to traditional human-only operations. However, human-robot collaboration introduces new challenges, particularly due to human uncertainty, which leads to dynamic interactions and unpredictable behavior. To address these complexities, this paper presents the Multi-Agent Pickup-and-Delivery with Human Collaboration (MAPDwHC) problem, an extension of the Multi-Agent Pickup-and-Delivery (MAPD) problem that incorporates human unpredictability. This problem adds key constraints, such as dynamic obstacles caused by robots temporarily blocking paths when interacting with humans, and the need for real-time responsiveness. We formalize the MAPDwHC problem with additional constraints and properties. Based on this problem setting, we propose the CARVS algorithm, which improves task assignment and path planning by integrating task swapping, validation checks, and random initialization. CARVS reduces congestion, enhances adaptability to human-robot interactions, and ensures real-time responsiveness. Simulation results show that CARVS significantly reduces timeouts of path planning in large-scale scenarios where traditional MAPD algorithms fail, improving both efficiency and scalability.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [Simulation Priors for Rapid Adaptation in Model-Based RL for Autonomous Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26757)
*Adrian Remonda,Jiajun Xi,Nicklas Hansen,Marcus Greiff,Thomas Lew,John Talbot,John Subosits,Xiaolong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PIA 是一个离线到在线的基于模型的强化学习框架，利用结构化仿真先验预训练世界模型，并通过不确定性正则化规划在目标域少量交互中快速校正残差，实现低成本、安全的真实系统适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习能学习高性能控制策略，但依赖大量交互，在数据昂贵且失败不安全的物理系统中难以部署；同时，策略迁移通常需要准确仿真器，而仿真与现实存在偏差。

Method: PIA 先在异构的低保真和高保真仿真轨迹上预训练世界模型，再在目标域中用有限交互进行在线适应，并采用不确定性正则化规划；它不要求精确仿真器用于策略迁移，而是将仿真轨迹视为有偏先验，在在线适应中校正残余失配。

Result: 在自动驾驶赛车的髙保真仿真和真实全尺寸赛车上评估：仿真中，PIA 在赛道和路面变化下比强基线适应更快，圈速更低且失败更少；硬件上，PIA 在少于1小时的车载交互内实现可靠的少样本适应。

Conclusion: 结构化仿真先验结合不确定性正则化的在线适应，可在无需准确仿真器的情况下实现样本高效且安全的真实世界强化学习部署，并在自动驾驶赛车中验证了有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning enables learning high-performance control policies, but its reliance on extensive interaction limits deployment on physical systems where data collection is costly, and failures are unsafe. We introduce Prior-Informed Adaptation (PIA), an offline-to-online model-based RL framework that uses structured simulation priors for rapid adaptation. PIA pretrains a world model on heterogeneous low- and high-fidelity simulated trajectories, then adapts it with limited target-domain interaction using uncertainty-regularized planning. Rather than requiring an accurate simulator for policy transfer, PIA treats simulated trajectories as biased priors whose residual mismatch is corrected during online adaptation. We evaluate PIA in autonomous racing using high-fidelity simulation and on a real full-size autonomous race car. In simulation, PIA adapts faster across track and surface shifts than strong baselines, achieving lower lap times and fewer failures. On hardware, PIA achieves reliable few-shot adaptation with less than one hour of on-vehicle interaction.

</details>


### [155] [VaDar: Implicit Goal Conditioning via Value Disaggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26629)
*Shashwat Saxena,Mehul Goel,Sreyas Venkataraman,Sarvesh Patil,Max Simchowitz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Value Disaggregation（VaDar），训练一个不依赖子目标的策略来最大化各子目标 critic 值的线性组合，从而在训练时利用子目标信息、部署时无需子目标选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程任务对强化学习仍具挑战。常见做法是将任务分解为子目标，并学习子目标条件策略依次执行子任务，但部署时可能需要昂贵或繁琐的子目标选择。

Method: VaDar 不训练子目标条件策略，而是训练单一子目标无关策略，其目标为最大化每个子目标对应 critic 值的线性组合；子目标信息仅在训练阶段用于增强 critic 学习。

Result: 实验表明 VaDar 始终与目标条件化等自然基线具有竞争力，且经常优于它们；它能处理非序列目标结构和多优化目标；在 RoboCasa 挑战性长时程任务上，VaDar 是首个成功的算法，即使从行为克隆预训练且初始任务成功率接近零的策略开始。

Conclusion: 结果表明，子目标信息主要通过提升 actor-critic 强化学习中 critic 学习的有效性来带来收益，而策略层面的目标条件化往往并非必要。

Abstract: Long-horizon tasks are important for modern AI and robotics, yet pose a challenge for reinforcement learning. Historically, a popular approach has been to decompose a task into subgoals, and learn a subgoal-conditioned policy to execute each subtask in sequence. We introduce an alternative called Value Disaggregation (VaDar), which trains a single subgoal-independent policy to maximize a linear combination of per-subgoal critic values. Because subgoal information is needed only at training-time, VaDar removes the need for possibly costly or cumbersome subgoal-selection during policy deployment. Moreover, through careful experiments, we show that VaDar (1) is always competitive with, and frequently outperforms, natural baselines like goal-conditioning and (2) succeeds on tasks with non-sequential goal structure and multiple optimization objectives. In particular, VaDar is the first algorithm to solve challenging, long-horizon tasks in the RoboCasa suite, even when starting from a policy pretrained from behavior cloning with near-zero initial task success. Taken together, VaDar's successes suggest that subgoal information benefits actor-critic reinforcement learning by enhancing the efficacy of critic learning, whereas policy goal-conditioning is often unnecessary.

</details>


### [156] [Progress-Guided Preference Generation for Aligning Generative Control Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25692)
*Tung M. Luu,Tri Ton,Duc M. Nguyen,Xuan Thanh Nguyen,Nguyen Gia Binh,Quang Tan Nguyen,Vu Duong,Daniel Sonntag,Duy M. H. Nguyen,Vien Anh Ngo,Chang D. Yoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PGPG：一种无需人工监督的偏好对齐框架，通过有序覆盖对齐从自主采集中生成伪偏好标签，用于微调预训练生成式策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式策略在模仿学习中表现良好，但受限于专家演示稀缺；现有基于偏好的微调虽可利用次优轨迹，却依赖昂贵的人工标注。

Method: PGPG利用有序覆盖对齐衡量自主采集rollout相对于少量专家演示的序列进度，自动提取伪偏好标签，并引入关键增强以提高进度引导偏好的可靠性；无需人工设计奖励函数，可微调预训练生成式策略。

Result: 在多种具有挑战性的长时程操作任务上，PGPG优于标准监督微调基线；该框架概念简单，可适用于多类生成式策略，并与扩散策略和流匹配策略集成时表现出较强通用性。

Conclusion: PGPG提供了一种无需人类在环监督的偏好对齐途径，可自动利用自主rollout改进生成式策略，并在长时程操作任务和多种生成式策略上验证了有效性与通用性。

Abstract: Generative policies have achieved strong performance in imitation learning tasks, but their success is often constrained by the limited availability of expert demonstrations. Although preference-based fine-tuning can leverage suboptimal rollouts to improve performance, existing approaches heavily rely on expensive human annotations. In this work, we introduce Progress-Guided Preference Generation (PGPG), an unsupervised preference alignment framework that eliminates the need for human-in-the-loop supervision. Our method leverages ordered coverage alignment to measure sequential progress relative to a small set of expert demonstrations, enabling the automatic extraction of pseudo-preference labels from autonomously collected rollouts. Furthermore, we introduce key enhancements to improve the reliability of these progress-guided preferences, enabling effective fine-tuning of pretrained generative policies without requiring manually designed reward functions. Extensive experiments across a suite of challenging long-horizon manipulation tasks demonstrate that PGPG outperforms standard supervised fine-tuning baselines. Moreover, our conceptually simple framework is broadly applicable across multiple classes of generative policies, demonstrating strong versatility when integrated with both diffusion and flow-matching policies.

</details>


### [157] [Latent World Models Should Plan with Distances Along the Latent Manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25013)
*Zunzhi You,Linwei Tao,Siyu Xu,Yunke Wang,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出LeWM等潜世界模型使用环境空间中的L2终端代价会形成虚假低代价盆地并导致规划失败，提出在互最近邻图上计算最短路距离的Neighborhood Graph Cost（NGC）来估计潜流形距离；仅替换终端代价、无需重训练，即可显著提升多任务规划成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜世界模型通常通过最小化预测终端潜变量与目标潜变量在环境范数（如欧氏距离）下的距离来规划，隐含假设潜空间中的邻近性对应环境中的目标邻近性。但作者发现该假设仅在目标附近的小邻域成立，远处L2代价会形成虚假低代价盆地并困住规划器；同时邻域嵌入表明潜变量仍保留物理状态。因此作者假设潜变量位于弯曲的低维流形上，终端代价应度量沿潜流形的距离而非环境空间距离。

Method: 提出Neighborhood Graph Cost（NGC）：在训练潜变量上构建互最近邻图，并在图上计算最短路径距离，以该流形距离作为规划终端代价。NGC只改变终端代价，不改变模型结构，也不需要重新训练。

Result: 在LeWM的四个基准任务上，NGC均提升规划成功率，并在TwoRoom和Reacher上近乎解决问题：即使在最远目标偏移测试中，NGC分别达到95%和100%成功率，而L2代价分别降至27%和81%。

Conclusion: 结果支持潜变量位于低维弯曲流形的假设，并表明潜世界模型应使用沿潜流形的距离进行规划，而非环境空间中的欧氏距离。

Abstract: Latent world models such as LeWM plan by searching for an action sequence whose predicted terminal latent is closest to the goal latent under an ambient norm, such as the Euclidean distance. Planning with such a cost assumes that proximity in the ambient latent space reflects proximity to the goal in the environment. Studying LeWM, we find that this assumption holds only in a small neighborhood around the goal, whereas farther away the L2 cost forms spurious low-cost basins that trap the planner. Yet neighborhood-based embeddings show that the latents retain the underlying physical state. We therefore hypothesize that the latents lie near a curved, low-dimensional manifold, and that the terminal cost should measure the distance along this latent manifold rather than through the ambient space. To estimate this manifold distance, we propose the Neighborhood Graph Cost (NGC), which computes shortest-path distances on a mutual nearest-neighbor graph constructed over training latents. NGC changes only the terminal cost and requires no retraining. This single change improves the planning success rate of LeWM across all four benchmark tasks and nearly solves TwoRoom and Reacher: even at the farthest goal offsets tested, NGC reaches 95% and 100% success, whereas the L2 cost falls to 27% and 81%. These results support the manifold hypothesis and our argument that latent world models should plan with distances along the latent manifold.

</details>


### [158] [Fast Construction of Inner Sphere Trees Using Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23940)
*Jianneng Huang,Wenjia Rao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 ML-IST，将内球树（IST）的叶球生成重构为基于机器学习的单次集合预测问题，以加速复杂非凸物体的碰撞表示构建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂非凸物体的碰撞检测是物理仿真、虚拟现实和机器人中的瓶颈；内球树虽能高效回答接近与穿透查询，但其贪心叶球构建是串行且计算昂贵的，限制了大规模预处理和运行时使用。

Method: ML-IST 将叶球生成建模为单次集合预测：网络从多通道体素几何中预测一组固定的内接球候选；可微覆盖反馈用于修正粗预测并在更细尺度填补残余空隙；轻量几何投影保证球体有效性。

Result: 在测试集上，单 GPU 实现的 ML-IST 在目标球数为 64--512 时，将平均叶球生成时间相较单核 CPU 构建缩短 2.0--2.6 倍；复杂模型上的定性结果显示多尺度球分布能跟随主体体积和细长特征；集成到 Jolt Physics 和 Bullet 后，在 alpha-puzzle 基准上达到 55.89 FPS，高于传统 IST、GImpact 和凸分解等表示。

Conclusion: 结果表明，学习式叶球生成可作为快速、运行时构建详细碰撞表示的实际组件。

Abstract: Collision detection for complex non-convex objects is a bottleneck in physics simulation, virtual reality, and robotics. Inner sphere trees (ISTs) answer proximity and penetration queries efficiently, but their greedy leaf-sphere construction is sequential and computationally expensive, which limits both large-scale preprocessing and runtime use. In this work, we present ML-IST, a machine-learning-based method that reformulates leaf-sphere generation as a single-shot set-prediction problem: a network predicts a fixed set of inscribed sphere candidates from multichannel voxel geometry, differentiable coverage feedback corrects the coarse prediction and fills residual gaps at finer scales, and a lightweight geometric projection enforces validity. Averaged over the test set, a single-GPU implementation of ML-IST reduces mean leaf-generation time by a factor of $2.0$--$2.6$ over single-core CPU construction across target capacities of 64--512 spheres, and qualitative results on complex models show multiscale sphere distributions that follow principal volumes and slender features. We further integrate ML-IST into the Jolt Physics and Bullet engines; in an alpha-puzzle benchmark it reaches 55.89~FPS, the highest among the compared representations (conventional IST, GImpact, and convex decomposition). These results support learned leaf generation as a practical component for fast, runtime construction of detailed collision representations.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [159] [VLA-Rolling: Fewer Denoising Steps, Better Control via Adaptive Action Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25043)
*Sicheng He,Yi Pan,Qiuting Yu,Jiaming Huang,Qi Lu,Shuyan Wu,Hongyan Feng,Xuanyu Liu,Jun Zhang,Jie Zhang,Wenqi Zhang,Xuhong Zhang,Jianwei Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VLA-Rolling 提出一种轻量推理框架，通过复用流匹配 VLA 在滚动时域中本应丢弃的动作尾部，并结合置信度自适应分配去噪预算，在冻结底层 VLA 的情况下实现更高效且更准确的推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配 VLA 每次从高斯噪声重新生成动作块，仅执行短前缀后重规划，导致未执行的动作尾部被丢弃。作者发现该尾部保留了可迁移的去噪进展，可减少去噪步数并匹配或超过原生 10 步高斯基线；但最佳去噪预算因样本而异，因此尾部复用应随更新后的环境自适应调整。

Method: VLA-Rolling 包含三个阶段：动作尾部继承、逐动作置信度估计，以及通过重新加噪与去噪预算分配进行置信度引导的细化；底层 VLA 模型保持冻结。

Result: 在 π0.5、SmolVLA、X-VLA 和 MolmoAct2 上，于 LIBERO、MetaWorld、LIBERO-Plus 和 RoboTwin 2.0 的 16 个模型-基准组合中，VLA-Rolling 全部降低动作块推理成本，最高实现 1.48× 加速；其中 14 个组合成功率提升，最高提升 15.08 个百分点。

Conclusion: 动作尾部并非无用的丢弃预测，而是可复用的中间状态，能够支持以更少去噪步数生成更优动作，从而实现更高效、更准确的 VLA 推理。

Abstract: Flow-matching vision-language-action (VLA) models generate an action chunk by iteratively denoising Gaussian noise, but typically execute only a short prefix before replanning with updated observations. Under this receding-horizon paradigm, the unexecuted remainder---the action tail---is discarded, and the next chunk is regenerated from fresh noise. We show that this discarded tail retains transferable denoising progress: action-tail initialization can match or outperform a native 10-step Gaussian baseline using fewer denoising steps. However, the optimal denoising budget varies substantially across samples, indicating that tail reuse should be adapted to the updated environment. Based on this observation, we introduce VLA-Rolling, a lightweight inference framework that reuses action tails through three stages: action-tail inheritance, per-action confidence estimation, and confidence-guided refinement through renoising and denoising budget allocation, while the underlying VLA remains frozen. Across $\pi_{0.5}$, SmolVLA, X-VLA, and MolmoAct2 on LIBERO, MetaWorld, LIBERO-Plus, and RoboTwin 2.0, VLA-Rolling reduces action-chunk inference cost in all 16 model--benchmark pairs, achieving up to a $1.48\times$ speedup, while improving success rate in 14 pairs by up to $15.08$ percentage points. These results show that action tails are not merely discarded predictions, but reusable intermediate states that enable more efficient and accurate VLA inference. These results demonstrate that the action tail can support the generation of better actions with fewer denoising steps. Our project is available at this https URL .

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [160] [PhantomTac: Hijacking Robotic Tactile Systems with Deploy-and-forget Tags](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26174)
*Binbin Zhang,Yifeng Zhang,Xiaomeng Chen,Dongyao Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示磁触觉传感器中的物理层漏洞 PhantomTac：攻击者仅需在物体表面贴附约0.5 mm厚、无电池的磁性标签，即可隐蔽篡改磁场并欺骗传感器力感知，进而劫持机器人抓取闭环控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉感知对机器人精细操作和闭环力控至关重要，磁触觉传感器因三维力感知能力强、抗视觉遮挡鲁棒性好而广泛应用；现有工作缺乏对其物理层安全风险的系统研究。

Method: 通过逆向分析商用现成磁触觉传感器的传感机制，设计可隐藏在条形码贴纸下的薄型无源磁性标签；利用标签改变磁场以伪造法向力和剪切力读数，并在真实机器人和机械臂上进行端到端攻击评估，同时提出基于多模态一致性的防御机制。

Result: 攻击可绕过现有抗干扰机制并有效劫持闭环控制；在抓取位置偏移不超过8 mm时攻击成功率超过95%。伪造法向力可压碎物体，伪造剪切力可导致抓取松弛并掉落物体。

Conclusion: 磁触觉传感器存在可被低成本、隐蔽、部署后离开的物理层攻击漏洞；仅依赖磁信号抗干扰不足，需引入多模态一致性防御以缓解此类物理层安全威胁。

Abstract: Tactile perception is crucial for precise robotic manipulation, providing an important foundation for dexterous, closed-loop force control. Among various tactile sensing techniques, magnetic tactile sensors stand out thanks to their fine-grained, three-dimensional force perception capability and strong robustness against visual occlusion. In this study, we discover a physical loophole called PhantomTac in magnetic tactile sensors. The attacker can alter the magnetic field, thus spoofing the force interpreted by magnetic tactile sensors. The attacker can stealthily deploy such an attack by simply attaching a thin (the thickness is around 0.5 mm), battery-free magnetic tag to the surface of an object. Hence, the attack tag can be easily concealed by a barcode sticker. Such an attack scheme is "deploy-and-forget" -- the attacker can deploy the tag and leave. When a robotic hand is picking up an object, it may severely damage the object. Specifically, spoofing the normal force can crush the object, whereas spoofing the shear force can lead to a loose grip, thus dropping the object. Compared with directly sabotaging the robotic hand, PhantomTac is much more concealed as the attacker does not need access to the device. We performed extensive reverse-engineering efforts to characterize the sensing mechanism of commercial-off-the-shelf tactile sensors and proposed a practical tag design. End-to-end evaluations conducted on real robots and robotic arms demonstrate that our attack can bypass existing anti-interference mechanisms and effectively hijack their closed-loop control processes, achieving an attack success rate exceeding 95% when the grasping-position offset is bounded within 8 mm. Finally, based on the limitations of existing anti-magnetic interference mechanisms, we propose a multimodal-consistency defense mechanism to mitigate such physical-layer security threats.

</details>
